# 建筑与家居

**house**
英/美 /haʊs/
n. 房屋；住宅
v. 供……居住；容纳
[义] A building where people live, especially one family; to provide a home for someone or something. 供人居住的建筑物，尤指一户人家（a detached house 独栋住宅，row house 联排屋，on the house 店家请客〔餐饮〕）。可数。动词指提供住所或容纳（house refugees 安置难民，house a collection 收藏陈列）。
[例] ① They bought a small house with a garden in the suburbs. 他们在郊区买了一座带花园的小房子。 ② The museum houses one of the largest art collections in the country. 这座博物馆收藏着全国规模最大的艺术藏品之一。
[搭] detached house 独栋住宅, row house 联排屋, house party 家庭派对, on the house 店家请客, house prices 房价
[联] home 家, apartment 公寓, building 建筑物, residence 住所, household 家庭, landlord 房东, rent 租金, mortgage 按揭
[记] 可数：a house。house 偏物理建筑；home 偏情感与归属。the House 可指议会下院（英）或众议院（美）

**home**
英/美 /həʊm/
n. 家；故乡
adv. 回家；在家
adj. 家庭的；本国的
[义] The place where you live and feel you belong; the town or country you come from. 居住并感到归属的地方；故乡（at home 在家/自在，go home 回家，home office 家庭办公室，feel at home 感到自在）。可数/不可数。作副词：head home 回家。形容词：home cooking 家常菜。
[例] ① After years abroad, she finally felt at home in her own kitchen again. 在国外多年后，她终于又在自己的厨房里找到了家的感觉。 ② He works from home three days a week. 他每周三天在家办公。
[搭] at home 在家/自在, go home 回家, home office 家庭办公室, feel at home 感到自在, home country 祖国, stay home 待在家里
[联] house 房屋, family 家庭, household 家庭, hometown 家乡, shelter 庇护所, cozy 舒适的, domestic 家庭的, abroad 在国外
[记] 可数/不可数。home 强调情感与日常归属；house 强调建筑物本身。homework 家庭作业，homesick 想家，同形不同场

**building**
英/美 /ˈbɪldɪŋ/
n. 建筑物；大楼
[义] A structure with walls and a roof, such as an office block, school, or factory. 有墙有顶的结构，如办公楼、学校、工厂（office building 办公楼，apartment building 公寓楼，historic building 历史建筑）。可数。
[例] ① The old building was converted into luxury flats. 那栋老楼被改建成豪华公寓。 ② Firefighters evacuated everyone from the building within minutes. 消防员在几分钟内将楼内人员全部疏散。
[源] 源自动词 build（建造）+ -ing；日耳曼语族，与 board（木板）、bower（凉亭）同根。
[族] build 建造, builder 建筑工人, rebuild 重建, building site 建筑工地
[搭] office building 办公楼, apartment building 公寓楼, historic building 历史建筑, high-rise building 高层建筑, building code 建筑规范
[联] house 住宅, structure 结构, construction 施工, architecture 建筑学, floor 楼层, elevator 电梯, tenant 租户, demolish 拆除
[记] 可数。building 泛指各类建筑；house 专指住宅。building 不可数时指「建造这一行业/活动」较少见，多作可数

**apartment**
英 /əˈpɑːtmənt/ 美 /əˈpɑːrtmənt/
n. 公寓（一套）
[义] A set of rooms for living in, usually on one floor of a larger building. 通常位于较大建筑某一层的成套居住单元（apartment building 公寓楼，studio apartment 开间公寓，rent an apartment 租公寓）。可数。英式日常更常说 flat。
[例] ① She rents a one-bedroom apartment near the university. 她在大学附近租了一居室公寓。 ② The apartment has a small balcony overlooking the park. 这套公寓有个小阳台，可以俯瞰公园。
[源] 源自法语 *appartement*，经意大利语 *appartare*（分开）→ 大建筑中「分隔出来」的一套。
[族] compartment 隔间, apart 分开地
[搭] apartment building 公寓楼, studio apartment 开间公寓, rent an apartment 租公寓, apartment complex 公寓群, furnished apartment 带家具公寓
[联] flat 公寓〔英〕, house 房屋, room 房间, tenant 租户, landlord 房东, lease 租约, condo 产权公寓, loft 阁楼式公寓
[记] 可数。美式 apartment = 英式 flat（日常口语）。apartment 亦指豪华套房（the presidential apartment），语境不同

**flat**
英 /flæt/ 美 /flæt/
n. 公寓（一套）〔尤英式〕
adj. 平的；扁平的；没气的
[义] A set of rooms for living in on one floor of a building (British English). 英式英语中指一套公寓。形容词义：表面平坦；轮胎没气；饮料走气；音调不变（ground-floor flat 底层公寓，flat tyre 瘪胎）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① They live in a two-bedroom flat above a bakery. 他们住在面包店楼上的两居室公寓里。 ② I got a flat tyre on the way to work this morning. 今天早上上班路上轮胎瘪了。
[搭] ground-floor flat 底层公寓, flat tyre 瘪胎, flat rate 统一费率, flat screen 平板屏幕, rent a flat 租公寓
[联] apartment 公寓〔美〕, house 房屋, level 水平的, even 平坦的, sharp 升高的〔音〕, puncture 扎孔, tire 轮胎
[记] 英式 flat = 美式 apartment。flat 作形容词极常见；作名词「公寓」主要在英联邦。flatly 副词：断然地

**room**
英/美 /ruːm/
n. 房间；空间
[义] A part of a building enclosed by walls, used for a particular purpose; space that is or could be used. 建筑内由墙围合、作特定用途的部分；可用空间（living room 客厅，make room for 给……腾地方，room for improvement 改进余地）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The hotel room was small but spotlessly clean. 酒店房间不大，但一尘不染。 ② There isn't enough room in the cupboard for another box. 橱柜里没有空间再放一个箱子了。
[搭] living room 客厅, bedroom 卧室, make room for 给……腾地方, room for improvement 改进余地, single room 单人间
[联] space 空间, apartment 公寓, house 房屋, floor 楼层, wall 墙, furniture 家具, spacious 宽敞的, cramped 拥挤的
[记] 可数/不可数。room 不可数指「余地、空间」。roommate 室友（美），flatmate 室友（英）

**structure**
英/美 /ˈstrʌktʃə(r)/
n. 结构；建筑物
v. 组织；安排
[义] The way parts are arranged or connected; something built, especially a large building. 各部分如何排列或连接；建造物，尤指大型建筑（steel structure 钢结构，supporting structure 支撑结构，sentence structure 句子结构）。可数/不可数。动词指按结构组织（structure an essay 组织文章结构）。
[例] ① Engineers inspected the structure after the earthquake. 地震后工程师检查了建筑结构。 ② The course is structured around three main themes. 这门课围绕三个主题展开。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *structura*，*struere*（建造、堆积）→ 堆叠成整体 → 结构。
[族] construct 建造, construction 施工, structural 结构的, infrastructure 基础设施, destroy 破坏〔de- + struere 反向〕
[搭] steel structure 钢结构, supporting structure 支撑结构, basic structure 基本结构, structure an argument 组织论证, physical structure 物理结构
[联] building 建筑物, framework 框架, foundation 地基, design 设计, architecture 建筑学, organize 组织, collapse 坍塌, engineer 工程师
[记] 可数/不可数。structure 可指抽象组织（social structure 社会结构）。structural 形容词；structurally adv.

**architect**
英/美 /ˈɑːkɪtekt/
n. 建筑师；设计师
[义] A person whose job is to design buildings; someone who plans and creates a complex system or idea. 设计建筑的人；喻指某复杂体系或理念的规划者（chief architect 总设计师，landscape architect 景观建筑师，software architect 软件架构师）。可数。
[例] ① The architect presented drawings for a new library wing. 建筑师提交了图书馆新翼的设计图。 ② She was the chief architect of the company's expansion into Asia. 她是公司进军亚洲的首席策划者。
[源] 源自希腊语 *arkhitektōn*，*arkhi-*（首席）+ *tektōn*（工匠、建造者）→ 总工匠 → 建筑师。
[族] architecture 建筑学, architectural 建筑的
[搭] chief architect 总设计师, landscape architect 景观建筑师, hire an architect 聘请建筑师, architect's office 建筑师事务所, software architect 软件架构师
[联] engineer 工程师, builder 建筑工人, designer 设计师, blueprint 蓝图, construction 施工, building 建筑物, plan 规划, draft 起草
[记] 可数。architect 喻用法在商业、政治报道中常见（architect of reform 改革的设计者）

**architecture**
英/美 /ˈɑːkɪtektʃə(r)/
n. 建筑学；建筑风格；体系结构
[义] The art and practice of designing buildings; the style of a building or group of buildings; the structure of a computer system or organization. 设计建筑的艺术与实践；建筑风格；计算机或组织的体系结构（modern architecture 现代建筑，Gothic architecture 哥特式建筑，system architecture 系统架构）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① She studied architecture at university and now runs her own studio. 她在大学学建筑，现在经营自己的事务所。 ② The cathedral is a fine example of medieval architecture. 这座大教堂是中世纪建筑的精品。
[源] 见 architect；*-tecture* 为 *tektōn* 的抽象名词形式。
[族] architect 建筑师, architectural 建筑的, architecturally adv.
[搭] modern architecture 现代建筑, Gothic architecture 哥特式建筑, landscape architecture 景观建筑, system architecture 系统架构, study architecture 学建筑
[联] building 建筑物, design 设计, construction 施工, style 风格, blueprint 蓝图, engineer 工程师, heritage 遗产, urban 城市的
[记] 不可数/可数。architecture 可指 IT 领域（software architecture）。architectural 形容词修饰风格或特征

**domestic**
英/美 /dəˈmestɪk/
adj. 家庭的；国内的；家养的
n. 家庭佣工；家用航班
[义] Relating to the home or family; relating to one country rather than foreign; kept as a pet or for use on a farm. 与家庭相关的；国内的（非国际）；家养的（domestic chores 家务，domestic flight 国内航班，domestic violence 家庭暴力）。形容词。名词指佣工或国内航线（可数）。
[例] ① They share domestic duties fairly between them. 他们公平分担家务。 ② Domestic tourism recovered faster than international travel after the pandemic. 疫情后国内旅游比国际旅行恢复得更快。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *domesticus*，*domus*（房屋、家）→ 与家有关的。
[族] domicile 住所, dominate 支配〔不同义〕, domain 领域
[搭] domestic chores 家务, domestic flight 国内航班, domestic market 国内市场, domestic appliance 家用电器, domestic violence 家庭暴力
[联] home 家, household 家庭, family 家庭, interior 室内的, foreign 外国的, international 国际的, pet 宠物, routine 日常
[记] 形容词。domestic 名词义「佣工」英式较常见。domesticated 驯化的；domesticity 家庭生活

**kitchen**
英/美 /ˈkɪtʃɪn/
n. 厨房
[义] A room where food is prepared and cooked. 准备和烹饪食物的房间（kitchen table 餐桌/厨房桌，open kitchen 开放式厨房，kitchen sink 厨房水槽）。可数。
[例] ① The smell of garlic filled the kitchen as she started dinner. 她开始做晚饭时，厨房里弥漫起大蒜的香味。 ② They renovated the kitchen with new cabinets and appliances. 他们换了新橱柜和电器，翻新了厨房。
[搭] kitchen table 厨房桌, open kitchen 开放式厨房, kitchen sink 厨房水槽, kitchen knife 厨刀, fitted kitchen 整体厨房
[联] cook 烹饪, stove 炉灶, oven 烤箱, refrigerator 冰箱, cabinet 橱柜, dining room 餐厅, pantry 食品储藏室, appliance 电器
[记] 可数。everything but the kitchen sink 俚语：什么都带上（太夸张）。kitchen 与 chicken 勿混拼写

**bedroom**
英/美 /ˈbedruːm/
n. 卧室
[义] A room used for sleeping. 用于睡眠的房间（master bedroom 主卧，two-bedroom flat 两居室，bedroom community 睡城）。可数。
[例] ① The children share a bedroom until the older one starts school. 孩子们合住一间卧室，直到大的上学。 ② The master bedroom has an ensuite bathroom. 主卧带独立卫生间。
[搭] master bedroom 主卧, two-bedroom apartment 两居室, spare bedroom 客卧, bedroom furniture 卧室家具, ensuite bedroom 带独卫的卧室
[联] bed 床, bathroom 浴室, mattress 床垫, pillow 枕头, blanket 毯子, sleep 睡觉, living room 客厅, wardrobe 衣柜
[记] 可数。复合词 bed + room。bedroom 亦可作形容词（bedroom slippers 拖鞋）。three-bedroom 作定语连字符

**bathroom**
英/美 /ˈbɑːθruːm/
n. 浴室；卫生间
[义] A room containing a bath or shower, a sink, and usually a toilet. 有浴缸或淋浴、洗手池，通常还有马桶的房间（ensuite bathroom 独立卫生间，go to the bathroom 上厕所〔美〕，public bathroom 公共卫生间）。可数。
[例] ① Could I use your bathroom? 我可以用一下卫生间吗？ ② They're retiling the bathroom this weekend. 他们这个周末要给浴室重铺瓷砖。
[搭] ensuite bathroom 独立卫生间, master bathroom 主卫, public bathroom 公共卫生间, bathroom mirror 浴室镜, bathroom scales 体重秤
[联] toilet 马桶, shower 淋浴, bath 浴缸, sink 洗手池, towel 毛巾, soap 肥皂, bedroom 卧室, plumbing 管道
[记] 可数。美式 go to the bathroom 指上厕所（委婉）；英式常说 go to the loo / the toilet。bath 英式可指浴室整体

**living room**
英/美 /ˈlɪvɪŋ ruːm/
n. 客厅；起居室
[义] The main room in a house where people sit, relax, and entertain guests. 家中供坐息、放松和待客的主要房间（sit in the living room 坐在客厅，living room furniture 客厅家具）。可数。英式亦常说 sitting room 或 lounge。
[例] ① We gathered in the living room to watch the match. 我们聚在客厅看比赛。 ② A large sofa dominates the living room. 一张大沙发占据了客厅的主要位置。
[搭] living room furniture 客厅家具, sit in the living room 坐在客厅, open-plan living room 开放式客厅, living room window 客厅窗户
[联] sofa 沙发, television 电视, coffee table 茶几, fireplace 壁炉, dining room 餐厅, bedroom 卧室, lounge 起居室〔英〕, cozy 舒适的
[记] 可数。美式 living room 最常见；英式 sitting room / lounge。open-plan living 指客餐厨一体

**hallway**
英/美 /ˈhɔːlweɪ/
n. 门厅；走廊
[义] An entrance hall or corridor in a house or building. 房屋或建筑中的入口门厅或通道（narrow hallway 窄走廊，down the hallway 沿着走廊）。可数。
[例] ① She hung her coat on a hook in the hallway. 她把外套挂在门厅的钩子上。 ② His office is just down the hallway on the left. 他的办公室就在走廊左侧前方。
[搭] narrow hallway 窄走廊, down the hallway 沿着走廊, hallway mirror 门厅镜, entrance hallway 入口门厅, long hallway 长走廊
[联] corridor 走廊, entrance 入口, door 门, stair 楼梯, foyer 门厅, passage 通道, apartment 公寓, echo 回声
[记] 可数。hallway 偏住宅；corridor 偏办公/学校等较长通道。hall 可指大厅、礼堂，义更广

**basement**
英/美 /ˈbeɪsmənt/
n. 地下室
[义] A room or floor below ground level in a building. 建筑地面以下的房间或楼层（finished basement 装修好的地下室，basement flat 地下室公寓〔英〕，store sth. in the basement 把东西存放在地下室）。可数。
[例] ① The washing machine is in the basement next to the boiler. 洗衣机在地下室，挨着锅炉。 ② They converted the basement into a home gym. 他们把地下室改成了家庭健身房。
[源] 源自 base（底部）+ -ment 名词后缀。
[族] base 底部, basic 基本的, foundation 地基
[搭] finished basement 装修好的地下室, basement apartment 地下室公寓, basement storage 地下室储物, flood the basement 地下室进水
[联] attic 阁楼, floor 楼层, cellar 地窖, underground 地下的, storage 储藏, laundry 洗衣, elevator 电梯, damp 潮湿的
[记] 可数。basement 在美式住宅极常见；英式可能说 lower ground floor。cellar 常指储酒/储物的地窖，较窄

**attic**
英/美 /ˈætɪk/
n. 阁楼；顶楼
[义] A space or room just below the roof of a house, often used for storage. 屋顶下方、常作储物的空间或房间（attic bedroom 阁楼卧室，in the attic 在阁楼里）。可数。
[例] ① Old trunks and photo albums filled the attic. 阁楼上堆满了旧箱子和相册。 ② They insulated the attic to cut heating bills. 他们给阁楼加了隔热层以降低取暖费。
[源] 源自 Attic（希腊 Attica 地区）式建筑风格，后指屋顶下的空间。
[搭] attic bedroom 阁楼卧室, in the attic 在阁楼里, attic conversion 阁楼改造, attic insulation 阁楼隔热, dusty attic 积灰的阁楼
[联] roof 屋顶, basement 地下室, loft 阁楼/开放式顶楼, storage 储藏, ceiling 天花板, beam 梁, spider 蜘蛛, treasure 宝藏
[记] 可数。loft 在美式可指阁楼或工业风大开间；英式 loft 常专指阁楼。attic 偏斜顶小空间

**garage**
英 /ˈɡærɑːʒ/ 美 /ɡəˈrɑːʒ/
n. 车库；汽车修理厂
v. 把（车）开进车库
[义] A building or part of a building for keeping a car; a place where cars are repaired and sold. 停放汽车的建筑或建筑的一部分；修车/售车场所（attached garage 连体车库，garage sale 车库甩卖〔美〕，park in the garage 把车停进车库）。可数。
[例] ① He pulled the car into the garage before the storm hit. 暴风雨来临前他把车开进了车库。 ② The garage on Main Street quoted a fair price for the repair. 主街上的修车厂报了合理的维修价。
[搭] attached garage 连体车库, garage door 车库门, garage sale 车库甩卖, park in the garage 停进车库, underground garage 地下车库
[联] car 汽车, parking 停车, driveway 车道, basement 地下室, vehicle 车辆, mechanic 技工, petrol 汽油, shelter 遮蔽
[记] 可数。英美音差异大：英 /ˈɡærɑːʒ/，美 /ɡəˈrɑːʒ/。garage sale 美式社区旧物甩卖

**balcony**
英/美 /ˈbælkəni/
n. 阳台；楼座
[义] A platform with a wall or rail on the outside of an upper floor of a building; the upper rows of seats in a theatre. 建筑物上层外挑、带护栏的平台；剧院楼座（balcony view 阳台景观，flower boxes on the balcony 阳台花箱）。可数。
[例] ① They had breakfast on the balcony overlooking the sea. 他们在俯瞰大海的阳台上吃早餐。 ② We had cheap tickets in the balcony, but the view of the stage was fine. 我们买了楼座廉价票，但看舞台的角度很好。
[源] 源自意大利语 *balcone*，可能经德语与 *balk-*（梁）相关 → 由梁挑出之平台。
[搭] balcony view 阳台景观, sit on the balcony 坐在阳台上, flower boxes on the balcony 阳台花箱, balcony door 阳台门, juliet balcony 装饰性小阳台
[联] terrace 露台, patio 庭院, window 窗户, apartment 公寓, railing 栏杆, view 视野, outdoor 户外的, theatre 剧院
[记] 可数。balcony 剧院义在英美都常见。与 terrace / patio 相比，balcony 多指悬挑在楼外

**wall**
英/美 /wɔːl/
n. 墙；壁
v. 用墙围住
[义] A vertical structure of brick, stone, or other material that divides or encloses a space. 用砖、石等材料筑成、分隔或围合空间的垂直结构（brick wall 砖墙，wall clock 挂钟，drive sb. up the wall 把某人逼疯〔习语〕）。可数/不可数。动词指筑墙隔离（walled garden 有围墙的花园）。
[例] ① They painted the living-room walls a soft grey. 他们把客厅的墙刷成了柔和的灰色。 ② A high wall separates the garden from the road. 高墙把花园和马路隔开。
[搭] brick wall 砖墙, wall clock 挂钟, drive sb. up the wall 把某人逼疯, wall paint 墙漆, load-bearing wall 承重墙
[联] floor 地板, ceiling 天花板, roof 屋顶, door 门, window 窗户, partition 隔断, plaster 灰泥, paint 油漆
[记] 可数/不可数。wall 不可数指「墙这一材质/概念」。the Wall 可指柏林墙等专名。wallet 钱包，同形异义

**floor**
英/美 /flɔː(r)/
n. 地板；楼层
v. 使惊呆；击倒
[义] The surface you walk on inside a room; a level of a building; the bottom of the sea or a forest. 室内行走的表面；建筑的楼层；海底或林下层（ground floor 底层〔英〕/一楼〔美〕，first floor 一楼〔英〕/二楼〔美〕，sweep the floor 扫地）。可数/不可数。动词 floor 口语指「使震惊」（The news floored me）。
[例] ① Please take your shoes off—the floor was just cleaned. 请脱鞋——地板刚拖过。 ② Her office is on the third floor of the building. 她的办公室在这栋楼四楼。
[搭] ground floor 底层, first floor 一楼, sweep the floor 扫地, floor plan 平面图, wooden floor 木地板
[联] ceiling 天花板, wall 墙, carpet 地毯, storey 楼层, ground 地面, tile 瓷砖, upstairs 楼上, downstairs 楼下
[记] 可数/不可数。英美楼层叫法不同：英 ground floor = 美 first floor。floor 动词：be floored 大吃一惊

**ceiling**
英/美 /ˈsiːlɪŋ/
n. 天花板；上限
[义] The top inner surface of a room; an upper limit on something. 房间内侧顶部表面；某事物的上限（high ceiling 层高高的，glass ceiling 玻璃天花板〔喻〕，price ceiling 价格上限）。可数。
[例] ① A fan hung from the ceiling above the bed. 床顶上方天花板上吊着一盏风扇。 ② The government imposed a ceiling on rent increases. 政府对租金涨幅设定了上限。
[源] 与 ceil（装天花板）相关，可能源自拉丁语 *caelum*（天空）。
[族] ceil 装天花板〔罕用动词〕
[搭] high ceiling 层高高的, glass ceiling 玻璃天花板, price ceiling 价格上限, ceiling fan 吊扇, hit the ceiling 暴怒〔口语〕
[联] floor 地板, wall 墙, roof 屋顶, height 高度, light 灯, fan 风扇, limit 上限, room 房间
[记] 可数。hit the ceiling = 勃然大怒。ceiling 喻义在新闻、经济语境常见

**roof**
英/美 /ruːf/
n. 屋顶
v. 给……盖顶
[义] The structure that covers the top of a building; the top inner surface of a vehicle or cave. 覆盖建筑顶部的结构；车辆或洞穴的内顶（flat roof 平屋顶，under one roof 在同一屋檐下，raise the roof 喧闹欢呼〔习语〕）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Rain drummed on the roof all night. 雨整夜敲打着屋顶。 ② Three generations live under one roof. 三代人同住在一个屋檐下。
[搭] flat roof 平屋顶, under one roof 在同一屋檐下, roof terrace 屋顶平台, roof tile 瓦片, leak in the roof 屋顶漏水
[联] ceiling 天花板, wall 墙, attic 阁楼, tile 瓦, chimney 烟囱, shelter 遮蔽, rain 雨, repair 修理
[记] 可数/不可数。复数 roofs。through the roof 俚语：价格等飙升。roof over one's head 住处（基本需求）

**door**
英/美 /dɔː(r)/
n. 门；通道
[义] A hinged or sliding barrier for opening and closing an entrance; a means of access or opportunity. 可开关的入口屏障；途径或机会（front door 前门，open the door to 为……打开大门，next door 隔壁）。可数。
[例] ① She locked the door and checked it twice before leaving. 她锁上门，离开前又检查了两次。 ② The internship opened the door to a full-time job. 这次实习为全职工作打开了大门。
[搭] front door 前门, back door 后门, open the door 开门, next door 隔壁, door handle 门把手
[联] window 窗户, wall 墙, key 钥匙, lock 锁, entrance 入口, gate 大门, knock 敲, welcome 欢迎
[记] 可数。door 与 floor 押韵但拼写不同。indoor / outdoor 室内外。doorstep 门阶

**window**
英/美 /ˈwɪndəʊ/
n. 窗户；窗口期
[义] An opening in a wall fitted with glass to let in light and air; a period when something is possible. 墙上装玻璃以采光通风的开口；某事可发生的一段时机（open the window 开窗，bay window 凸窗，window of opportunity 机会窗口）。可数。
[例] ① He leaned out of the window to call to his friend below. 他探出窗口喊楼下的朋友。 ② There is a narrow window to apply before the deadline. 截止前有一小段可申请的时间窗口。
[搭] open the window 开窗, bay window 凸窗, window seat 窗座, window frame 窗框, window of opportunity 机会窗口
[联] door 门, wall 墙, glass 玻璃, curtain 窗帘, view 视野, light 光线, sill 窗台, blind 百叶窗
[记] 可数。window shopping 只逛不买。Windows 操作系统专名大写

**stair**
英/美 /steə(r)/
n. 楼梯；（楼梯的）一级
[义] One of a set of steps from one floor to another; stairs 常复数指整段楼梯（go up the stairs 上楼，bottom of the stairs 楼梯底，stair carpet 楼梯地毯）。可数，常用复数 stairs。
[例] ① The children ran up the stairs to their bedrooms. 孩子们跑上楼回卧室。 ② She sat on the bottom stair, tying her shoes. 她坐在最下面一级台阶上系鞋带。
[源] 源自古英语 *stǣger*（阶梯），日耳曼语族。
[族] staircase 楼梯间, stairway 楼梯通道, upstairs 楼上, downstairs 楼下
[搭] go up the stairs 上楼, bottom of the stairs 楼梯底, stair rail 楼梯扶手, flight of stairs 一段楼梯, spiral stair 螺旋楼梯
[联] step 台阶, elevator 电梯, floor 楼层, landing 平台, hallway 走廊, climb 爬, rail 扶手, ladder 梯子
[记] 常用复数 stairs。stair 单数指一级台阶。a flight of stairs 一段楼梯。staircase 指楼梯结构整体

**elevator**
英 /ˈelɪveɪtə(r)/ 美 /ˈelɪveɪtər/
n. 电梯〔尤美式〕
[义] A platform or compartment housed in a shaft for raising and lowering people or goods between floors. 在竖井中升降、运送人或物的平台或厢体（take the elevator 乘电梯，elevator music 电梯音乐〔喻〕，service elevator 货梯）。可数。
[例] ① We took the elevator to the tenth floor. 我们乘电梯上了十楼。 ② The elevator was out of order, so we used the stairs. 电梯坏了，我们走楼梯。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *elevare*（举起），*e-*（出）+ *levare*（减轻、抬起）。
[族] elevate 提升, elevation 海拔/立面, levitate 悬浮
[搭] take the elevator 乘电梯, elevator shaft 电梯井, service elevator 货梯, elevator button 电梯按钮, stuck in an elevator 困在电梯里
[联] lift 电梯〔英〕, stair 楼梯, floor 楼层, button 按钮, lobby 门厅, escalator 自动扶梯, shaft 井道, ride 乘坐
[记] 可数。美式 elevator = 英式 lift（日常）。elevator pitch 指简短推介语（借「电梯间几十秒」之喻）

**lift**
英 /lɪft/ 美 /lɪft/
n. 电梯〔英式〕；搭便车
v. 举起；抬起
[义] A machine that carries people up and down in a building (British); a free ride in someone's vehicle. 英式英语中的电梯；搭便车。动词：举起、抬起（take the lift 乘电梯，give sb. a lift 让某人搭车，lift weights 举重）。可数。
[例] ① The lift was crowded during the morning rush. 早高峰时电梯里挤满了人。 ② Could you give me a lift to the station? 你能让我搭个便车到车站吗？
[搭] take the lift 乘电梯, give sb. a lift 让某人搭车, lift weights 举重, lift off 发射升空, face lift 整容/翻新
[联] elevator 电梯〔美〕, raise 举起, carry 搬运, ride 乘坐, stair 楼梯, heavy 重的, boost 提升, thumb 搭车〔thumb a lift〕
[记] 英式 lift = 美式 elevator。lift 动词极常见；lift 名词「搭便车」英式口语。shoplifting 商店行窃

**furniture**
英/美 /ˈfɜːnɪtʃə(r)/
n. 家具
[义] Large movable objects such as tables, chairs, and beds used to make a room ready for use. 使房间适于使用的大型可移动物品，如桌、椅、床（a piece of furniture 一件家具，office furniture 办公家具，assemble furniture 组装家具）。不可数。
[例] ① They bought new furniture when they moved into the flat. 搬进公寓后他们买了新家具。 ② The room felt empty until we arranged the furniture. 摆好家具之前，房间显得很空。
[源] 源自法语 *fourniture*，*fournir*（供应、装备）→ 装备房间之物。
[族] furnish 布置, furnished 带家具的, furnishing 陈设〔常复〕
[搭] a piece of furniture 一件家具, office furniture 办公家具, assemble furniture 组装家具, antique furniture 古董家具, flat-pack furniture 平板包装家具
[联] chair 椅子, table 桌子, bed 床, sofa 沙发, desk 书桌, cabinet 柜子, decoration 装饰, IKEA 宜家
[记] 不可数；一件说 a piece of furniture。furnish 动词：布置房间。furnished flat 带家具出租的公寓

**bed**
英/美 /bed/
n. 床；苗床；河床
v. 安置；上床睡觉
[义] A piece of furniture for sleeping on; a garden plot or the bottom of a river. 供睡眠的家具；花坛或河床（go to bed 上床睡觉，make the bed 铺床，double bed 双人床，flower bed 花坛）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① I'm exhausted—I'm going to bed early tonight. 我累坏了——今晚要早点睡。 ② They planted tulips in the flower bed by the window. 他们在窗边的花坛里种了郁金香。
[搭] go to bed 上床睡觉, make the bed 铺床, double bed 双人床, bunk bed 双层床, go to bed with 与……上床〔俚〕
[联] mattress 床垫, pillow 枕头, blanket 毯子, sheet 床单, bedroom 卧室, sleep 睡觉, sofa 沙发, duvet 羽绒被
[记] 可数/不可数。bed 与 bad 元音不同。bedtime 就寝时间；bed and breakfast（B&B）民宿

**table**
英/美 /ˈteɪbl/
n. 桌子；表格
v. 提交讨论；搁置〔美〕
[义] A piece of furniture with a flat top and legs, used for eating, working, or placing things on; a set of facts arranged in rows and columns. 有平面和桌腿的家具，用于用餐、工作或放置物品；表格（dining table 餐桌，coffee table 茶几，set the table 摆餐具，table manners 餐桌礼仪）。可数。
[例] ① We gathered around the kitchen table for Sunday lunch. 我们围坐在厨房餐桌旁吃周日午餐。 ② Please put your phones on the table during the meeting. 开会期间请把手机放在桌上。
[搭] dining table 餐桌, coffee table 茶几, set the table 摆餐具, table manners 餐桌礼仪, round table 圆桌
[联] chair 椅子, desk 书桌, furniture 家具, meal 餐, kitchen 厨房, shelf 架子, surface 表面, wood 木头
[记] 可数。table 动词：英式 table a motion 提交动议；美式 table a bill 搁置法案，义相反，国际会议须看语境

**chair**
英/美 /tʃeə(r)/
n. 椅子；主席职位
v. 主持（会议）
[义] A separate seat for one person, with a back and four legs; the position of being in charge of a meeting or department. 供一人坐的独立座位，有靠背和四腿；会议或部门的主席职位（office chair 办公椅，armchair 扶手椅，take the chair 主持会议）。可数。
[例] ① Pull up a chair and tell me what happened. 拉把椅子过来，跟我说说发生了什么。 ② She was elected chair of the housing committee. 她当选住房委员会主席。
[搭] office chair 办公椅, armchair 扶手椅, rocking chair 摇椅, take the chair 主持会议, folding chair 折叠椅
[联] table 桌子, desk 书桌, seat 座位, sofa 沙发, furniture 家具, sit 坐, stool 凳子, bench 长凳
[记] 可数。chair 动词：chair a meeting 主持会议。chairman/chairperson 主席；cathedra 与 chair 同族

**sofa**
英/美 /ˈsəʊfə/
n. 长沙发
[义] A long upholstered seat with a back and arms, for more than one person. 带靠背和扶手、供多人就座的长型软垫座椅（leather sofa 皮沙发，sofa bed 沙发床，sit on the sofa 坐在沙发上）。可数。
[例] ① He fell asleep on the sofa watching television. 他看电视时在沙发上睡着了。 ② The sofa bed is handy when guests stay overnight. 有客人留宿时，沙发床很方便。
[源] 源自阿拉伯语 *ṣuffa*（长凳、平台），经土耳其语入欧洲。
[搭] leather sofa 皮沙发, sofa bed 沙发床, corner sofa 转角沙发, sit on the sofa 坐在沙发上, sofa cushion 沙发垫
[联] couch 长沙发, armchair 扶手椅, living room 客厅, cushion 靠垫, furniture 家具, sit 坐, nap 小睡, upholstery 软垫面料
[记] 可数。sofa 与 couch 美式常互换；couch 略口语，且有「心理诊疗榻」义。settee 英式旧式说法

**desk**
英/美 /desk/
n. 书桌；办公桌；服务台
[义] A table, often with drawers, used for writing or working; a counter in a hotel, airport, or office. 常带抽屉、供书写或工作的桌子；酒店、机场或办公室的服务台（writing desk 写字台，reception desk 前台，sit at one's desk 坐在工位）。可数。
[例] ① Her desk was buried under stacks of reports. 她的办公桌上堆满了报告。 ② Please check in at the reception desk on arrival. 到达请到前台办理入住。
[搭] writing desk 写字台, reception desk 前台, desk job 案头工作, sit at one's desk 坐在工位, desk lamp 台灯
[联] table 桌子, chair 椅子, office 办公室, drawer 抽屉, computer 电脑, study 书房, counter 柜台, work 工作
[记] 可数。desk 偏工作/学习；table 偏用餐或通用平面。desktop 桌面；help desk 技术支持台

**shelf**
英/美 /ʃelf/
n. 架子；搁板
（复数 shelves /ʃelvz/）
[义] A flat board fixed horizontally to a wall or in a cupboard for storing things. 水平固定在墙上或柜内的平板，用于存放物品（bookshelf 书架，on the shelf 被搁置/滞销，top shelf 最高层）。可数。
[例] ① She put the cereal back on the top shelf. 她把麦片放回最上层架子。 ② The project has been on the shelf for months. 这个项目已被搁置数月。
[搭] bookshelf 书架, on the shelf 被搁置, top shelf 最高层, shelf life 保质期, floating shelf 悬空搁板
[联] cupboard 橱柜, cabinet 柜, drawer 抽屉, wall 墙, storage 储藏, book 书, rack 架, display 展示
[记] 复数 shelves。on the shelf 喻指人「嫁不出去/被闲置」已过时，但指项目搁置仍常用。shelve 动词：搁置

**cupboard**
英/美 /ˈkʌbəd/
n. 橱柜；壁橱
[义] A piece of furniture or a space in a wall with a door, used for storage, especially of food, dishes, or clothes. 带门的家具或墙内空间，用于储物，尤指食品、餐具或衣物（kitchen cupboard 厨房橱柜，airing cupboard 烘干柜〔英〕，cupboard door 柜门）。可数。
[例] ① The cups are in the cupboard above the sink. 杯子在水槽上方的橱柜里。 ② She cleared out the cupboard and donated old coats. 她清空了壁橱，把旧外套捐了出去。
[搭] kitchen cupboard 厨房橱柜, cupboard door 柜门, built-in cupboard 嵌入式壁橱, airing cupboard 烘干柜, lock the cupboard 锁柜
[联] cabinet 柜, closet 衣橱, shelf 搁板, drawer 抽屉, pantry 食品储藏室, wardrobe 衣柜, storage 储藏, kitchen 厨房
[记] 可数。cupboard 原义 cup + board（放杯子的板），现泛指橱柜。美式 pantry / cabinet 更常指厨房储物

**cabinet**
英/美 /ˈkæbɪnət/
n. 橱柜；内阁
[义] A cupboard with shelves or drawers; a group of senior government ministers. 带搁板或抽屉的柜子；政府高级部长集团（kitchen cabinet 厨房橱柜/心腹智囊，medicine cabinet 药柜，cabinet meeting 内阁会议）。可数。
[例] ① The plates are stored in the cabinet next to the fridge. 盘子放在冰箱旁的橱柜里。 ② The prime minister reshuffled the cabinet after the election. 选举后首相改组了内阁。
[源] 源自意大利语 *cabinetto*，*cabin*（小房间）的指小形式 → 小房间式储物柜 → 内阁（私密议事小房间）。
[族] cabin 小木屋/舱室
[搭] kitchen cabinet 厨房橱柜, medicine cabinet 药柜, filing cabinet 文件柜, cabinet meeting 内阁会议, display cabinet 展示柜
[联] cupboard 橱柜, drawer 抽屉, shelf 搁板, kitchen 厨房, minister 部长, storage 储藏, furniture 家具, handle 把手
[记] 可数。cabinet 政治义在新闻中极常见。kitchen cabinet 亦指「私下顾问团」

**wardrobe**
英/美 /ˈwɔːdrəʊb/
n. 衣柜；全部衣物
[义] A tall cupboard for hanging clothes; the clothes a person owns. 挂放衣物的高柜；某人拥有的全部服装（built-in wardrobe 嵌入式衣柜，wardrobe malfunction 着装意外〔媒体〕）。可数。
[例] ① She opened the wardrobe and chose a coat for the evening. 她打开衣柜，挑了一件外套去参加晚会。 ② His entire wardrobe fits into one suitcase when he travels. 他旅行时全部衣物装在一个行李箱里。
[源] 复合词 ward（保管）+ robe（长袍）→ 存放长袍之处。
[搭] built-in wardrobe 嵌入式衣柜, fitted wardrobe 定制衣柜, wardrobe door 衣柜门, walk-in wardrobe 步入式衣帽间, capsule wardrobe 精简衣橱
[联] closet 衣橱, cupboard 橱柜, clothes 衣服, hanger 衣架, shelf 搁板, bedroom 卧室, dress 穿衣, outfit 一套穿搭
[记] 可数。wardrobe 英式常指衣柜；美式更常说 closet（built-in closet）。wardrobe 亦指全部衣物（不可数义）

**drawer**
英/美 /drɔː(r)/
n. 抽屉
[义] A box-shaped storage compartment in a desk, chest, or other piece of furniture, that slides open and shut. 书桌、柜子等家具中可推拉开合的箱形储物格（chest of drawers 五斗柜，top drawer 最上层抽屉，out of the top drawer 出身名门〔习语〕）。可数。
[例] ① The keys are in the top drawer of the desk. 钥匙在书桌最上面的抽屉里。 ② She rummaged through the drawer looking for a pen. 她在抽屉里翻找钢笔。
[搭] chest of drawers 五斗柜, top drawer 最上层抽屉, drawer handle 抽屉把手, junk drawer 杂物抽屉, open a drawer 拉开抽屉
[联] desk 书桌, cabinet 橱柜, shelf 搁板, cupboard 橱柜, storage 储藏, handle 把手, organize 整理, clutter 杂乱
[记] 可数。drawer /ˈdrɔːə/ 与 draw /drɔː/（绘画、拉）同族。drawers 复数亦可指内裤（尤老式说法）

**mirror**
英/美 /ˈmɪrə(r)/
n. 镜子
v. 反映；映照
[义] A piece of glass with a reflective surface, used to see yourself; to show or reflect something. 表面能反光的玻璃片，用于照见自己；反映、映照（look in the mirror 照镜子，mirror image 镜像，smoke and mirrors 障眼法〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① She checked her hair in the bathroom mirror. 她在浴室镜子里看了看头发。 ② The calm lake mirrored the mountains perfectly. 平静的湖面完美地映出山影。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *mirare*（惊奇地看）→ 令人惊奇地映出影像之物。
[族] miracle 奇迹, admire 钦佩, mirage 海市蜃楼
[搭] look in the mirror 照镜子, mirror image 镜像, bathroom mirror 浴室镜, full-length mirror 全身镜, rear-view mirror 后视镜
[联] glass 玻璃, reflection 倒影, window 窗户, vanity 梳妆台, appearance 外表, selfie 自拍, frame 镜框, polish 擦亮
[记] 可数。mirror 动词：mirror sb.'s views 与某人看法一致。smoke and mirrors 指骗人的花招

**mattress**
英/美 /ˈmætrəs/
n. 床垫
[义] A fabric case filled with soft material, used for sleeping on. 内填软料的布套，供睡眠使用（memory foam mattress 记忆棉床垫，air mattress 充气床垫，mattress cover 床垫罩）。可数。
[例] ① The old mattress was too soft and hurt his back. 旧床垫太软，弄得他背疼。 ② They bought a new mattress and slept much better. 他们买了新床垫，睡眠好多了。
[源] 源自阿拉伯语 *maṭraḥ*（垫子、投掷处），经法语入英。
[搭] memory foam mattress 记忆棉床垫, air mattress 充气床垫, mattress cover 床垫罩, flip the mattress 翻转床垫, firm mattress 硬床垫
[联] bed 床, pillow 枕头, sheet 床单, blanket 毯子, duvet 羽绒被, sleep 睡觉, spring 弹簧, foam 泡沫
[记] 可数。mattress 与 bed 不同：bed 含床架，mattress 仅指垫层。mat 小垫，勿混

**pillow**
英/美 /ˈpɪləʊ/
n. 枕头
[义] A soft cushion used to support the head in bed. 床上支撑头部的软垫（throw pillow 抱枕，pillow case 枕套，pillow fight 枕头大战）。可数。
[例] ① She fluffed the pillows before the guests arrived. 客人到来前她把枕头拍松。 ② He propped himself up on one elbow against the pillows. 他单肘撑在枕头上支起身子。
[搭] throw pillow 抱枕, pillow case 枕套, pillow fight 枕头大战, soft pillow 软枕头, rest on a pillow 枕在枕头上
[联] mattress 床垫, blanket 毯子, sheet 床单, bed 床, cushion 靠垫, sleep 睡觉, neck 脖子, rest 休息
[记] 可数。pillow talk 枕边细语。pillow 亦可指靠垫（sofa pillows）

**blanket**
英/美 /ˈblæŋkɪt/
n. 毯子；覆盖层
v. 覆盖
adj. 总括的
[义] A large piece of thick cloth used for warmth on a bed; a layer that covers something. 床上保暖的大块厚布；覆盖层（electric blanket 电热毯，blanket ban 全面禁止，blanket coverage 全面报道）。可数。动词指覆盖；形容词指总括的。
[例] ① She pulled the blanket up to her chin and fell asleep. 她把毯子拉到下巴处睡着了。 ② Snow blanketed the rooftops overnight. 一夜之间大雪覆盖了屋顶。
[搭] electric blanket 电热毯, wool blanket 羊毛毯, blanket ban 全面禁止, wet blanket 扫兴的人, under the blanket 在毯子下
[联] sheet 床单, duvet 羽绒被, quilt 被子, pillow 枕头, warm 温暖的, cover 覆盖, wool 羊毛, layer 层
[记] 可数。a wet blanket 指扫兴的人。blanket 形容词：blanket rule 一刀切规定

**sheet**
英/美 /ʃiːt/
n. 床单；一张（纸）；薄板
[义] A large piece of thin cloth used on a bed; a single piece of paper; a thin flat piece of metal or other material. 床上用的大块薄布；一张纸；金属或其他材料的薄板（bed sheet 床单，a sheet of paper 一张纸，sheet metal 金属薄板）。可数。
[例] ① Change the sheets before the guests arrive. 客人来之前换床单。 ② He handed me a sheet of figures to check. 他递给我一张数据表让我核对。
[搭] bed sheet 床单, a sheet of paper 一张纸, sheet metal 金属薄板, fitted sheet 床笠, clean sheets 干净床单
[联] blanket 毯子, duvet 羽绒被, pillow 枕头, mattress 床垫, laundry 洗衣, cotton 棉, fold 折叠, paper 纸
[记] 可数。a clean sheet 体育语境指零失球/清白记录。sheet 与 shit 拼写勿混

**duvet**
英/美 /ˈduːveɪ/
n. 羽绒被；被芯
[义] A soft quilt filled with down or synthetic fibres, used instead of blankets on a bed (chiefly British). 内填羽绒或化纤的软被，床上替代毯子使用（主要英式；美式常说 comforter）。可数。
[例] ① The duvet is too warm for summer nights. 这条羽绒被夏天夜里盖太热了。 ② She bought a duvet cover that matches the curtains. 她买了与窗帘配套的被套。
[源] 源自法语 *duvet*（羽绒），与 down（绒毛）同源概念。
[搭] duvet cover 被套, duck-down duvet 鸭绒被, lightweight duvet 薄被, throw off the duvet 掀开被子, wash the duvet 洗被子
[联] blanket 毯子, sheet 床单, quilt 被子, pillow 枕头, comforter 棉被〔美〕, bedding 床上用品, warm 温暖的, sleep 睡觉
[记] 可数。英式 duvet = 美式 comforter（大致）。duvet 亦可指被芯本身，duvet cover 为被套

**cushion**
英/美 /ˈkʊʃən/
n. 靠垫；缓冲物
v. 缓冲；减轻
[义] A soft bag filled with material, used to make a seat more comfortable or as decoration; something that protects against shock or hardship. 内填材料的软垫，使座位更舒适或作装饰；缓冲物（sofa cushion 沙发靠垫，cushion the blow 缓和打击〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① She arranged the cushions on the sofa before the guests arrived. 客人来前她把沙发靠垫摆好。 ② Savings can cushion you against sudden job loss. 储蓄可以帮你缓冲突然失业的冲击。
[搭] sofa cushion 沙发靠垫, floor cushion 坐垫, cushion cover 靠垫套, cushion the blow 缓和打击, soft cushion 软垫
[联] pillow 枕头, sofa 沙发, chair 椅子, pad 垫子, comfort 舒适, decorate 装饰, foam 泡沫, support 支撑
[记] 可数。cushion 动词：cushion the impact 缓冲冲击。cushion 与 pillow 相比更小、多用于装饰或倚靠

**armchair**
英/美 /ˈɑːmtʃeə(r)/
n. 扶手椅
adj. 空谈的；不切实际的
[义] A comfortable chair with side supports for your arms. 两侧有扶手支撑的舒适座椅（leather armchair 皮扶手椅，armchair critic 坐而论道者〔喻〕）。可数。形容词喻指脱离实际、空谈。
[例] ① He sank into the armchair with a cup of tea. 他端着茶杯陷进扶手椅里。 ② Armchair experts flooded social media with opinions after the match. 赛后社交媒体涌上一群空谈「专家」。
[搭] leather armchair 皮扶手椅, comfortable armchair 舒适的扶手椅, armchair travel 神游, wing armchair 翼背扶手椅, sit in an armchair 坐在扶手椅上
[联] chair 椅子, sofa 沙发, living room 客厅, cushion 靠垫, relax 放松, read 阅读, fireplace 壁炉, critic 批评者
[记] 可数。复合词 arm + chair。armchair 形容词：armchair general 纸上谈兵的将军

**rug**
英/美 /rʌɡ/
n. 小地毯；毯子
[义] A piece of thick material used to cover part of a floor, smaller than a carpet. 铺在地上一部分的厚织物，比地毯小（area rug 块毯，pull the rug 拆台〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① A colourful rug lay in front of the fireplace. 壁炉前铺着一块彩色小地毯。 ② The dog was sleeping on the rug by the door. 狗睡在门边的小地毯上。
[搭] area rug 块毯, wool rug 羊毛毯, pull the rug from under sb. 拆某人的台, braided rug 编织小毯, shake the rug 抖地毯
[联] carpet 地毯, floor 地板, mat 垫子, wool 羊毛, living room 客厅, vacuum 吸尘, weave 编织, cosy 舒适的
[记] 可数。rug 比 carpet 小，常可移动。pull the rug from under sb. 突然撤销支持

**carpet**
英/美 /ˈkɑːpɪt/
n. 地毯；覆盖层
v. 铺地毯；严厉训斥〔英〕
[义] A thick woven floor covering that may cover an entire room; a layer covering a surface. 可铺满整间房的厚编织地面覆盖物；覆盖层（wall-to-wall carpet 满铺地毯，red carpet 红毯，carpet of snow 一层白雪）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① They replaced the old carpet with wooden flooring. 他们用木地板换掉了旧地毯。 ② The meadow was a carpet of wild flowers in spring. 春天草地上开满野花，如地毯一般。
[搭] wall-to-wall carpet 满铺地毯, red carpet 红毯, carpet stain 地毯污渍, fitted carpet 满铺地毯, roll of carpet 一卷地毯
[联] rug 小地毯, floor 地板, mat 垫子, vacuum 吸尘, weave 编织, wool 羊毛, stain 污渍, layer 层
[记] 可数/不可数。carpet 动词英式口语：be carpeted 被上司训斥。red carpet treatment 隆重礼遇

**stove**
英/美 /stəʊv/
n. 炉灶；火炉
[义] A piece of equipment for cooking food, with rings on top and often an oven below; a enclosed heater burning fuel. 烹饪设备，顶部有炉盘、下方常有烤箱；封闭式燃烧取暖炉（gas stove 燃气灶，electric stove 电炉，camping stove 露营炉）。可数。
[例] ① She put a pan of water on the stove to boil. 她把一锅水放在炉灶上烧开。 ② The old cottage still has a wood-burning stove for heat. 那座老小屋仍用烧柴火炉取暖。
[搭] gas stove 燃气灶, electric stove 电炉, on the stove 在炉子上, stove top 灶台, wood-burning stove 柴火炉
[联] oven 烤箱, kitchen 厨房, cook 烹饪, pot 锅, flame 火焰, gas 煤气, boil 煮沸, heat 加热
[记] 可数。美式 stove 常指灶台+烤箱整体；英式 cooker 更常说整台厨灶。stove 亦可指取暖炉

**oven**
英/美 /ˈʌvən/
n. 烤箱；烤炉
[义] A closed compartment in a kitchen, used for baking or roasting food by dry heat. 厨房中用于干热烘烤或炙烤食物的封闭腔体（bake in the oven 用烤箱烤，preheat the oven 预热烤箱，microwave oven 微波炉）。可数。
[例] ① Preheat the oven to 180 degrees before baking the cake. 烤蛋糕前把烤箱预热到 180 度。 ② The pizza came out of the oven golden and crisp. 披萨从烤箱里出来时金黄酥脆。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *fornus*（烤炉），经古英语 *ofen* 演变。
[搭] preheat the oven 预热烤箱, bake in the oven 用烤箱烤, oven door 烤箱门, oven temperature 烤箱温度, gas oven 燃气烤箱
[联] stove 炉灶, bake 烘烤, roast 炙烤, kitchen 厨房, heat 加热, timer 定时器, dish 菜肴, microwave 微波炉
[记] 可数。oven 专指封闭烘烤空间；stove 偏灶台。hot from the oven 刚出炉。a Dutch oven 荷兰锅（炖锅）

**refrigerator**
英 /rɪˈfrɪdʒərətə(r)/ 美 /rɪˈfrɪdʒərətər/
n. 冰箱
[义] An appliance that keeps food and drinks cold. 使食物和饮料保持低温的电器（refrigerator door 冰箱门，put sth. in the refrigerator 把东西放进冰箱）。可数。口语常简称 fridge。
[例] ① Milk should be kept in the refrigerator. 牛奶应放在冰箱里。 ② She opened the refrigerator and stared at the empty shelves. 她打开冰箱，盯着空空的搁架发呆。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *refrigerare*（使冷却），*re-*（再）+ *friger*（冷）+ -ator。
[族] refrigerate 冷藏, refrigeration 制冷, fridge 冰箱〔口语〕, frigid 寒冷的
[搭] refrigerator door 冰箱门, put sth. in the refrigerator 放进冰箱, refrigerator magnet 冰箱贴, side-by-side refrigerator 对开门冰箱
[联] fridge 冰箱, freezer 冷冻室, kitchen 厨房, cold 冷的, food 食物, appliance 电器, cool 冷却, ice 冰
[记] 可数。口语几乎总说 fridge。refrigerator 较正式或书面。freezer 指冷冻室或独立冰柜

**sink**
英/美 /sɪŋk/
n. 水槽；洗涤盆
v. 下沉；下降
[义] A fixed basin with a tap, used for washing dishes, hands, or food. 带水龙头、用于洗碗、洗手或洗食物的固定盆（kitchen sink 厨房水槽，bathroom sink 洗手池，everything but the kitchen sink 什么都带上〔习语〕）。可数。动词义：下沉、下降。
[例] ① She filled the sink with hot soapy water and started washing up. 她在水槽里放满热肥皂水，开始洗碗。 ② The boat began to sink after hitting the rock. 船撞礁后开始下沉。
[搭] kitchen sink 厨房水槽, bathroom sink 洗手池, sink tap 水龙头, block the sink 堵住水槽, rinse in the sink 在水槽里冲洗
[联] tap 水龙头, faucet 水龙头〔美〕, drain 排水, dish 盘子, wash 洗, kitchen 厨房, basin 盆, soap 肥皂
[记] 可数（名词）。sink 动词：sink / sank / sunk / sinking。名词 sink 与动词同形。everything but the kitchen sink 带太多东西

**microwave**
英/美 /ˈmaɪkrəweɪv/
n. 微波炉；微波
v. 用微波炉加热
[义] An oven that cooks or heats food using electromagnetic waves; the waves themselves. 用电磁波烹饪或加热食物的烤箱；微波本身（microwave oven 微波炉，microwave meal 微波即食餐，microwave popcorn 微波爆米花）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① He reheated the leftovers in the microwave for two minutes. 他用微波炉把剩菜加热了两分钟。 ② Microwave meals are convenient but often high in salt. 微波即食餐方便，但往往含盐量高。
[源] 复合词 micro-（微小）+ wave（波）→ 微波。
[族] micro- 微, wave 波

[联] oven 烤箱, kitchen 厨房, heat 加热, cook 烹饪, appliance 电器, fridge 冰箱, timer 定时器, convenient 方便的
[记] 可数/不可数。动词 microwave sth. 用微波加热。microwave-safe 形容词：可微波加热的容器

**dishwasher**

[义] A machine for washing dishes automatically. 自动清洗餐具的机器（load the dishwasher 装洗碗机，dishwasher tablet 洗碗块，built-in dishwasher 嵌入式洗碗机）。可数。
[例] ① After dinner, he loaded the dishwasher and pressed start. 晚饭后他把碗碟装进洗碗机，按下了启动键。 ② The dishwasher broke down, so we washed everything by hand. 洗碗机坏了，我们只好手洗所有餐具。

[联] sink 水槽, kitchen 厨房, dish 盘子, wash 洗, appliance 电器, soap 肥皂, clean 清洁, plate 盘子

**tap**
英/美 /tæp/
n. 水龙头〔尤英式〕；轻敲
v. 轻敲；开发利用
[义] A device for controlling the flow of water from a pipe (British English); a light hit. 控制管道水流量的装置（英式）；轻敲。动词：轻敲；开发利用（turn on the tap 开水龙头，tap water 自来水，on tap 随时可用〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① She turned on the tap and splashed cold water on her face. 她打开水龙头，用冷水拍了拍脸。 ② Clean drinking water is not always on tap in remote villages. 偏远村庄并非总能随时喝到洁净饮用水。

[联] faucet 水龙头〔美〕, sink 水槽, water 水, pipe 管道, shower 淋浴, bath 浴缸, drip 滴水, plumbing 管道

**faucet**
英/美 /ˈfɒsɪt/
n. 水龙头〔尤美式〕
[义] A device at the end of a pipe that controls the flow of water (American English). 管道末端控制水流的装置（美式英语）。可数。
[例] ① The faucet in the bathroom drips all night. 浴室水龙头整夜滴水。 ② She replaced the old faucet with a modern one-touch model. 她把旧水龙头换成了现代触控款。

[联] tap 水龙头〔英〕, sink 水槽, water 水, pipe 管道, drip 滴水, plumbing 管道, shower 淋浴, leak 漏水

**shower**
英/美 /ˈʃaʊə(r)/
n. 淋浴；阵雨；一大批
v. 淋浴；洒落
[义] A device or act of washing your body under a spray of water; a brief fall of rain; a large number of things arriving together. 喷淋洗浴的设备或行为；短时阵雨；大批同时出现的事物（take a shower 淋浴，shower head 花洒，baby shower 迎婴派对）。可数。
[例] ① I take a quick shower every morning before work. 我每天上班前快速冲个澡。 ② A sudden shower caught us without umbrellas in the park. 公园里一场骤雨突然袭来，我们没带伞。
[搭] take a shower 淋浴, shower head 花洒, power shower 强力淋浴, baby shower 迎婴派对, shower curtain 浴帘
[联] bath 浴缸, bathroom 浴室, tap 水龙头, water 水, soap 肥皂, towel 毛巾, rain 雨, spray 喷洒
[记] 可数。shower 动词：shower sb. with praise 对某人赞不绝口。bridal shower 新娘送礼派对（美）

**toilet**
英/美 /ˈtɔɪlət/
n. 马桶；卫生间；梳妆
[义] A fixed bowl with a seat, used for getting rid of urine and faeces; a room containing this; the process of washing and dressing. 带座圈的固定便器；含此设备的房间；梳洗打扮（flush the toilet 冲马桶，go to the toilet 上厕所，public toilet 公共卫生间）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Could you tell me where the toilet is? 请问卫生间在哪里？ ② Please flush the toilet after use. 用后请冲马桶。
[搭] flush the toilet 冲马桶, go to the toilet 上厕所, public toilet 公共卫生间, toilet paper 卫生纸, toilet seat 马桶座圈
[联] bathroom 浴室, bath 浴缸, shower 淋浴, sink 洗手池, flush 冲水, WC 卫生间〔英〕, restroom 洗手间〔美〕, plumbing 管道
[记] 可数/不可数。英式 toilet 可指房间；美式更常说 restroom / bathroom。toilet 旧义「梳妆」今罕用

**bath**
英/美 /bɑːθ/
n. 浴缸；洗澡；浴盆
v. 给……洗澡〔英〕
[义] A large container for sitting in while washing your body; the act of washing in one; the liquid in it. 坐入其中洗浴的大容器；洗浴行为；其中液体（take a bath 泡澡，bubble bath 泡泡浴，bath towel 浴巾）。可数/不可数。动词 bath 英式指给婴儿等洗澡。
[例] ① She ran a hot bath and soaked for twenty minutes. 她放了一缸热水，泡了二十分钟。 ② The baby loves his evening bath. 宝宝喜欢晚上的洗澡时间。
[搭] take a bath 泡澡, bubble bath 泡泡浴, bath towel 浴巾, run a bath 放洗澡水, hot bath 热水澡
[联] shower 淋浴, bathroom 浴室, bathtub 浴缸, soap 肥皂, towel 毛巾, water 水, soak 浸泡, relax 放松
[记] 可数/不可数。take a bath 美式口语可喻「吃大亏」。bath 与 bathe /beɪð/ 相关：bathe 游泳/洗澡〔较正式〕

**bathtub**
英/美 /ˈbɑːθtʌb/
n. 浴缸
[义] A large fixed container that you fill with water to sit in and wash your body. 固定安装、注水洗浴的大型容器（climb into the bathtub 进浴缸, bathtub drain 浴缸排水口, freestanding bathtub 独立浴缸）。可数。
[例] ① The hotel room has a deep bathtub and a separate shower. 酒店房间有深浴缸和独立淋浴。 ② She scrubbed the bathtub after every guest checked out. 每位客人退房后她都刷浴缸。
[搭] climb into the bathtub 进浴缸, bathtub drain 浴缸排水口, freestanding bathtub 独立浴缸, fill the bathtub 放浴缸水, clean the bathtub 清洁浴缸
[联] bath 洗澡, bathroom 浴室, shower 淋浴, water 水, soap 肥皂, towel 毛巾, faucet 水龙头, soak 浸泡, tile 瓷砖, hotel 酒店
[记] 可数：a bathtub。美式常简称 tub。bathtub 指容器；take a bath 指行为

**curtain**
英/美 /ˈkɜːtən/
n. 窗帘；幕布
v. 覆盖；遮蔽
[义] A piece of material hung to cover a window or divide a room; the screen in a theatre that rises at the start of a play. 悬挂以遮挡窗户或分隔房间的织物；剧院演出开始时升起的幕布（draw the curtains 拉上窗帘，curtain call 谢幕，curtain of rain 雨幕）。可数。
[例] ① She drew the curtains to block the morning sun. 她拉上窗帘挡住晨光。 ② The audience applauded until the curtain fell. 观众鼓掌直到幕布落下。
[搭] draw the curtains 拉上窗帘, curtain rod 窗帘杆, shower curtain 浴帘, curtain call 谢幕, lace curtains 蕾丝窗帘
[联] window 窗户, blind 百叶窗, drape 帘〔美〕, fabric 织物, light 光线, privacy 隐私, stage 舞台, decorate 装饰
[记] 可数。curtain 动词：curtains of smoke 烟幕。curtain raiser 开场短剧；curtain call 谢幕

**lamp**
英/美 /læmp/
n. 灯；台灯
[义] A device that produces light, especially one with a bulb and shade. 发光的装置，尤指带灯泡和灯罩的（desk lamp 台灯，floor lamp 落地灯，street lamp 路灯，oil lamp 油灯）。可数。
[例] ① She turned on the bedside lamp and read for a while. 她打开床头灯读了一会儿书。 ② Street lamps lit the path through the park at night. 夜里路灯照亮了穿过公园的小路。
[源] 源自希腊语 *lampas*（火炬），经拉丁语 *lampas* 入英。
[族] lantern 灯笼, chandelier 吊灯
[搭] desk lamp 台灯, floor lamp 落地灯, bedside lamp 床头灯, turn on the lamp 开灯, oil lamp 油灯
[联] light 灯/光, bulb 灯泡, shade 灯罩, switch 开关, electricity 电, candle 蜡烛, bright 明亮的, illuminate 照明
[记] 可数。lamp 偏具体灯具；light 更泛指光源或照明。lamppost 路灯柱

**blind**
英/美 /blaɪnd/
n. 百叶窗；卷帘
adj. 盲的；盲目的
v. 使失明；蒙蔽
[义] A covering for a window made of slats or fabric that can be raised or adjusted. 由板条或织物制成、可升降或调节的窗户遮挡物（window blinds 百叶窗，Venetian blind 百叶帘，roller blind 卷帘）。可数。形容词：盲的；盲目的。
[例] ① She lowered the blinds to keep the room cool in summer. 夏天她放下百叶窗以保持室内凉爽。 ② The office has blinds instead of curtains for a cleaner look. 办公室用百叶窗代替窗帘，看起来更利落。
[搭] window blinds 百叶窗, Venetian blind 百叶帘, roller blind 卷帘, pull down the blinds 放下百叶帘, blind spot 盲点
[联] curtain 窗帘, window 窗户, shade 遮光帘, light 光线, privacy 隐私, adjust 调节, slat 板条, cover 遮挡
[记] 可数（名词）。blind 形容词极常见。blind spot 喻盲点；blind date 相亲（未见过面）

**fireplace**
英/美 /ˈfaɪəpleɪs/
n. 壁炉
[义] A structure built into a wall of a room, where a fire can be made for heat or atmosphere. 砌在房间墙内的结构，可生火取暖或营造氛围（open fireplace 开放式壁炉，electric fireplace 电壁炉，sit by the fireplace 坐在壁炉旁）。可数。
[例] ① They gathered around the fireplace on cold winter evenings. 寒冷的冬夜，他们围坐在壁炉旁。 ② The cottage has a stone fireplace that dates back a century. 小屋有一座百年历史的石砌壁炉。
[搭] open fireplace 开放式壁炉, electric fireplace 电壁炉, sit by the fireplace 坐在壁炉旁, fireplace mantel 壁炉台, light a fire in the fireplace 在壁炉里生火
[联] fire 火, chimney 烟囱, wood 木柴, warm 温暖的, living room 客厅, cosy 舒适的, flame 火焰, smoke 烟
[记] 可数。复合词 fire + place。mantelpiece / mantel 壁炉台架。fireplace 与 furnace（工业锅炉）不同

**cottage**
英/美 /ˈkɒtɪdʒ/
n. 小屋；村舍
[义] A small house, especially in the countryside or as a holiday home. 小房子，尤指乡间或度假用（country cottage 乡间小屋，holiday cottage 度假小屋，cottage industry 家庭手工业〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① They rented a cottage by the lake for the summer. 他们夏天在湖边租了一间小屋。 ② The stone cottage had roses climbing up the walls. 石砌小屋的墙上爬满了玫瑰。
[源] 源自中世纪拉丁语 *cotagium*，与 cot（小棚）相关 → 小住所。
[族] cot 小床/小屋〔罕〕
[搭] country cottage 乡间小屋, holiday cottage 度假小屋, thatched cottage 茅草屋, cottage garden 村舍花园, rent a cottage 租小屋
[联] house 房屋, villa 别墅, hut 棚屋, countryside 乡村, rural 乡下的, cosy 舒适的, garden 花园, retreat 隐居处
[记] 可数。cottage 偏英式田园意象；美式 countryside cabin 亦常见。cottage cheese 茅屋芝士，与 cottage 房屋义无关

**mansion**
英/美 /ˈmænʃən/
n. 豪宅；大厦
[义] A very large, impressive house; a large block of flats (British, formal). 非常大、气派显赫的住宅；大型公寓楼（英式正式）。可数。
[例] ① The film opens with a shot of a grand mansion on a hill. 影片开场是一座山丘上宏伟豪宅的镜头。 ② They toured the historic mansion and its landscaped gardens. 他们参观了那座历史豪宅及其景观花园。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *mansio*（住所、停留），*manere*（停留）→ 大型住所。
[族] remain 停留, permanent 永久的, manor 庄园
[搭] grand mansion 宏伟豪宅, historic mansion 历史豪宅, mansion block 公寓大楼〔英〕, country mansion 乡间豪宅, mansion tax 豪宅税
[联] house 房屋, villa 别墅, palace 宫殿, estate 庄园, luxury 奢华, wealthy 富有的, ballroom 舞厅, garden 花园
[记] 可数。mansion 强调规模与气派；house 中性。manor 庄园；manor house 庄园宅邸

**condo**
英/美 /ˈkɒndəʊ/
n. 产权公寓〔缩写〕
[义] A condominium: a flat that is owned rather than rented, or the building containing such flats (chiefly American). 产权公寓：自有而非租赁的单元，或含此类单元的建筑（主要美式）。可数。全称 condominium。
[例] ① They bought a condo near the beach as a second home. 他们在海边买了一套产权公寓作第二居所。 ② Condo fees cover maintenance of shared areas like the pool and gym. 公寓费涵盖泳池、健身房等公共区域的维护。
[源] 缩写自 condominium，拉丁语 *con-*（共同）+ *dominium*（所有权）→ 共同产权。
[族] condominium 产权公寓, dominate 支配〔不同义〕
[搭] buy a condo 买产权公寓, condo association 业主协会, condo fees 公寓管理费, beach condo 海滨公寓, high-rise condo 高层公寓
[联] apartment 公寓, flat 公寓〔英〕, owner 业主, rent 租金, mortgage 按揭, HOA 业主协会, property 房产, loft 阁楼公寓
[记] 可数。美式 condo 强调自有产权；英式更常说 flat（租或买视情况）。condo fees 每月管理费

n. 阁楼；开放式大空间
v. 把……储存在阁楼
[义] A space under the roof used for storage; a large open living area converted from industrial space (American). 屋顶下用于储物的空间；由工业空间改造的大开间居住区（美式）。可数。
[例] ① Old boxes were piled high in the loft. 旧箱子高高堆在阁楼里。 ② They converted a warehouse into a stylish loft with exposed brick walls. 他们把仓库改造成带裸露砖墙的时尚 loft。
[搭] loft conversion 阁楼改造, loft apartment 阁楼式公寓, storage loft 储物阁楼, loft space 阁楼空间, artist's loft 艺术家工作室
[联] attic 阁楼, apartment 公寓, warehouse 仓库, open-plan 开放式的, ceiling 天花板, beam 梁, studio 工作室, urban 城市的
[记] 可数。英式 loft = 阁楼；美式 loft 常指工业风大开间公寓。loft 动词：loft a ball 高高击出（球）

**villa**
英/美 /ˈvɪlə/
n. 别墅；庄园宅邸
[义] A large house in the countryside or abroad, especially one used for holidays; a Roman country house. 乡间或国外的较大住宅，尤指度假用；古罗马乡间宅邸。可数。
[例] ① They spent two weeks in a villa overlooking the Mediterranean. 他们在俯瞰地中海的别墅里住了两周。 ② The resort offers private villas with pools and sea views. 度假村提供带泳池和海景的私人别墅。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *villa*（乡间住宅、农场），与 village（村庄）同族。

[记] 可数。villa 度假语境极常见；cottage 更小更朴素。mansion 更大更气派。villa 在古罗马史中指乡间庄园

**warehouse**
英/美 /ˈweəhaʊs/
n. 仓库；货栈
v. 存入仓库
[义] A large building where goods are stored before they are sold or distributed. 出售或分发前存放货物的大型建筑（warehouse store 仓储式商店，data warehouse 数据仓库〔IT〕）。可数。
[例] ① The furniture is stored in a warehouse until the new house is ready. 家具存放在仓库里，等新房子准备好。 ② Online retailers rely on huge warehouses near major cities. 网购平台依赖大城市附近的大型仓库。
[源] 复合词 ware（货物）+ house（房屋）→ 存放货物的房屋。

英/美 /ˈdaɪnɪŋ ruːm/

[例] ① The family gathers in the dining room for holiday dinners. 家人节假日聚餐都在餐厅。 ② A chandelier hangs above the dining room table. 餐厅餐桌上方挂着一盏吊灯。
[搭] formal dining room 正式餐厅, dining room table 餐桌, dining room chairs 餐椅, open dining room 开放式餐厅, seat in the dining room 在餐厅就座

**pantry**

n. 食品储藏室；茶水间
[义] A small room or cupboard where food, dishes, and cooking equipment are kept. 存放食物、餐具和厨具的小房间或柜橱（walk-in pantry 步入式食品库，kitchen pantry 厨房储藏间，pantry staples 常备食品）。可数。
[例] ① She stocked the pantry with canned goods before winter. 入冬前她在储藏室里囤了罐头食品。 ② The walk-in pantry has shelves from floor to ceiling. 步入式食品库里的架子从地板一直延伸到天花板。

[例] ① The laundry is piling up—I need to do it this weekend. 待洗衣服越堆越多——这周末得洗了。 ② She carried the basket down to the laundry room in the basement. 她把洗衣篮拿到地下室的洗衣房。

[义] A room in a house for a baby or young child; a place where young children are cared for; a place where plants are grown. 家中供婴幼儿使用的房间；照料幼儿的场所；培育植物的苗圃（baby's nursery 婴儿房，nursery school 幼儿园，plant nursery 苗圃）。可数。
[例] ① They painted the nursery pale yellow before the baby arrived. 宝宝出生前他们把婴儿房漆成了淡黄色。 ② She drops her toddler at the nursery every morning. 她每天早上把幼儿送到托儿所。

[记] 可数。nursery 植物义在园艺语境常见。nursery rhyme 童谣；night nursery 旧时指儿童晚间卧室

**study**
