[例] ① He retreated to his study to finish the report. 他躲进书房写完报告。 ② The house has a small study with built-in bookshelves. 这房子有个带嵌入式书架的小书房。
[搭] home study 家庭书房, study desk 书桌, study lamp 台灯, private study 私人书房, study room 书房
[联] desk 书桌, bookshelf 书架, office 办公室, read 阅读, work 工作, quiet 安静的, library 图书馆, computer 电脑
[记] 可数（房间义）。study 动词极常见。study 与 office 相比更偏居家私人空间；office 可指家中办公区但更正式

**closet**
英/美 /ˈklɒzɪt/
n. 壁橱；衣帽间〔尤美式〕
adj. 隐秘的
v. 把……关入狭小空间
[义] A small room or cupboard for storing clothes; used in compounds for secret or private activities. 存放衣物的小房间或柜橱（walk-in closet 步入式衣帽间，closet door 壁橱门，skeleton in the closet 家丑〔习语〕）。可数。
[例] ① Her shoes are organized on racks in the closet. 她的鞋子整齐摆在衣帽间架子上。 ② He came out of the closet and told his family he was gay. 他公开出柜，告诉家人自己是同性恋。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *clausus*（关闭），*claudere*（关闭）→ 可关闭的储物空间。
[族] close 关闭, enclosure 封闭空间, disclose 揭露
[搭] walk-in closet 步入式衣帽间, closet door 壁橱门, closet organizer 衣柜收纳架, come out of the closet 出柜/公开秘密, built-in closet 嵌入式壁橱
[联] wardrobe 衣柜, cupboard 橱柜, clothes 衣服, hanger 衣架, shelf 搁板, bedroom 卧室, storage 储藏, skeleton 骷髅
[记] 可数。美式 closet = 英式 wardrobe（衣柜/衣帽间）。come out of the closet 出柜；skeleton in the closet 家丑

**porch**
英/美 /pɔːtʃ/
n. 门廊；阳台
[义] A covered structure at the entrance to a building, or a covered area along the outside of a house. 建筑入口处的有顶结构，或沿房屋外墙的有顶区域（front porch 前门廊，sit on the porch 坐在门廊上，screened porch 纱窗门廊）。可数。
[例] ① They sat on the porch watching the sunset. 他们坐在门廊上看日落。 ② A wreath hung on the front porch during the holidays. 节日期间前门廊上挂着花环。
[搭] front porch 前门廊, back porch 后门廊, screened porch 纱窗门廊, sit on the porch 坐在门廊上, porch light 门廊灯
[联] door 门, entrance 入口, balcony 阳台, veranda 游廊, steps 台阶, garden 花园, swing 秋千, welcome 欢迎
[记] 可数。porch 美式住宅文化意象强（front porch politics 邻里政治）。veranda 游廊，常更大、更敞

**terrace**
英/美 /ˈterəs/
n. 露台；梯田
v. 使成梯田
[义] A flat paved area next to a house where you can sit; one of a series of flat areas cut into a slope. 房屋旁可就座的平整铺砌平台；山坡上开垦的梯田（roof terrace 屋顶露台，terrace house 联排屋〔英〕，dine on the terrace 在露台用餐）。可数。
[例] ① We had breakfast on the terrace overlooking the valley. 我们在俯瞰山谷的露台上吃早餐。 ② Rice is grown on terraces carved into the hillside. 水稻种在山坡开垦的梯田上。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *terra*（土地）→ 平地平台。
[族] terrain 地形, territory 领土, terrestrial 陆地的
[搭] roof terrace 屋顶露台, terrace house 联排屋, dine on the terrace 在露台用餐, garden terrace 花园露台, wide terrace 宽阔露台
[联] balcony 阳台, patio 庭院, garden 花园, outdoor 户外的, view 视野, paved 铺砌的, slope 斜坡, furniture 家具
[记] 可数。terrace 英式常指联排屋（terraced house）。与 patio 相比 terrace 多指铺砌平台、可附于楼上

**patio**
英/美 /ˈpætiəʊ/
n. 庭院；露台
[义] A paved area next to a house where people sit or eat outdoors. 房屋旁供户外坐卧或用餐的铺砌区域（back patio 后院庭院，patio furniture 庭院家具，patio door 庭院门）。可数。
[例] ① They set up patio furniture for the summer barbecue. 他们为夏日烧烤摆好了庭院家具。 ② Sliding patio doors open onto the garden. 滑动庭院门通向花园。
[源] 源自西班牙语 *patio*（庭院），与拉丁语 *patere*（敞开）相关。
[搭] back patio 后院庭院, patio furniture 庭院家具, patio door 庭院门, patio table 庭院桌, paved patio 铺砌庭院
[联] terrace 露台, garden 花园, balcony 阳台, outdoor 户外的, barbecue 烧烤, furniture 家具, lawn 草坪, shade 阴凉
[记] 可数。patio 美式极常见；偏地面铺砌后院。terrace 可指屋顶或楼上平台；patio 多指与房屋平齐的户外区

**chimney**
英/美 /ˈtʃɪmni/
n. 烟囱；烟道
[义] A vertical pipe or structure through which smoke and gases escape from a fire or furnace. 火或炉子产生的烟和气体排出的竖管或结构（chimney stack 烟囱体，chimney sweep 扫烟囱工人，Santa down the chimney 圣诞老人从烟囱下来〔文化〕）。可数。
[例] ① Smoke rose from the chimney on a cold winter morning. 寒冷的冬晨，烟囱升起袅袅炊烟。 ② The chimney needs cleaning before we use the fireplace again. 再用壁炉前得先清理烟囱。
[搭] chimney stack 烟囱体, chimney pot 烟囱帽, chimney sweep 扫烟囱工, chimney breast 壁炉墙, block the chimney 堵住烟道
[联] fireplace 壁炉, roof 屋顶, smoke 烟, fire 火, brick 砖, flue 烟道, Santa 圣诞老人, sweep 清扫
[记] 可数。chimney breast 指壁炉突出的墙垛。chimney 与 funnel（漏斗/烟囱〔船〕）不同场

**foundation**
英/美 /faʊnˈdeɪʃn/
n. 地基；基础；基金会
[义] The solid base on which a building is built; the underlying basis of something; an organization funded to support a cause. 建筑物所依的坚实基座；某事物的根本基础；资助某项事业的组织（lay the foundation 奠定基础，foundation stone 基石，crack in the foundation 地基裂缝）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Engineers checked the foundation before approving the renovation. 工程师在批准翻新前检查了地基。 ② Trust and respect are the foundation of any good relationship. 信任与尊重是任何良好关系的基础。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *fundare*（奠定），*fundus*（底部）→ 底部 → 地基。
[族] found 建立, fundamental 基本的, fund 资金
[搭] lay the foundation 奠定基础, foundation stone 基石, crack in the foundation 地基裂缝, solid foundation 坚实基础, charitable foundation 慈善基金会
[联] basement 地下室, building 建筑物, structure 结构, brick 砖, concrete 混凝土, ground 地面, basis 基础, establish 建立
[记] 可数/不可数。foundation 化妆义「粉底」可数。foundations 复数可指地基整体

**beam**
英/美 /biːm/
n. 梁；光束
v. 眉开眼笑；发射
[义] A long thick piece of wood or metal used to support a building; a ray of light. 支撑建筑物的长条粗木或金属；光线。动词指满面笑容或发射（wooden beam 木梁，steel beam 钢梁，beam of light 一束光）。可数。
[例] ① Exposed wooden beams give the loft a rustic charm. 裸露的木梁给这间 loft 增添了乡村魅力。 ② A beam of sunlight fell across the kitchen table. 一束阳光落在厨房餐桌上。
[源] 源自古英语 *bēam*（树、梁），日耳曼语族。
[搭] wooden beam 木梁, steel beam 钢梁, exposed beam 裸露梁, beam of light 一束光, support beam 支撑梁
[联] roof 屋顶, ceiling 天花板, structure 结构, loft 阁楼公寓, light 光, support 支撑, timber 木材, column 柱子
[记] 可数。beam 动词：beam with pride 满面春风。off the beam 偏离目标〔俚〕。beam 与 girder（大梁）相比 beam 更通用

**fence**
英/美 /fens/
n. 栅栏；围栏
v. 围起来；击剑
[义] A structure that divides or encloses an area, usually made of wood or wire. 分隔或围合区域的结构，通常由木或金属丝制成（garden fence 花园围栏，fence post 栅栏柱，sit on the fence 骑墙观望〔习语〕）。可数。
[例] ① They built a wooden fence around the vegetable garden. 他们在菜园周围建了木栅栏。 ② The property is enclosed by a high stone fence. 这处房产被高石墙围起来。
[搭] garden fence 花园围栏, fence post 栅栏柱, chain-link fence 铁丝网围栏, sit on the fence 骑墙观望, mend a fence 修补围栏
[联] gate 大门, wall 墙, yard 院子, garden 花园, boundary 边界, neighbour 邻居, wire 金属丝, enclose 围合
[记] 可数。sit on the fence 喻骑墙、不表态。fence 动词：击剑。fence off 用栅栏隔开

**gate**
英/美 /ɡeɪt/
n. 大门；登机口
[义] A hinged barrier used to close an opening in a fence or wall; an exit at an airport where passengers board. 栅栏或墙上开口处可开合的屏障；机场旅客登机的出口（garden gate 花园门，front gate 前门，boarding gate 登机口）。可数。
[例] ① She heard the gate creak open in the wind. 她听见大门在风中吱呀作响。 ② Our flight leaves from gate twelve. 我们的航班从十二号登机口出发。
[搭] garden gate 花园门, front gate 前门, boarding gate 登机口, iron gate 铁门, close the gate 关门
[联] fence 栅栏, door 门, wall 墙, entrance 入口, lock 锁, key 钥匙, airport 机场, driveway 车道
[记] 可数。gate 机场义极常见。gatecrasher 擅闯派对者。-gate 后缀喻丑闻（Watergate 水门事件）

**driveway**
英/美 /ˈdraɪvweɪ/
n. 车道
[义] A short private road leading from a street to a house or garage. 从街道通往房屋或车库的短私家路（park in the driveway 停在车道上，snow on the driveway 车道积雪）。可数。
[例] ① Two cars were parked side by side in the driveway. 车道上并排停着两辆车。 ② He shovelled snow off the driveway every morning in winter. 冬天他每天早上铲车道上的雪。
[搭] park in the driveway 停在车道上, long driveway 长车道, gravel driveway 碎石车道, shovel the driveway 铲车道, pull into the driveway 驶入车道
[联] garage 车库, gate 大门, car 汽车, park 停车, path 小路, pavement 路面, suburban 郊区的, house 房屋
[记] 可数。driveway 专指通往住宅的私家车道；drive 作名词可指较短车道但不如 driveway 精确

**brick**
英/美 /brɪk/
n. 砖；砖块
v. 用砖砌
[义] A small rectangular block of baked clay used for building walls. 用于砌墙的小块矩形烧土块（brick wall 砖墙，red brick 红砖，brick house 砖房）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The old factory was built of red brick. 那座老工厂是红砖砌的。 ② They repaired the garden wall with new bricks. 他们用新砖修补了花园围墙。
[源] 源自古法语 *brique*，可能经荷兰语，与 break（碎片）有远亲关系。
[搭] brick wall 砖墙, red brick 红砖, brick house 砖房, lay bricks 砌砖, exposed brick 裸露砖墙
[联] wall 墙, stone 石头, cement 水泥, mortar 灰浆, tile 瓷砖, building 建筑物, chimney 烟囱, pavement 铺路石
[记] 可数/不可数。a brick 指一块砖；bricks 作材料可不可数。brick 俚语：a brick 可靠的人〔旧〕。hit a brick wall 碰壁

**tile**
英/美 /taɪl/
n. 瓷砖；瓦片
v. 铺瓷砖
[义] A thin flat piece of baked clay or other material used for covering roofs, walls, or floors. 用于覆盖屋顶、墙面或地面的薄平烧土块或其他材料（floor tiles 地砖，roof tiles 瓦片，ceramic tile 瓷砖）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① White tiles made the bathroom feel bright and clean. 白色瓷砖让浴室显得明亮洁净。 ② They retiled the kitchen floor with patterned tiles. 他们用花纹地砖重铺了厨房地面。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *tegula*（瓦片），*tegere*（覆盖）→ 覆盖之物。
[族] textile 纺织品〔tegere 同族〕, protect 保护
[搭] floor tiles 地砖, roof tiles 瓦片, ceramic tile 瓷砖, wall tiles 墙砖, tile the bathroom 给浴室铺砖
[联] brick 砖, floor 地板, wall 墙, roof 屋顶, bathroom 浴室, kitchen 厨房, grout 填缝剂, pattern 图案
[记] 可数/不可数。tile 动词：tile a roof 铺瓦。tiling 铺砖/瓦作业。tile 与 plate（板）不同材质场

**paint**
英/美 /peɪnt/
n. 油漆；颜料
v. 刷漆；绘画
[义] A liquid substance spread on surfaces to colour or protect them; to cover with paint or create a picture. 涂覆于表面以着色或保护的液态物质；刷漆或绘画（wall paint 墙漆，paint the walls 刷墙，wet paint 油漆未干）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① We chose a soft grey paint for the bedroom walls. 我们给卧室墙选了柔和的灰色漆。 ② The walls were painted white to reflect more light. 墙壁刷成白色以反射更多光线。
[搭] wall paint 墙漆, paint the walls 刷墙, wet paint 油漆未干, oil paint 油漆, touch up the paint 补漆
[联] brush 刷子, colour 颜色, wall 墙, decorate 装饰, wallpaper 壁纸, coat 涂层, canvas 画布, renovate 翻新
[记] 不可数/可数。paint 动词：paint a room 粉刷房间。nail polish 指甲油。paint 与 dye（染料）不同用途

**wallpaper**
英/美 /ˈwɔːlpeɪpə(r)/
n. 壁纸；墙纸
v. 贴壁纸
[义] Paper with patterns or pictures pasted onto walls as decoration. 带图案或图画、贴于墙面作装饰的纸（strip of wallpaper 一条壁纸，wallpaper paste 壁纸胶，peel off wallpaper 撕下壁纸）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① They stripped the old wallpaper before repainting the room. 他们在重新粉刷前撕掉了旧壁纸。 ② Floral wallpaper gave the dining room a cosy feel. 花卉壁纸让餐厅有了温馨感。
[搭] strip of wallpaper 一条壁纸, wallpaper paste 壁纸胶, peel off wallpaper 撕壁纸, floral wallpaper 花卉壁纸, hang wallpaper 贴壁纸
[联] paint 油漆, wall 墙, decorate 装饰, pattern 图案, paste 糨糊, room 房间, peel 剥落, interior 室内的
[记] 不可数/可数。wallpaper 动词：wallpaper a room 给房间贴壁纸。desktop wallpaper 电脑桌面背景，喻义延伸

**insulation**
英/美 /ˌɪnsjəˈleɪʃn/
n. 隔热；隔音；绝缘
[义] Material used to prevent heat, sound, or electricity from passing through. 用于阻止热、声或电通过的材料（wall insulation 墙体隔热, thermal insulation 隔热, sound insulation 隔音）。不可数。
[例] ① Good insulation keeps the house warm in winter and cool in summer. 良好的隔热让房子冬暖夏凉。 ② They added insulation in the attic to cut energy bills. 他们在阁楼加了隔热层以降低能源账单。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *insulare*（使成岛、隔离），*insula*（岛）→ 隔绝。
[族] insulate 使隔热/绝缘, island 岛屿, isolate 隔离
[搭] wall insulation 墙体隔热, thermal insulation 隔热, sound insulation 隔音, install insulation 安装隔热层, loft insulation 阁楼隔热
[联] attic 阁楼, wall 墙, heat 热, energy 能源, warm 温暖的, foam 泡沫, material 材料, efficiency 效率
[记] 不可数。insulation 建筑语境指保温隔音材料。insulate 动词；insulator 绝缘体

**towel**
英/美 /ˈtaʊəl/
n. 毛巾；浴巾
v. 用毛巾擦干
[义] A piece of thick absorbent cloth used for drying yourself or surfaces. 用于擦干身体或表面的厚吸水布（bath towel 浴巾，paper towel 纸巾，throw in the towel 认输〔习语〕）。可数。
[例] ① She wrapped a towel around her hair after the shower. 淋浴后她用毛巾包住头发。 ② Grab a paper towel to wipe the spill on the counter. 拿张纸巾擦掉台面上的泼洒物。
[搭] bath towel 浴巾, hand towel 手巾, paper towel 纸巾, kitchen towel 厨房抹布, throw in the towel 认输
[联] shower 淋浴, bath 浴缸, soap 肥皂, dry 擦干, bathroom 浴室, laundry 洗衣, absorb 吸收, wipe 擦拭
[记] 可数。throw in the towel 源自拳击认输。towel 动词：towel off 擦干身体

**hanger**
英/美 /ˈhæŋə(r)/
n. 衣架；悬挂物
[义] A shaped piece of wood, plastic, or metal used to hang clothes on. 用于挂衣服的成形木、塑料或金属件（coat hanger 衣架，clothes hanger 晾衣架，wire hanger 铁丝衣架）。可数。
[例] ① She hung the dress on a hanger and put it in the wardrobe. 她把连衣裙挂在衣架上放进衣柜。 ② Wire hangers can leave marks on delicate fabrics. 铁丝衣架会在精细面料上留下印子。
[搭] coat hanger 衣架, clothes hanger 晾衣架, wire hanger 铁丝衣架, hang on a hanger 挂在衣架上, wooden hanger 木衣架
[联] wardrobe 衣柜, closet 壁橱, clothes 衣服, hook 钩子, rack 架子, shirt 衬衫, fold 折叠, laundry 洗衣
[记] 可数。hanger 与 hangar（飞机库）拼写不同。coat hanger 亦可喻简易武器〔俚〕

**key**
英/美 /kiː/
n. 钥匙；关键
v. 用键入；调整
adj. 关键的
[义] A metal object used to open a lock; something essential; to enter data with a keyboard. 开锁用的金属物件；关键事物；键盘输入。可数（key to the door 门钥匙，key factor 关键因素，key in 键入）。
[例] ① I locked the door and put the key in my pocket. 我锁上门，把钥匙放进口袋。 ② She keyed in the password and opened the file. 她键入密码打开了文件。
[搭] key to the door 门钥匙, house key 房门钥匙, key ring 钥匙圈, lose the key 丢了钥匙, key factor 关键因素
[联] lock 锁, door 门, gate 大门, pocket 口袋, open 打开, password 密码, crucial 关键的, entrance 入口
[记] 可数。under lock and key 妥善锁好。key 音乐义「调」；Florida Keys 佛罗里达群岛

**lock**
英/美 /lɒk/
n. 锁；一绺头发
v. 锁上；卡住
[义] A device that keeps a door closed until opened with a key; a section of hair; to fasten with a lock. 须用钥匙才能打开的闭门装置；一绺头发；上锁（door lock 门锁，lock the door 锁门，combination lock 密码锁）。可数。
[例] ① She checked the lock twice before going to bed. 睡前她检查了两次门锁。 ② Don't forget to lock the front door when you leave. 出门别忘了锁前门。
[搭] door lock 门锁, lock the door 锁门, combination lock 密码锁, deadbolt lock 死锁, under lock and key 锁好
[联] key 钥匙, door 门, safe 保险箱, secure 安全的, bolt 门闩, unlock 开锁, padlock 挂锁, burglar 窃贼
[记] 可数。lock 动词：lock / locked / locked / locking。lock 与 clock 拼写勿混

**handle**
英/美 /ˈhændl/
n. 把手；柄
v. 处理；搬运
[义] The part of an object designed to be held; to deal with or manage something. 物体上供握持的部分；处理、应付。可数（door handle 门把手，cup handle 杯柄，handle a problem 处理问题）。
[例] ① The door handle was loose and needed tightening. 门把手松了，需要拧紧。 ② She knows how to handle difficult customers calmly. 她懂得如何冷静应对难缠的客户。
[搭] door handle 门把手, cup handle 杯柄, drawer handle 抽屉把手, grab the handle 抓住把手, handle with care 小心轻放
[联] door 门, drawer 抽屉, knob 球形把手, grip 抓握, pull 拉, open 打开, touch 触摸, lever 杠杆
[记] 可数。handle 动词极常见。handlebar 自行车把手。broom handle 扫帚柄

**switch**
英/美 /swɪtʃ/
n. 开关；转换
v. 切换；转换
[义] A device for turning electricity on and off; a change from one thing to another. 控制电流通断的装置；从一事到另一事的转换（light switch 电灯开关，flip the switch 扳动开关，switch off 关掉）。可数。
[例] ① She flipped the switch and the kitchen lights came on. 她扳动开关，厨房灯亮了。 ② He switched jobs and moved to a new city. 他换了工作，搬到了新城市。
[搭] light switch 电灯开关, flip the switch 扳动开关, switch off 关掉, switch on 打开, power switch 电源开关
[联] lamp 灯, light 灯, bulb 灯泡, electricity 电, turn 转动, off 关, on 开, button 按钮
[记] 可数。switch 动词：switch between 在……之间切换。switch 铁路义「道岔」；switchblade 弹簧刀

**bulb**
英/美 /bʌlb/
n. 灯泡；球茎
[义] The glass part of an electric lamp that produces light; a rounded underground plant stem. 电灯发光的玻璃部分；圆形地下茎（light bulb 灯泡，change a bulb 换灯泡，LED bulb LED 灯泡）。可数。
[例] ① The bulb in the hallway burned out and needed replacing. 走廊灯泡烧坏了，需要更换。 ② She screwed the new bulb into the lamp socket. 她把新灯泡拧进灯座。
[搭] light bulb 灯泡, change a bulb 换灯泡, LED bulb LED 灯泡, burnt-out bulb 烧坏的灯泡, screw in a bulb 拧入灯泡
[联] lamp 灯, light 灯, switch 开关, electricity 电, socket 插座, bright 明亮的, watt 瓦特, glow 发光
[记] 可数。bulb 植物义「鳞茎」：tulip bulbs 郁金香球茎。compact fluorescent bulb 节能灯泡

**plug**
英/美 /plʌɡ/
n. 插头；塞子
v. 塞住；推广
[义] A device on the end of a wire that connects an appliance to electricity; something that blocks an opening. 电线末端连接电器与电源的装置；堵塞开口之物（plug in 插上电源，pull the plug 拔掉插头/终止〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① She plugged the charger into the socket by the bed. 她把充电器插进床边的插座。 ② Plug the sink before filling it with water. 往水槽放水前先塞上塞子。
[搭] plug in 插上电源, pull the plug 拔掉插头, power plug 电源插头, spark plug 火花塞, plug the gap 填补缺口
[联] socket 插座, wire 电线, electricity 电, appliance 电器, charge 充电, sink 水槽, cable 电缆, connect 连接
[记] 可数。plug 动词：plug a product 推广产品。plug and play 即插即用。British plug 英式三脚插头较大

**socket**
英/美 /ˈsɒkɪt/
n. 插座；孔穴
[义] A device in a wall or on equipment into which a plug fits to connect to electricity; a hollow part that fits around something. 墙上或设备上接入插头的电力接口；套接的孔穴（power socket 电源插座，wall socket 墙插，eye socket 眼眶）。可数。
[例] ① There are no sockets near the desk, so she uses an extension cord. 书桌旁没有插座，她用了延长线。 ② He plugged the lamp into the socket behind the sofa. 他把台灯插进沙发后面的插座。
[搭] power socket 电源插座, wall socket 墙插, socket wrench 套筒扳手, plug into a socket 插入插座, empty socket 空插座
[联] plug 插头, electricity 电, wire 电线, wall 墙, charge 充电, appliance 电器, outlet 插座〔美〕, cable 电缆
[记] 可数。美式 wall outlet 与 socket 同义日常。socket 计算机义「套接字」；eye socket 眼眶

**drain**
英/美 /dreɪn/
n. 排水管；下水道
v. 排空；耗尽
[义] A pipe that carries away waste water or liquid; to remove liquid or gradually use up resources. 排走废水或液体的管道；排空液体或逐渐耗尽资源（kitchen drain 厨房排水管，block the drain 堵住下水道，brain drain 人才外流〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① Food scraps clogged the kitchen drain. 食物残渣堵住了厨房下水道。 ② Leave the pasta to drain in the colander. 把意面留在滤锅里沥干。
[搭] kitchen drain 厨房排水管, block the drain 堵住下水道, drain pipe 排水管, down the drain 白费〔习语〕, drain the sink 排空水槽
[联] sink 水槽, pipe 管道, water 水, shower 淋浴, plumbing 管道, clog 堵塞, sewer 污水管, leak 漏水
[记] 可数。down the drain 喻白费。drain 动词：drain the battery 耗尽电量。brain drain 人才外流

**hut**
英/美 /hʌt/
n. 小屋；棚屋
[义] A small simple building with one room, often made of wood or metal. 简陋的小型单间建筑，常由木或金属制成（wooden hut 木屋，beach hut 海滩小屋，mountain hut 山间小屋）。可数。
[例] ① The fishermen stored their gear in a hut by the shore. 渔民把装备存放在岸边的小棚屋里。 ② We sheltered in a hut until the rain stopped. 我们在一间小屋里躲雨，直到雨停。
[搭] wooden hut 木屋, beach hut 海滩小屋, mountain hut 山间小屋, thatched hut 茅草屋, fishing hut 渔棚
[联] cottage 小屋, cabin 木屋, shed 棚屋, shelter 庇护所, simple 简陋的, remote 偏远的, camp 营地, shack 棚屋
[记] 可数。hut 比 cottage 更简陋、更小。mud hut 泥屋；guard hut 门卫亭

**cabin**
英/美 /ˈkæbɪn/
n. 小木屋；舱室
[义] A small simple house, especially in a rural or wild area; a room on a ship or aircraft. 简陋小屋，尤指乡村或野外；船或飞机上的舱室（log cabin 原木小屋，cabin crew 机组人员，holiday cabin 度假木屋）。可数。
[例] ① They spent the weekend in a log cabin by the lake. 他们在湖边一座原木小屋里过了周末。 ② The cabin crew demonstrated the safety procedures before take-off. 起飞前机组演示了安全程序。
[源] 见 cabinet；*cabin* 本义小房间 → 小木屋 → 机舱。
[族] cabinet 橱柜
[搭] log cabin 原木小屋, holiday cabin 度假木屋, cabin fever 幽闭烦躁, pilot's cabin 驾驶舱, mountain cabin 山间小屋
[联] cottage 小屋, hut 棚屋, lodge 旅舍, wood 木头, forest 森林, crew 机组, ship 船, rustic 乡村的
[记] 可数。cabin 航空/航海义极常见。cabin fever 久居室内焦躁。cabinet 橱柜，同族不同义

**shed**
英/美 /ʃed/
n. 棚屋；简易房
v. 脱落；流出
[义] A small simple building used for storage or as a workshop. 用于储物或作工棚的简陋小建筑（garden shed 花园工具棚，tool shed 工具棚，shed tears 流泪〔动词〕）。可数。
[例] ① He keeps the lawnmower in the garden shed. 他把割草机放在花园工具棚里。 ② They built a wooden shed at the back of the house for extra storage. 他们在屋后建了个木棚增加储物空间。
[搭] garden shed 花园工具棚, tool shed 工具棚, storage shed 储物棚, wooden shed 木棚, bike shed 自行车棚
[联] garage 车库, hut 棚屋, warehouse 仓库, storage 储藏, garden 花园, tool 工具, backyard 后院, workshop 工棚
[记] 可数。shed 动词：shed light on 阐明。shed 与 shred（碎片）不同。shed 亦可指铁路调车场小屋

**skyscraper**
英/美 /ˈskaɪskreɪpə(r)/
n. 摩天大楼
[义] A very tall modern building with many floors. 有许多层楼的极高现代建筑（glass skyscraper 玻璃摩天楼，skyscraper district 摩天楼区）。可数。
[例] ① Glass skyscrapers dominate the downtown skyline. 玻璃摩天楼主导着市中心天际线。 ② The city banned new skyscrapers near the historic quarter. 该市禁止在历史街区附近新建摩天大楼。
[源] 复合词 sky（天空）+ scraper（擦过者）→ 擦过天空之物。
[搭] glass skyscraper 玻璃摩天楼, skyscraper district 摩天楼区, tall skyscraper 高楼, office skyscraper 办公摩天楼, skyline of skyscrapers 摩天楼天际线
[联] building 建筑物, tower 塔楼, high-rise 高层建筑, city 城市, downtown 市中心, elevator 电梯, floor 楼层, architect 建筑师
[记] 可数。skyscraper 20 世纪随钢结构与电梯普及而兴起。high-rise 更泛指高层建筑，不一定极夸张

**rent**
英/美 /rent/
n. 租金
v. 租用；出租
[义] Money paid regularly for the use of a room, house, or other property; to pay to use something for a period. 定期支付以使用房间、房屋或其他财产的钱；付费短期使用（pay rent 付租金，rent an apartment 租公寓，for rent 招租）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Rent in the city has risen sharply over the past five years. 过去五年里市区租金大幅上涨。 ② They decided to rent a flat rather than buy. 他们决定租房而不是买。
[源] 源自古法语 *rente*（收入），拉丁语 *rendita*（归还）→ 定期付还之物 → 租金。
[族] rental 租赁的, renter 租户
[搭] pay rent 付租金, rent an apartment 租公寓, for rent 招租, monthly rent 月租, raise the rent 涨租
[联] landlord 房东, tenant 租户, lease 租约, apartment 公寓, mortgage 按揭, housing 住房, deposit 押金, property 房产
[记] 不可数/可数。rent 动词：rent out 出租。rent-free 免租。rent 与 lend 不同：rent 通常指付费使用

**landlord**
英/美 /ˈlændlɔːd/
n. 房东；店主
[义] A person who owns a building or room and allows others to live or work there for rent; the owner or manager of a pub. 拥有建筑或房间并出租给他人居住或工作的人；酒吧老板（private landlord 私人房东，landlord and tenant 房东与租户）。可数。
[例] ① The landlord agreed to fix the leaking roof within a week. 房东同意一周内修好漏雨的屋顶。 ② They complained to the landlord about the broken heating. 他们就坏掉的暖气向房东投诉。
[搭] private landlord 私人房东, landlord and tenant 房东与租户, contact the landlord 联系房东, landlord approval 房东同意, pub landlord 酒吧老板
[联] tenant 租户, rent 租金, lease 租约, apartment 公寓, property 房产, owner 业主, housing 住房, deposit 押金
[记] 可数。landlord 字面「土地之主」；landlady 女房东。英式 pub landlord 指酒吧老板

**tenant**
英/美 /ˈtenənt/
n. 租户；房客
[义] A person who pays rent to live or work in a building owned by someone else. 付费租用他人名下建筑居住或工作的人（new tenant 新租户，tenant rights 租户权益，evict a tenant 驱逐租户）。可数。
[例] ① The previous tenant left the flat in good condition. 上一任房客把公寓保持得很好。 ② Tenants must give one month's notice before moving out. 租户搬出前须提前一个月通知。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *tenere*（持有）→ 暂时持有使用权者。
[族] tenancy 租期, tenement 廉价公寓〔历史〕, tenure 保有期
[搭] new tenant 新租户, tenant rights 租户权益, evict a tenant 驱逐租户, tenant agreement 租户协议, long-term tenant 长期租户
[联] landlord 房东, rent 租金, lease 租约, apartment 公寓, occupant 占用者, evict 驱逐, housing 住房, deposit 押金
[记] 可数。tenant 亦可指土地租户。tenancy 租约/租期；tenement 旧指廉价多层住宅

**mortgage**
英/美 /ˈmɔːɡɪdʒ/
n. 按揭；抵押贷款
v. 抵押
[义] A loan from a bank to buy a house or flat, paid back over many years; to borrow money using property as security. 银行为购房提供的长期贷款；以房产作担保借款（take out a mortgage 申请按揭，mortgage payment 月供，pay off the mortgage 还清贷款）。可数。
[例] ① They took out a thirty-year mortgage to buy their first home. 他们申请了三十年按揭买下第一套房。 ② Monthly mortgage payments take up half their income. 月供占去了他们一半的收入。
[源] 源自法语 *mort gage*（死亡pledge）→ 贷款还清前抵押品「死押」于贷方 → 按揭。
[搭] take out a mortgage 申请按揭, mortgage payment 月供, pay off the mortgage 还清贷款, mortgage rate 按揭利率, mortgage lender 按揭贷款方
[联] loan 贷款, bank 银行, house 房屋, property 房产, rent 租金, buy 购买, interest 利息, deposit 首付
[记] 可数。mortgage 动词：mortgage one's house 以房屋抵押。remortgage 再按揭。与 rent 相对：买 vs 租

**lease**
英/美 /liːs/
n. 租约；租赁
v. 出租；租用
[义] A legal agreement allowing use of property for a fixed period in return for rent; to grant or take such an agreement. 允许在固定期限内使用财产并支付租金的法律协议；签订此类协议（sign a lease 签租约，lease agreement 租赁协议，break a lease 违约退租）。可数。
[例] ① She signed a two-year lease on the apartment. 她签了为期两年的公寓租约。 ② The company leases office space in the city centre. 公司在市中心租用办公空间。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *laissare*（放开、允许），经法语 *laisser* → 让与使用权。
[族] leaseholder 租期持有人, release 释放〔re- + lease〕
[搭] sign a lease 签租约, lease agreement 租赁协议, break a lease 违约退租, long-term lease 长期租约, lease renewal 续租
[联] rent 租金, landlord 房东, tenant 租户, contract 合同, apartment 公寓, property 房产, term 期限, renew 续签
[记] 可数。lease 比口头 rent 更正式、强调合同。leasehold 租赁权；freehold 永久产权〔英〕

**furnish**
英/美 /ˈfɜːnɪʃ/
v. 布置家具；提供
[义] To put furniture in a room or house; to supply or provide something. 为房间或房屋配备家具；提供（furnish a room 布置房间，fully furnished 家具齐全，furnish details 提供细节）。及物动词。
[形] furnished 带家具的, furnishing 陈设〔常复〕, unfurnished 不带家具的
[例] ① They furnished the flat with second-hand pieces from a charity shop. 他们用慈善商店的二手货布置了公寓。 ② The report furnished clear evidence of the problem. 报告提供了问题的明确证据。
[源] 见 furniture；*furnish* 本义「装备、供应」。
[族] furniture 家具, furnishing 陈设
[搭] furnish a room 布置房间, fully furnished 家具齐全, furnish an apartment 配置公寓, unfurnished flat 空房出租, furnish details 提供细节
[联] furniture 家具, decorate 装饰, apartment 公寓, room 房间, equip 装备, supply 供应, sofa 沙发, move in 搬入
[记] furnish 比 decorate 偏「配家具」；decorate 偏装饰、粉刷。furnishings 常复数指软装陈设

**renovate**
英/美 /ˈrenəveɪt/
v. 翻新；修复
[义] To repair and improve a building so that it is in good condition again. 修缮并改善建筑，使其恢复良好状态（renovate a kitchen 翻新厨房，fully renovated 全面翻新，renovate an old house 翻修老房）。及物动词。
[形] renovated 翻新过的, renovation 翻新〔名词〕
[例] ① They renovated the bathroom and installed a walk-in shower. 他们翻新了浴室，装了步入式淋浴。 ② The couple bought a cheap house and renovated it themselves. 这对夫妇买了套便宜房子，自己动手翻新。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *renovare*（更新），*re-*（再）+ *novare*（使新）→ 使再新。
[族] novel 新的, innovate 创新, renew 更新
[搭] renovate a kitchen 翻新厨房, fully renovated 全面翻新, renovate an old house 翻修老房, renovate on a budget 预算内翻新, under renovation 翻新中
[联] repair 修理, decorate 装饰, remodel 改造, building 建筑物, house 房屋, kitchen 厨房, contractor 承包商, DIY 自己动手
[记] renovate 强调「旧变新、全面改善」；repair 偏局部修复。renovation 名词；renovator 翻新者

**decorate**
英/美 /ˈdekəreɪt/
v. 装饰；粉刷
[义] To make a room or building more attractive by adding colour, paint, or ornaments; to paint the walls of a room. 用颜色、油漆或饰品使房间或建筑更美观；粉刷墙壁（decorate a room 装饰房间，decorate for Christmas 布置圣诞装饰，redecorate 重新装修）。及物动词。
[形] decorated 装饰过的, decoration 装饰, decorative 装饰性的
[例] ① They decorated the nursery with stars and moons on the ceiling. 他们在婴儿房天花板上装饰了星星和月亮。 ② We need to decorate the living room before the party. 派对前我们得把客厅重新粉刷一下。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *decorare*（美化），*decor*（美观）。
[族] decoration 装饰, decorative 装饰性的, decor 装饰风格
[搭] decorate a room 装饰房间, decorate for Christmas 布置圣诞装饰, redecorate 重新装修, decorate the walls 装饰墙面, interior decorating 室内装饰
[联] furnish 布置, paint 油漆, wallpaper 壁纸, renovate 翻新, ornament 饰品, style 风格, festive 节日的, interior 室内的
[记] 英式 decorate a room 常指「粉刷」；美式更常说 paint。decoration 可数/不可数；decorations 指圣诞等挂饰

**residence**
英/美 /ˈrezɪdəns/
n. 住所；居住
[义] A place where someone lives; the fact of living in a place. 某人居住的地方；居住这一事实（private residence 私人住宅，official residence 官邸，take up residence 定居）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The ambassador's official residence is in the capital. 大使的官邸在首都。 ② They took up residence in a small town after retirement. 退休后他们在一座小城定居。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *residere*（停留、居住），*re-* + *sedere*（坐）→ 坐回去 → 居住。
[族] resident 居民, residential 住宅的, reside 居住
[搭] private residence 私人住宅, official residence 官邸, take up residence 定居, place of residence 居住地, permanent residence 永久居留
[联] house 房屋, home 家, dwelling 住所, apartment 公寓, live 居住, address 地址, property 房产, inhabit 居住
[记] 可数/不可数。residence 比 house 正式；legal/formal 语境常用。residential area 住宅区

**property**
英/美 /ˈprɒpəti/
n. 房产；财产；属性
[义] A building or piece of land owned by someone; things owned; a quality of something. 某人拥有的建筑或土地；财产；事物的性质（buy property 买房，rental property 出租房产，intellectual property 知识产权）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Property prices in the area have doubled in a decade. 这一带房价十年翻了一番。 ② The hotel is not responsible for loss of personal property. 酒店对个人财物丢失不承担责任。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *proprietas*（所有权），*proprius*（自己的）。
[族] proper 适当的, proprietor 业主, propriety 得体
[搭] buy property 买房, rental property 出租房产, property market 房产市场, property owner 业主, personal property 个人财产
[联] house 房屋, land 土地, rent 租金, mortgage 按揭, landlord 房东, estate 地产, asset 资产, own 拥有
[记] 不可数/可数。property 指房产时常可数。a property 一处房产。intellectual property 知识产权（IP）

**tower**
英/美 /ˈtaʊə(r)/
n. 塔；塔楼
v. 高耸；远超
[义] A tall narrow building or part of a building; to be much taller than surrounding things. 高而窄的建筑或建筑的一部分；远高于周围事物（clock tower 钟楼，tower block 塔楼，tower over 高耸于/远胜于）。可数。
[例] ① The church tower can be seen from miles away. 数英里外都能看见教堂塔楼。 ② New office towers are rising along the river. 沿河一座座办公塔楼拔地而起。
[搭] clock tower 钟楼, tower block 塔楼, control tower 控制塔, tower over 高耸于, bell tower 钟塔
[联] skyscraper 摩天楼, building 建筑物, high-rise 高层建筑, spire 尖塔, castle 城堡, height 高度, rise 升起, landmark 地标
[记] 可数。tower 动词：tower over sb. 比某人高出很多/远胜于。tower block 英式指高层住宅/办公块

**palace**
英/美 /ˈpæləs/
n. 宫殿；豪华娱乐场所
[义] The official home of a king, queen, or other high-ranking person; a large splendid public building. 国王、女王或其他要员的官方住所；大型豪华公共建筑（Buckingham Palace 白金汉宫，ice palace 冰宫〔溜冰场〕，palace coup 宫廷政变）。可数。
[例] ① Tourists gather outside the palace to watch the changing of the guard. 游客在宫殿外聚集观看换岗仪式。 ② The old cinema was a real palace compared to modern multiplexes. 与现代多厅影院相比，那家老电影院真是豪华宫殿。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *Palatium*（罗马帕拉蒂尼山皇宫）→ 宫殿。
[族] palatial 宫殿般的, palatine 宫殿的
[搭] Buckingham Palace 白金汉宫, royal palace 王宫, palace grounds 宫苑, palace guard 宫廷卫队, ice palace 冰宫
[联] castle 城堡, mansion 豪宅, king 国王, queen 女王, royal 皇家的, throne 王座, court 宫廷, grand 宏伟的
[记] 可数。palace 喻指极豪华的场所（movie palace 老派豪华影院）。the Palace 英媒可指王室或政府

**barn**
英/美 /bɑːn/
n. 谷仓；牲口棚
[义] A large farm building used for storing crops or housing animals. 用于存放作物或圈养牲畜的大型农场建筑（convert a barn 改造谷仓，barn door 谷仓大门，barn owl 仓鸮）。可数。
[例] ① Hay was stacked to the roof of the old barn. 干草堆到了旧谷仓的屋顶。 ② They converted the barn into a holiday home with exposed beams. 他们把谷仓改造成带裸露木梁的度假屋。
[源] 源自古英语 *berern*（ barley house）→ 存 barley 之处 → 谷仓。
[搭] convert a barn 改造谷仓, barn door 谷仓大门, barn conversion 谷仓改造, red barn 红色谷仓, barn owl 仓鸮
[联] farm 农场, shed 棚屋, warehouse 仓库, hay 干草, cattle 牛, rural 乡下的, storage 储藏, loft 阁楼
[记] 可数。barn conversion 英欧常见：谷仓改住宅。barn dance 乡村舞会；can't hit the broad side of a barn 枪法极差

**soap**
英/美 /səʊp/
n. 肥皂；肥皂剧
v. 用肥皂擦洗
[义] A substance used with water for washing and cleaning; a television drama about personal relationships. 与水配合用于洗涤清洁的物质；讲述人际关系的电视连续剧（bar of soap 一块肥皂，soap opera 肥皂剧，liquid soap 洗手液）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① She bought a mild soap for sensitive skin. 她买了款温和肥皂给敏感肌用。 ② He soaped his hands thoroughly before rinsing. 他先把双手打上肥皂彻底搓洗，再冲洗。
[搭] bar of soap 一块肥皂, liquid soap 洗手液, soap dish 肥皂碟, soap opera 肥皂剧, soap and water 肥皂水
[联] wash 洗, shower 淋浴, bath 浴缸, towel 毛巾, sponge 海绵, clean 清洁, shampoo 洗发水, detergent 洗涤剂
[记] 不可数/可数。a bar of soap 一块皂。soap opera 因早期由肥皂厂商赞助得名。soft soap 奉承话

**sponge**
英/美 /spʌndʒ/
n. 海绵；海绵块
v. 用海绵擦；蹭
[义] A soft substance full of small holes that absorbs water; a piece of this or similar material used for washing. 充满小孔、吸水的软质材料；用于擦洗的这种或类似材料（kitchen sponge 厨房海绵，sponge cake 海绵蛋糕，sponge off sb. 蹭吃蹭住〔口语〕）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① She wiped the counter with a damp sponge. 她用湿海绵擦了台面。 ② Use a soft sponge to avoid scratching the non-stick pan. 用软海绵以免刮伤不粘锅。
[搭] kitchen sponge 厨房海绵, sponge cake 海绵蛋糕, sponge bath 擦浴, squeeze a sponge 挤海绵, sponge off sb. 蹭吃蹭住
[联] soap 肥皂, wipe 擦, absorb 吸收, clean 清洁, mop 拖把, cloth 抹布, wash 洗, sink 水槽
[记] 可数/不可数。sponge 动词：sponge off 依赖他人。natural sponge 天然海绵；sponge cake 因质地得名

**mop**
英/美 /mɒp/
n. 拖把
v. 用拖把拖；擦
[义] A tool with a long handle and soft material at the end, used for washing floors; to clean with a mop. 长柄末端带软料、用于拖地的工具；用拖把清洁（mop the floor 拖地，mop and bucket 拖把和水桶）。可数。
[例] ① He mopped the kitchen floor after cooking. 做完饭后他拖了厨房地。 ② A mop stood in the corner of the laundry room. 洗衣房角落里立着一把拖把。
[搭] mop the floor 拖地, mop and bucket 拖把和水桶, wet mop 湿拖把, mop up 拖干净/收拾残局, floor mop 地拖
[联] broom 扫帚, floor 地板, clean 清洁, bucket 水桶, sponge 海绵, wash 洗, laundry 洗衣, wipe 擦
[记] 可数。mop up 喻收拾残局（mop up the opposition 彻底击败对手）。mop 头发：messy mop of hair 乱蓬蓬的头发

**bin**
英 /bɪn/ 美 /bɪn/
n. 垃圾桶；储物箱
[义] A container for storing or putting rubbish in (chiefly British; American often trash can). 存放或扔垃圾的容器（主要英式；美式常说 trash can）（wheelie bin 带轮垃圾桶，recycling bin 回收桶，loose bin 散仓〔粮〕）。可数。
[例] ① Put the empty bottles in the recycling bin. 把空瓶子放进回收桶。 ② The council collects the bins every Tuesday morning. 市政每周二早上收垃圾。
[搭] wheelie bin 带轮垃圾桶, recycling bin 回收桶, rubbish bin 垃圾桶, bin liner 垃圾袋, put out the bins 把垃圾桶摆出去
[联] trash 垃圾, rubbish 垃圾, waste 废物, garbage 垃圾〔美〕, recycle 回收, container 容器, kitchen 厨房, lid 盖子
[记] 可数。英式 bin = 美式 trash can / garbage can。bin 亦指储物箱（coal bin 煤箱）

**trash**
英/美 /træʃ/
n. 垃圾；废话
v. 丢弃；捣毁
[义] Things that are no longer wanted and have been thrown away (American English); nonsense. 不再想要而丢弃的东西（美式）；废话。不可数。动词指丢弃或严重破坏（take out the trash 倒垃圾，talk trash 胡说〔口语〕）。
[例] ① Remember to take out the trash before you leave. 走之前记得把垃圾拿出去。 ② Protesters trashed the storefront during the riot. 骚乱中抗议者捣毁了店面。
[搭] take out the trash 倒垃圾, trash can 垃圾桶, trash bag 垃圾袋, talk trash 胡说/放狠话, trash talk 垃圾话
[联] garbage 垃圾, rubbish 垃圾, bin 垃圾桶, waste 废物, recycle 回收, dump 倾倒, litter 乱扔, junk 破烂
[记] 不可数。美式 trash；英式更常说 rubbish。trash 动词：trash a hotel room 把酒店房间搞得一团糟

**freezer**
英/美 /ˈfriːzə(r)/
n. 冷冻室；冰柜
[义] A large container or compartment that keeps food frozen. 使食物保持冷冻的大型容器或隔间（chest freezer 卧式冰柜，freezer compartment 冷冻室，freezer burn 冻伤〔食物〕）。可数。
[例] ① She stored leftover soup in the freezer for later. 她把剩下的汤放进冷冻室以后吃。 ② The freezer is full of frozen vegetables and ice cream. 冰柜里塞满了冷冻蔬菜和冰淇淋。
[源] 源自 freeze（冻结）+ -er（装置）。
[族] freeze 冻结, frozen 冷冻的, freezing 冰冻的
[搭] chest freezer 卧式冰柜, freezer compartment 冷冻室, deep freezer 深冷冻柜, freezer bag 冷冻袋, stock the freezer 填满冰柜
[联] refrigerator 冰箱, fridge 冰箱, ice 冰, frozen 冷冻的, food 食物, store 储存, thaw 解冻, cold 冷的
[记] 可数。freezer 可独立于 fridge 或作为 fridge 的一部分。freezer burn 食物冻干变色。defrost 除霜

**kettle**
英/美 /ˈketl/
n. 水壶；锅
[义] A container with a lid, spout, and handle, used for boiling water. 带盖、嘴和把手的容器，用于烧水（electric kettle 电水壶，put the kettle on 烧开水〔英〕，a different kettle of fish 另当别论〔习语〕）。可数。
[例] ① She filled the kettle and switched it on for tea. 她装满水壶，通电烧水泡茶。 ② The electric kettle boils water in under two minutes. 电水壶不到两分钟就能烧开。
[搭] electric kettle 电水壶, put the kettle on 烧开水, kettle whistle 水壶鸣叫, boil the kettle 烧水壶, stovetop kettle 炉灶水壶
[联] tea 茶, boil 煮沸, water 水, mug 马克杯, kitchen 厨房, pot 锅, coffee 咖啡, appliance 电器
[记] 可数。put the kettle on 英式表「烧水泡个茶」。a different kettle of fish 完全另一回事

**toaster**
英/美 /ˈtəʊstə(r)/
n. 烤面包机
[义] An electrical device for making slices of bread brown and crisp by heating them. 通过加热使面包片变褐变脆的电器（pop-up toaster 跳式烤面包机，toaster oven 小烤箱）。可数。
[例] ① The toaster ejected two golden slices with a click. 烤面包机咔嗒一声弹出两片金黄的吐司。 ② Bread crumbs collect in the tray at the bottom of the toaster. 面包屑会积在烤面包机底部的托盘里。
[搭] pop-up toaster 跳式烤面包机, toaster oven 小烤箱, slice of toast 一片吐司, toaster settings 烤面包机档位, clean the toaster 清理烤面包机
[联] toast 吐司, bread 面包, kitchen 厨房, appliance 电器, breakfast 早餐, oven 烤箱, heat 加热, slice 片
[记] 可数。toast 名词/动词：烤面包片。toaster 与 toaster oven（小烤箱）不同

**knob**
英/美 /nɒb/
n. 球形把手；旋钮
[义] A rounded handle or switch that you turn or pull; a small round control on a machine. 可转动或拉动的圆形把手或开关；机器上的小圆控制钮（door knob 门球形把手，volume knob 音量旋钮）。可数。
[例] ① She turned the knob and pushed the door open. 她转动球形把手，推开了门。 ② Adjust the heat using the knob on the front of the stove. 用炉灶正面的旋钮调节火力。
[搭] door knob 门球形把手, volume knob 音量旋钮, brass knob 黄铜把手, turn the knob 转动旋钮, knob of butter 一块黄油
[联] handle 把手, door 门, switch 开关, dial 刻度盘, grip 抓握, turn 转动, drawer 抽屉, lock 锁
[记] 可数。knob 与 handle 相比更圆、常可旋转。knob 英式口语可指「人」（a bit of a knob）含贬义

**bolt**
英/美 /bəʊlt/
n. 门闩；螺栓
v. 闩上；猛冲
[义] A metal bar that slides into place to lock a door; a metal pin; to lock with a bolt or run suddenly. 滑入到位以锁门的金属杆；螺栓；用闩锁上或突然奔跑（deadbolt 死锁，bolt the door 闩门，lightning bolt 闪电）。可数。
[例] ① She bolted the door before going to bed. 睡前她闩上了门。 ② He slid the bolt across and felt safer. 他插上闩，觉得安全多了。
[搭] deadbolt 死锁, bolt the door 闩门, lightning bolt 闪电, bolt of fabric 一匹布, nuts and bolts 螺母螺栓/基本细节
[联] lock 锁, key 钥匙, door 门, screw 螺丝, hinge 铰链, secure 加固, fasten 系紧, nut 螺母
[记] 可数。bolt 动词：bolt upright 笔直；bolt one's food 狼吞虎咽。the nuts and bolts 基本细节

**hinge**
英/美 /hɪndʒ/
n. 铰链；关键
v. 依赖；装铰链
[义] A piece of metal joined to a door or lid so it can swing open and shut; the central point on which something depends. 连接门或盖使其可开合的金属件；某事所依赖的中心点（door hinge 门铰链，hinge on 取决于，squeaky hinge 吱呀作响的铰链）。可数。
[例] ① The door hinge squeaked every time it opened. 每次开门门铰链都吱呀作响。 ② The whole plan hinges on getting approval by Friday. 整个计划取决于周五前能否获批。
[搭] door hinge 门铰链, hinge on 取决于, squeaky hinge 吱呀作响的铰链, hinge joint 铰链关节, oil the hinges 给铰链上油
[联] door 门, gate 大门, bolt 门闩, swing 摆动, attach 连接, pivot 枢轴, depend 依赖, cabinet 橱柜
[记] 可数。hinge on/upon 喻取决于。hinge 动词：hinged on 依赖于

**radiator**
英/美 /ˈreɪdieɪtə(r)/
n. 暖气片；散热器
[义] A device for heating a room, usually a metal container connected to central heating; a device for cooling an engine. 通常连接集中供暖、用于加热房间的金属装置；冷却发动机的装置（central heating radiator 暖气暖气片，bleed the radiator 给暖气片放气〔英〕，car radiator 汽车水箱）。可数。
[例] ① The radiator under the window keeps the bedroom warm in winter. 窗下的暖气片让卧室冬天很暖和。 ② She hung wet socks on the radiator to dry. 她把湿袜子挂在暖气片上烘干。
[源] 源自 radiate（辐射热）+ -or。
[族] radiate 辐射, radiation 辐射, radiant 发光的
[搭] central heating radiator 暖气片, bleed the radiator 给暖气片放气, car radiator 汽车水箱, radiator valve 暖气阀, warm the radiator 把暖气片弄热
[联] heat 加热, warm 温暖的, thermostat 恒温器, boiler 锅炉, winter 冬天, towel 毛巾, engine 发动机, central heating 集中供暖
[记] 可数。英式 radiator 常指房间暖气；美式 heat 多指暖气系统，radiator 亦指汽车水箱。bleed a radiator 放气除杂音

**thermostat**
英/美 /ˈθɜːməstæt/
n. 恒温器；温控器
[义] A device that automatically controls temperature by switching heating or cooling on and off. 通过自动开关制冷或供暖来控制温度的装置（adjust the thermostat 调节温控器，smart thermostat 智能恒温器，thermostat setting 温度设定）。可数。
[例] ① She turned the thermostat down to save energy at night. 她为夜间节能把恒温器调低了。 ② The smart thermostat learns your schedule and adjusts the heat. 智能恒温器会学习你的作息并自动调节暖气。
[源] 复合词 thermo-（热）+ stat（固定、保持）→ 保持固定温度。
[族] thermal 热的, thermometer 温度计, statistics 统计〔stat 同根不同义〕
[搭] adjust the thermostat 调节温控器, smart thermostat 智能恒温器, thermostat setting 温度设定, room thermostat 室温控制器, turn up the thermostat 调高温度
[联] radiator 暖气片, heat 加热, temperature 温度, air conditioning 空调, control 控制, energy 能源, warm 温暖的, cool 冷却
[记] 可数。thermostat 现代住宅与 HVAC 系统核心词。thermo- 亦见于 thermometer、thermal

**skylight**
英/美 /ˈskaɪlaɪt/
n. 天窗
[义] A window built into a roof or ceiling to let in daylight. 开在屋顶或天花板上以引入日光的窗户（install a skylight 安装天窗，skylight shaft 天窗井）。可数。
[例] ① A skylight in the kitchen flooded the room with morning sun. 厨房的天窗让晨光洒满整个房间。 ② Rain leaked through the old skylight during the storm. 暴风雨中雨水从旧天窗渗了进来。
[搭] install a skylight 安装天窗, roof skylight 屋顶天窗, skylight shaft 天窗井, open skylight 可开启天窗, natural light 自然光
[联] window 窗户, roof 屋顶, ceiling 天花板, light 光线, attic 阁楼, glass 玻璃, bright 明亮的, ventilation 通风
[记] 可数。复合词 sky + light。skylight 与 roof window 常互换。安装不当易漏雨

**shutter**
英/美 /ˈʃʌtə(r)/
n. 百叶窗板；快门
v. 关闭；停业
[义] One of a pair of wooden or metal covers that can be closed over a window; the part of a camera that opens to allow light in. 可关闭以遮挡窗户的一对木或金属板之一；相机中允许光线进入的部件（window shutters 窗百叶板，close the shutters 关上百叶窗，camera shutter 相机快门）。可数，常用复数 shutters。
[例] ① They closed the shutters against the storm. 他们为防暴风雨关上了百叶窗板。 ② Blue wooden shutters framed the cottage windows. 蓝色木百叶窗板装点了小屋的窗户。
[搭] window shutters 窗百叶板, close the shutters 关上百叶窗, camera shutter 相机快门, shutter speed 快门速度, plantation shutters 宽条百叶窗
[联] window 窗户, blind 百叶窗, curtain 窗帘, close 关闭, camera 相机, wood 木, storm 暴风雨, privacy 隐私
[记] 可数，常用复数。shutter 动词：shutter a business 永久停业。shutter 与 shutter speed（摄影）同词

**yard**
英/美 /jɑːd/
n. 院子；码
[义] An area of land outside a house, often enclosed; a unit of length equal to three feet. 房屋外常围合的一片土地；长度单位码（front yard 前院，back yard 后院，play in the yard 在院子里玩）。可数。
[例] ① The kids were playing in the yard after school. 放学后孩子们在院子里玩。 ② They planted vegetables in the back yard. 他们在后院种了蔬菜。
[搭] front yard 前院, back yard 后院, yard sale 庭院旧货出售, play in the yard 在院子里玩, fenced yard 有围栏的院子
[联] garden 花园, lawn 草坪, backyard 后院, fence 栅栏, outdoor 户外的, house 房屋, patio 庭院, driveway 车道
[记] 可数。yard 美式指房屋周围土地；英式更常说 garden（即使只铺草）。yard 亦作长度单位「码」

**backyard**
英/美 /ˌbækˈjɑːd/
n. 后院
[义] An area of land behind a house, often used for gardening or recreation. 房屋后面的土地，常作园艺或休闲用（backyard barbecue 后院烧烤，in one's own backyard 在自己门口/自家地盘〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① They set up a trampoline in the backyard for the children. 他们在后院给孩子们装了蹦床。 ② Environmental issues in your own backyard deserve attention too. 自家门口的环境问题同样值得关注。
[搭] backyard barbecue 后院烧烤, backyard garden 后院花园, in the backyard 在后院, backyard pool 后院泳池, small backyard 小后院
[联] yard 院子, garden 花园, patio 庭院, lawn 草坪, fence 栅栏, barbecue 烧烤, shed 棚屋, outdoor 户外的
[记] 可数。复合词 back + yard。not in my backyard（NIMBY）指「别在我家后院」的邻避心态

**lawn**
英/美 /lɔːn/
n. 草坪
[义] An area of short, regularly cut grass in a garden or park. 花园或公园中定期修剪的短草地（mow the lawn 割草坪，lawn mower 割草机，front lawn 前院草坪）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① He mows the lawn every Saturday morning. 他每周六上午割草坪。 ② Children were running across the lawn in the park. 孩子们在公园的草坪上奔跑。
[搭] mow the lawn 割草坪, lawn mower 割草机, front lawn 前院草坪, lawn chair 草坪椅, keep off the lawn 勿踏草坪
[联] grass 草, garden 花园, yard 院子, mow 割, green 绿色的, backyard 后院, sprinkler 洒水器, hedge 树篱
[记] 可数/不可数。mow the lawn 固定搭配。lawn 亦可指细薄毛料（lawn fabric），与草坪不同义

**garden**
英/美 /ˈɡɑːdn/
n. 花园；菜园
v. 从事园艺
[义] A piece of land next to a house where flowers, vegetables, or grass are grown. 房屋旁种植花卉、蔬菜或草坪的土地（back garden 后花园, vegetable garden 菜园, garden furniture 花园家具）。可数。
[例] ① They spent the weekend planting roses in the garden. 他们周末在花园种玫瑰。 ② Fresh herbs from the garden taste better than shop-bought ones. 花园现采的香草比买的更香。
[搭] back garden 后花园, vegetable garden 菜园, garden furniture 花园家具, front garden 前花园, work in the garden 在花园干活
[联] lawn 草坪, yard 院子, backyard 后院, flower 花, plant 种植, hedge 树篱, patio 庭院, shed 棚屋, outdoor 户外的, neighbour 邻居
[记] 可数：a garden。英式 garden 常指整片私家花园；美式更常说 yard。garden 动词指「做园艺」

**neighbour**
英 /ˈneɪbə(r)/ 美 /ˈneɪbər/
n. 邻居
v. 邻近；与……接壤
[义] A person who lives near you; a country or area next to another. 住在附近的人；邻近的国家或地区（next-door neighbour 隔壁邻居，friendly neighbours 友好的邻居，neighbouring countries 邻国）。可数。
[例] ① Our neighbours watered the plants while we were on holiday. 我们度假时邻居帮忙浇了花。 ② She borrowed a cup of sugar from the neighbour next door. 她从隔壁邻居那里借了一 cup 糖。
[源] 源自古英语 *nēahgebur*，*nēah*（近）+ *gebur*（居住者）→ 近处居住者。
[族] neighbourhood 街区, neighboring 邻近的, neighbourly 友善的
[搭] next-door neighbour 隔壁邻居, friendly neighbours 友好的邻居, neighbour dispute 邻里纠纷, neighbour country 邻国, get to know the neighbours 认识邻居
[联] house 房屋, apartment 公寓, fence 栅栏, community 社区, nearby 附近的, friendly 友好的, noise 噪音, borrow 借
[记] 可数。美式拼写 neighbor。neighbourhood 指街区/社区；neighbourly 形容词：友善的

**repair**
英/美 /rɪˈpeə(r)/
v. 修理；修复
n. 修理；修补
[义] To fix something that is broken or damaged; the act of fixing or the condition after fixing. 修复损坏之物；修理行为或修后状态（repair the roof 修屋顶，under repair 维修中，in good repair 保养良好）。及物动词；可数/不可数。
[形] repaired 修好的, repairable 可修复的
[例] ① The landlord sent someone to repair the broken window. 房东派人来修碎了的窗户。 ② The house is old but in good repair. 房子虽旧，但保养良好。
[搭] repair the roof 修屋顶, under repair 维修中, in good repair 保养良好, beyond repair 无法修复, carry out repairs 进行维修
[联] fix 修理, renovate 翻新, maintain 维护, broken 坏的, damage 损坏, plumber 水管工, DIY 自己动手, cost 费用
[记] 可数/不可数。beyond repair 无法修复。repair 比 fix 略正式；renovate 偏全面翻新

**install**
英/美 /ɪnˈstɔːl/
v. 安装；设置
[义] To put equipment or furniture in place and make it ready to use; to put someone in an official position. 将设备或家具就位并使其可用；使某人就职（install a shower 安装淋浴，install central heating 装集中供暖，newly installed 新安装的）。及物动词。
[形] installed 已安装的, installation 安装
[例] ① They hired a plumber to install the new bathroom. 他们请了水管工安装新浴室。 ② The software installs automatically when you plug in the device. 插入设备后软件会自动安装。
[源] 源自中世纪拉丁语 *installare*，*in-* + *stallum*（位置）→ 放入位置。
[族] installation 安装, installment 分期付款
[搭] install a shower 安装淋浴, install central heating 装集中供暖, install software 安装软件, newly installed 新安装的, easy to install 易于安装
[联] fit 安装, set up 设置, appliance 电器, plumber 水管工, electrician 电工, equipment 设备, connect 连接, mount 挂载
[记] install 比 put in 更强调「装妥可用」。installation 可数指安装物；installment 分期付款

**maintain**
英/美 /meɪnˈteɪn/
v. 维护；保持
[义] To keep a building, machine, or system in good condition by checking and repairing it; to continue at the same level. 通过检查与修理使建筑、机器或系统保持良好状态；维持（maintain the garden 维护花园，well maintained 维护良好，maintain the heating system 维护供暖系统）。及物动词。
[形] maintained 维护良好的, maintenance 维护
[例] ① The building is expensive to maintain because of its age. 这栋建筑因年代久远，维护费用很高。 ② She maintains the house herself, painting and fixing things as needed. 她亲自维护房子，按需粉刷和修补。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *manu tenere*（用手持有）→ 持续保有 → 维护。
[族] maintenance 维护, maintainer 维护者
[搭] maintain the garden 维护花园, well maintained 维护良好, maintain equipment 维护设备, maintain a property 维护房产, routine maintenance 日常维护
[联] repair 修理, keep 保持, upkeep 保养, clean 清洁, service 检修, preserve 保存, condition 状况, cost 费用
[记] maintain 强调持续保养；repair 偏事后修复。maintenance 不可数为主

**construct**
英/美 /kənˈstrʌkt/
v. 建造；构建
n. 构想；构念
[义] To build something by putting parts together; an idea or concept formed in the mind. 将部件组合以建造；头脑中形成的观念（construct a house 盖房，under construction 施工中，social construct 社会建构〔名词〕）。及物动词；可数（名词）。
[形] constructed 建造的, construction 施工, constructive 建设性的
[例] ① They plan to construct a new block of flats on the empty plot. 他们计划在空地上建一排新公寓。 ② The road is closed while workers construct the bridge. 工人建桥期间道路封闭。
[源] 见 structure；*con-*（一起）+ *struere*（建造）→ 一起搭建。
[族] structure 结构, construction 施工, reconstruct 重建, destroy 破坏
[搭] construct a house 盖房, under construction 施工中, construct a building 建造大楼, construction site 工地, construct from 用……建造
[联] build 建造, demolish 拆除, architect 建筑师, engineer 工程师, foundation 地基, brick 砖, design 设计, project 工程
[记] construct 比 build 略正式、偏工程语境。under construction 施工中标志常见

**demolish**
英/美 /dɪˈmɒlɪʃ/
v. 拆除；推翻
[义] To completely destroy a building, especially on purpose; to show that an idea is wrong. 完全摧毁建筑，尤指有意为之；推翻观点（demolish a building 拆除大楼，demolish an argument 驳倒论点）。及物动词。
[形] demolished 被拆除的, demolition 拆除
[例] ① The old factory was demolished to make way for apartments. 旧工厂被拆除，腾出地方建公寓。 ② Critics demolished his claims in the press. 评论界在媒体上驳倒了他的说法。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *demoliri*，*de-*（向下）+ *moliri*（建造）→ 拆毁建造之物。
[族] demolition 拆除
[搭] demolish a building 拆除大楼, demolish an argument 驳倒论点, scheduled for demolition 计划拆除, completely demolished 完全拆毁, demolition crew 拆除队
[联] destroy 摧毁, build 建造, construct 建造, wreck 毁坏, bulldoze 推平, ruin 废墟, clear 清场, rebuild 重建
[记] demolish 专指有意拆除或彻底摧毁。demolition derby 撞车大赛，不同语境

**utility**
英/美 /juːˈtɪləti/
n. 公用事业；效用
[义] A service such as gas, water, or electricity supplied to a building; the quality of being useful. 向建筑供应的燃气、水、电等服务；有用性（utility bill 水电煤气账单，utility room 杂物间/洗衣房，public utility 公用事业）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Rent includes all utilities except electricity. 租金含除电以外的所有公用费用。 ② The utility room has space for a washing machine and dryer. 杂物间有地方放洗衣机和烘干机。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *utilitas*（有用），*uti*（使用）。
[族] utilize 利用, useful 有用的, utilization 利用
[搭] utility bill 水电账单, utility room 杂物间, public utility 公用事业, utility company 公用事业公司, pay utilities 付公用事业费
[联] electricity 电, gas 煤气, water 水, bill 账单, rent 租金, meter 表, supply 供应, household 家庭
[记] 可数/不可数。utilities 常复数指各项公用费用。utility room 英式常指洗衣/储物间

**deposit**
英/美 /dɪˈpɒzɪt/
n. 押金；定金；存款
v. 存放；支付押金
[义] Money paid as part of the full price or as security when renting; to put money in a bank or leave a substance on a surface. 租房时预付的部分款项或保证金；存入银行。可数/不可数。
[例] ① They paid a two-month deposit before moving into the flat. 搬进公寓前他们付了两月押金。 ② You'll get your deposit back if the apartment is undamaged. 公寓若无损坏，押金会退还。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *deponere*（放下），*de-* + *ponere*（放）→ 放下钱 → 押金。
[族] deposition 沉积, depository 存放处, position 位置
[搭] pay a deposit 付押金, security deposit 保证金, deposit refund 退还押金, return the deposit 退回押金, rental deposit 租赁押金
[联] rent 租金, landlord 房东, tenant 租户, lease 租约, refund 退款, payment 付款, bank 银行, advance 预付款
[记] 可数/不可数。security deposit 租房保证金。deposit 动词：deposit money 存款

**estate agent**
英/美 /ɪˈsteɪt ˈeɪdʒənt/
n. 房产经纪人〔尤英式〕
[义] A person whose job is to sell and rent houses and land for others. 替他人出售和出租房屋、土地的人（local estate agent 本地房产中介，estate agency 房产中介公司，through an estate agent 经中介）。可数。
[例] ① They found the flat through a local estate agent. 他们通过本地房产中介找到了这套公寓。 ② The estate agent showed us three houses in one afternoon. 房产经纪人一个下午带看了三套房。
[搭] local estate agent 本地中介, estate agency 房产中介公司, through an estate agent 经中介, contact an estate agent 联系中介, estate agent fees 中介费
[联] realtor 房产经纪人〔美〕, property 房产, house 房屋, rent 租金, buy 购买, mortgage 按揭, viewing 看房, commission 佣金
[记] 可数。英式 estate agent = 美式 realtor / real estate agent。estate 此指房产，非「庄园」

**realtor**
英/美 /ˈriːltə(r)/
n. 房产经纪人〔尤美式〕
[义] A person whose job is to sell and rent real estate (American English; trademark often capitalized as Realtor). 买卖和出租房地产的人（美式；商标常大写作 Realtor）。可数。
[例] ① Their realtor helped them find a house within their budget. 房产经纪人帮他们找到了预算内的房子。 ② She works as a licensed realtor in California. 她在加州做持证房产经纪人。
[搭] licensed realtor 持证经纪人, hire a realtor 请经纪人, realtor commission 经纪人佣金, local realtor 本地经纪人, realtor listing 房源 listing
[联] estate agent 房产经纪人〔英〕, property 房产, house 房屋, buy 购买, sell 出售, mortgage 按揭, listing 房源, commission 佣金
[记] 可数。Realtor 为美国 Realtors 协会注册商标，日常泛指房产经纪。real estate 房地产

**staircase**
英/美 /ˈsteəkeɪs/
n. 楼梯；楼梯间
[义] A set of stairs and its surrounding structure inside a building. 建筑内的一段阶梯及其周围结构（spiral staircase 螺旋楼梯，marble staircase 大理石楼梯，grand staircase 大楼梯）。可数。
[例] ① A grand staircase leads up to the first floor. 一道宏伟的楼梯通向一楼。 ② She paused halfway down the staircase to listen. 她在楼梯半途停下细听。
[搭] spiral staircase 螺旋楼梯, marble staircase 大理石楼梯, grand staircase 大楼梯, staircase landing 楼梯平台, wooden staircase 木楼梯
[联] stair 楼梯, landing 平台, hallway 走廊, floor 楼层, rail 扶手, climb 爬, elevator 电梯, banister 栏杆
[记] 可数。staircase 指整段楼梯结构；stair 可指一级台阶或 stairs 统称。banister 栏杆

**landing**
英/美 /ˈlændɪŋ/
n. 楼梯平台；着陆
[义] A flat area at the top or bottom of a staircase, or between flights of stairs. 楼梯顶部、底部或两段之间的平台（first-floor landing 一楼平台，landing strip 跑道〔航空〕）。可数。
[例] ① There is a small table on the landing between the two floors. 两层之间的楼梯平台上有一张小桌。 ② She waited on the landing until the noise downstairs stopped. 她在楼梯平台上等到楼下的声响停止。
[搭] first-floor landing 一楼平台, stair landing 楼梯平台, landing area 着陆区, narrow landing 窄平台, wait on the landing 在平台等候
[联] staircase 楼梯, stair 楼梯, hallway 走廊, floor 楼层, step 台阶, upstairs 楼上, rail 扶手, platform 平台
[记] 可数。landing 航空义「着陆」与楼梯义同词。half-landing 小平台

**corridor**
英/美 /ˈkɒrɪdɔː(r)/
n. 走廊；通道
[义] A long narrow passage in a building with doors leading off it. 建筑内两侧有门的长窄通道（hotel corridor 酒店走廊，corridor of power 权力走廊〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① Her room is at the end of the corridor on the left. 她的房间在走廊尽头左侧。 ② Footsteps echoed along the empty corridor at night. 夜里空荡的走廊里回荡着脚步声。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *currere*（跑）→ 可奔跑的通道。
[族] current 流动, occur 发生, course 路线
[搭] hotel corridor 酒店走廊, long corridor 长走廊, corridor wall 走廊墙壁, end of the corridor 走廊尽头, school corridor 学校走廊
[联] hallway 门厅/走廊, passage 通道, door 门, floor 楼层, echo 回声, office 办公室, apartment 公寓, narrow 窄的
[记] 可数。corridor 偏办公/酒店等较长通道；hallway 偏住宅门厅/短廊。corridors of power 权力核心

**entrance**
英/美 /ˈentrəns/
n. 入口；进入
v. 使入迷
[义] A door or opening where you enter a building; the act of entering. 进入建筑的门口或开口；进入行为（main entrance 主入口，back entrance 后门，entrance hall 门厅）。可数/不可数。动词 /ɪnˈtrɑːns/ 使入迷。
[例] ① Wait for me at the main entrance of the shopping centre. 在购物中心主入口等我。 ② The back entrance is used for deliveries. 后门用于送货。
[源] 源自法语 *entrer*（进入），拉丁语 *intrare*。
[族] enter 进入, entry 入口/条目, internal 内部的
[搭] main entrance 主入口, back entrance 后门, entrance hall 门厅, entrance fee 入场费, make an entrance 登场
[联] door 门, exit 出口, gate 大门, hallway 门厅, foyer 门厅, welcome 欢迎, sign 标识, access 进入
[记] 可数/不可数。entrance 名词 /ˈentrəns/；动词 entrance 重音在后。make a dramatic entrance 隆重登场

**gutter**
英/美 /ˈɡʌtə(r)/
n. 排水沟；檐槽
v. （蜡烛）淌蜡；边缘化
[义] A channel along the edge of a roof or at the side of a road that carries away rain water. 沿屋顶边缘或路边排走雨水的沟槽（clean the gutters 清理檐槽，in the gutter 在阴沟/潦倒〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① Leaves blocked the gutters and caused water to overflow. 树叶堵住了檐槽，导致水溢出来。 ② They had the gutters cleaned before the rainy season. 雨季前他们请人清理了檐槽。

[联] roof 屋顶, drain 排水, pipe 管道, rain 雨, leak 漏水, downspout 落水管, road 道路, flood 积水

n. 泄漏；漏洞
v. 漏；泄露
[义] A hole or crack that lets liquid or gas escape; to allow or reveal something secretly. 使液体或气体逸出的孔或缝；秘密泄露（water leak 漏水，roof leak 屋顶漏水，leak information 泄露信息）。可数/不可数。

v. 排放；发泄
