n. 灰泥；石膏
v. 抹灰；贴满
[义] A smooth paste that hardens when dry, used for covering walls and ceilings; material for setting broken bones. 干后变硬的平滑糊状物，用于覆盖墙面和天花板；固定断骨的材料（plaster wall 抹灰墙，plaster cast 石膏绷带，plaster over 掩盖〔喻〕）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① They removed the old plaster to expose the brick underneath. 他们铲掉旧灰泥，露出下面的砖。 ② Posters were plastered all over his bedroom walls. 海报贴满了他卧室的墙。
[搭] plaster wall 抹灰墙, plaster cast 石膏绷带, plaster ceiling 抹灰天花板, wet plaster 湿灰泥, plaster over cracks 用灰泥填缝
[联] wall 墙, ceiling 天花板, paint 油漆, brick 砖, smooth 平滑的, repair 修理, bandage 绷带, cover 覆盖
[记] 不可数/可数。plaster 英式亦可指创可贴（plaster on a cut）。plastered 俚语：大醉的

**lumber**
英 /ˈlʌmbə(r)/ 美 /ˈlʌmbər/
n. 木材；木料〔尤美式〕
v. 笨重地移动
[义] Wood that has been prepared for building (American English; British timber). 已加工用于建筑的木材（美式；英式 timber）。不可数。
[例] ① They bought lumber to build shelves in the garage. 他们买了木料在车库做架子。 ② Prices for lumber rose sharply during the housing boom. 建房热潮期间木材价格大幅上涨。
[搭] lumber yard 木材场, lumber store 木材店, cut lumber 锯木料, stack lumber 堆木料, lumber industry 木材业
[联] wood 木头, timber 木材〔英〕, plywood 胶合板, beam 梁, build 建造, nail 钉子, saw 锯, construction 施工
[记] 不可数。美式 lumber = 英式 timber（建材义）。lumber 动词：lumber along 笨重前行

**plywood**
英/美 /ˈplaɪwʊd/
n. 胶合板；夹板
[义] A board made of thin layers of wood glued together. 薄木层胶合而成的板材（sheet of plywood 一张胶合板，plywood floor 胶合板地板，plywood furniture 夹板家具）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① He cut a sheet of plywood to fit the window opening. 他裁了一张胶合板盖住窗洞。 ② Temporary shelves were built from plywood during the renovation. 翻新期间用胶合板做了临时架子。
[源] 复合词 ply（层）+ wood（木）→ 多层木板。
[搭] sheet of plywood 一张胶合板, plywood floor 胶合板地板, plywood board 胶合板, cut plywood 切割胶合板, plywood sheet 胶合板张
[联] lumber 木材, wood 木头, board 板材, shelf 搁板, nail 钉子, layer 层, furniture 家具, DIY 自己动手
[记] 不可数/可数。a sheet of plywood 一张。ply 亦指「一股绳」

**flooring**
英/美 /ˈflɔːrɪŋ/
n. 地板材料；铺地板
[义] Material used to make or cover a floor. 用于制作或覆盖地面的材料（wooden flooring 木地板，install new flooring 铺新地板，flooring options 地板选材）。不可数。
[例] ① They chose wooden flooring for the living room. 他们给客厅选了木地板。 ② The flooring in the kitchen is easy to wipe clean. 厨房地面材质便于擦洗。
[源] 源自 floor（地板）+ -ing。
[族] floor 地板, floorboard 地板条
[搭] wooden flooring 木地板, install new flooring 铺新地板, laminate flooring 复合地板, flooring material 地板材料, replace the flooring 更换地板
[联] floor 地板, carpet 地毯, tile 瓷砖, wood 木头, hardwood 硬木, laminate 复合的, install 安装, room 房间
[记] 不可数。flooring 指材料或铺装整体；floor 指表面或楼层

**hardwood**
英/美 /ˈhɑːdwʊd/
n. 硬木；阔叶材
[义] Wood from deciduous trees, used for floors and furniture; the floor made from it. 落叶树木材，用于地板和家具；由此制成的地板（hardwood floor 实木地板，solid hardwood 实木）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The apartment has original hardwood floors from the 1920s. 这套公寓保留着二十世纪二十年代的原始硬木地板。 ② Hardwood is more durable than softwood for flooring. 铺地板时硬木比软木更耐用。
[搭] hardwood floor 实木地板, solid hardwood 实木, hardwood furniture 硬木家具, polish hardwood 给硬木上光, hardwood decking 硬木露台板
[联] flooring 地板材料, wood 木头, lumber 木材, oak 橡木, furniture 家具, durable 耐用的, floor 地板, grain 木纹
[记] 不可数/可数。hardwood floor 常指实木/硬木地板。与 softwood（软木/针叶材）相对

**doorbell**
英/美 /ˈdɔːbel/
n. 门铃
[义] A bell or chime at the entrance to a house that visitors press to announce their arrival. 房屋入口处供访客按响以告知到来的铃或电子门铃（ring the doorbell 按门铃，doorbell camera 门铃摄像头，wireless doorbell 无线门铃）。可数。
[例] ① Someone rang the doorbell while she was in the shower. 她淋浴时有人按门铃。 ② They installed a doorbell camera for extra security. 他们装了门铃摄像头以增加安全。
[搭] ring the doorbell 按门铃, doorbell camera 门铃摄像头, wireless doorbell 无线门铃, hear the doorbell 听见门铃, answer the doorbell 应门
[联] door 门, entrance 入口, ring 响, visitor 访客, knock 敲, alarm 警报, security 安全, welcome 欢迎
[记] 可数。复合词 door + bell。doorbell 与 knock 并用：先 knock 再按 bell

**mailbox**
英/美 /ˈmeɪlbɒks/
n. 信箱；邮箱
[义] A box outside a house or at a post office where mail is delivered and collected (American English; British letterbox / postbox). 房屋外或邮局供收投邮件的箱子（美式；英式 letterbox / postbox）。可数。
[例] ① She checked the mailbox on her way in from work. 下班回来路上她查看了信箱。 ② The post was stuffed into the mailbox overnight. 邮件夜里塞满了信箱。
[搭] check the mailbox 查看信箱, mailbox key 信箱钥匙, empty the mailbox 清空信箱, wall-mounted mailbox 壁挂信箱, mailbox flag 信箱信号旗
[联] mail 邮件, letter 信, post 邮政, package 包裹, delivery 投递, front door 前门, key 钥匙, neighbour 邻居
[记] 可数。美式 mailbox；英式 front door letterbox 或 street postbox。email mailbox 电子邮箱，喻义延伸

**peephole**
英/美 /ˈpiːphəʊl/
n. 猫眼；窥视孔
[义] A small hole in a door with a lens, allowing someone inside to see who is outside. 门上带透镜的小孔，供室内的人看清门外是谁（look through the peephole 透过猫眼看，install a peephole 装猫眼）。可数。
[例] ① She looked through the peephole before opening the door. 开门前她透过猫眼看了看。 ② The hotel room door had a security chain and a peephole. 酒店房门配有防盗链和猫眼。
[搭] look through the peephole 透过猫眼看, install a peephole 装猫眼, door peephole 门猫眼, peephole cover 猫眼盖, security peephole 安全猫眼
[联] door 门, lock 锁, security 安全, visitor 访客, spyhole 窥视孔〔英〕, chain 防盗链, entrance 入口, see 看
[记] 可数。复合词 peek + hole。英式亦说 spyhole。peep 动词：偷看

**smoke alarm**
英/美 /ˈsməʊk əˌlɑːm/
n. 烟雾报警器
[义] A device that makes a loud sound when it detects smoke, warning of fire. 检测到烟雾时发出巨响、警示火灾的装置（install smoke alarms 安装烟雾报警器，smoke alarm battery 报警器电池，test the smoke alarm 测试报警器）。可数。
[例] ① The smoke alarm went off when toast burned in the kitchen. 厨房烤面包烤焦时烟雾报警器响了。 ② Test your smoke alarms once a month. 每月测试一次烟雾报警器。
[搭] install smoke alarms 安装烟雾报警器, smoke alarm battery 报警器电池, test the smoke alarm 测试报警器, false alarm 误报, ceiling smoke alarm 天花板烟雾报警
[联] fire 火, alarm 警报, safety 安全, detector 探测器, battery 电池, kitchen 厨房, emergency 紧急, burn 燃烧
[记] 可数。亦说 smoke detector，义近。fire alarm 可指整栋楼的火警系统，范围更大

**washing machine**
英/美 /ˈwɒʃɪŋ məˌʃiːn/
n. 洗衣机
[义] A machine for washing clothes, sheets, and other fabric items. 洗涤衣物、床单等织物的机器（load the washing machine 装洗衣机，front-loading washing machine 滚筒洗衣机，washing machine cycle 洗涤程序）。可数。
[例] ① She put a load of towels in the washing machine. 她把一批毛巾放进洗衣机。 ② The washing machine started leaking from the bottom. 洗衣机底部开始漏水。
[搭] load the washing machine 装洗衣机, front-loading washing machine 滚筒洗衣机, run the washing machine 开洗衣机, washing machine cycle 洗涤程序, broken washing machine 坏了的洗衣机
[联] laundry 洗衣, dryer 烘干机, soap 肥皂, detergent 洗涤剂, clothes 衣服, spin 脱水, appliance 电器, kitchen 厨房
[记] 可数。复合词 washing + machine。washer 口语可指洗衣机；亦指垫圈

**dryer**
英/美 /ˈdraɪə(r)/
n. 烘干机；干燥机
[义] A machine that dries clothes after washing, using heat and tumbling. 洗涤后用加热和翻滚使衣物干燥的机器（tumble dryer 滚筒烘干机，washer-dryer 洗烘一体机，hair dryer 吹风机）。可数。
[例] ① He moved the clothes from the washer to the dryer. 他把衣服从洗衣机移到烘干机。 ② The dryer stopped heating, so the laundry stayed damp. 烘干机不加热了，衣服还是潮的。
[搭] tumble dryer 滚筒烘干机, washer-dryer 洗烘一体机, run the dryer 开烘干机, dryer sheet 烘干纸, clothes dryer 干衣机
[联] washing machine 洗衣机, laundry 洗衣, dry 烘干, heat 热, clothes 衣服, appliance 电器, towel 毛巾, vent 通风口
[记] 可数。美式 dryer；英式亦说 tumble dryer。dryer sheet 防静电器皿；hair dryer 吹风机

**iron**
英/美 /ˈaɪən/
n. 熨斗；铁
v. 熨烫
[义] A device that heats up and is used to remove creases from clothes; to press clothes with such a device. 加热后用于烫平衣物的装置；用熨斗压平衣物（steam iron 蒸汽熨斗，iron a shirt 熨衬衫，ironing board 熨衣板）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① She plugged in the iron and waited for it to heat up. 她插上熨斗，等它加热。 ② He hates ironing and sends his shirts to the laundry. 他讨厌熨衣服，衬衫送洗衣店烫。
[搭] steam iron 蒸汽熨斗, iron a shirt 熨衬衫, ironing board 熨衣板, clothes iron 熨衣熨斗, iron settings 熨斗档位
[联] laundry 洗衣, clothes 衣服, shirt 衬衫, crease 褶皱, press 压平, steam 蒸汽, wrinkle 皱纹, fold 折叠
[记] 可数/不可数。iron 金属义「铁」不可数。ironing 名词：熨烫。cast iron 铸铁

**hood**
英/美 /hʊd/
n. 抽油烟机；引擎盖；兜帽
[义] A device above a cooker that removes smoke and smells; the cover over a car engine. 厨灶上方抽走油烟和异味的装置；汽车引擎盖（cooker hood / range hood 抽油烟机，extractor hood 排气罩）。可数。
[例] ① Turn on the hood before you start frying. 开始煎之前先开抽油烟机。 ② Grease built up inside the kitchen hood over time. 厨房抽油烟机里渐渐积了油垢。
[搭] cooker hood 抽油烟机, range hood 抽油烟机, extractor hood 排气罩, turn on the hood 开抽油烟机, clean the hood 清理烟机
[联] kitchen 厨房, stove 炉灶, vent 通风口, smoke 烟, cook 烹饪, fan 风扇, grease 油垢, filter 滤网
[记] 可数。hood 汽车义「引擎盖」；clothing 义「兜帽」。under the hood 喻「内部机制」

**cot**
英 /kɒt/ 美 /kɑːt/
n. 婴儿床；行军床
[义] A small bed for a baby (British English; American crib); a narrow portable bed. 婴儿小床（英式；美式 crib）；窄便携床（行军床）。可数。
[例] ① The baby slept in a cot next to the parents' bed. 婴儿睡在父母床边的小床里。 ② Campers slept on cots inside the tent. 露营者在帐篷里的行军床上睡觉。
[搭] baby cot 婴儿床, cot bed 幼儿床, fold-up cot 折叠床, cot mattress 婴儿床垫, travel cot 便携婴儿床
[联] crib 婴儿床〔美〕, nursery 婴儿房, baby 婴儿, bed 床, mattress 床垫, sleep 睡觉, portable 便携的, camp 露营
[记] 可数。英式 cot = 美式 crib（婴儿床）。cot 亦可指折叠行军床，语境不同

**bungalow**
英/美 /ˈbʌŋɡələʊ/
n. 平房；单层小屋
[义] A house with only one main floor, sometimes with rooms in the roof space. 仅有一层主楼、有时利用屋顶空间的房屋（detached bungalow 独栋平房，retirement bungalow 退休 bungalow）。可数。
[例] ① They bought a bungalow because stairs were becoming difficult. 他们买了平房，因为上下楼梯越来越吃力。 ② The seaside bungalow had a wide veranda around it. 海滨平房四周环绕着宽游廊。
[源] 源自印地语 *banglā*（孟加拉式房屋）→ 单层屋。
[搭] detached bungalow 独栋平房, seaside bungalow 海滨平房, retirement bungalow 退休平房, single-storey bungalow 单层平房, bungalow community 平房社区
[联] house 房屋, cottage 小屋, flat 单层, storey 楼层, porch 门廊, elderly 年长的, suburban 郊区的, accessible 无障碍的
[记] 可数。bungalow 强调单层；cottage 不强调层数。美式 ranch house 亦指单层宽屋

**duplex**
英/美 /ˈdjuːpleks/
n. 双拼住宅；复式
adj. 双层的
[义] A house divided into two separate living units, or an apartment on two floors. 分成两套独立居住单元的房屋，或占两层的公寓（live in a duplex 住双拼，duplex apartment 复式公寓）。可数。
[例] ① They rent the upper unit of a duplex near the park. 他们租了公园附近一套双拼的楼上单元。 ② The duplex has its own entrance and a small backyard. 这套双拼有独立入口和小后院。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *duplex*（双层），*duo*（二）+ *plicare*（折叠）。
[族] duplicate 复制, dual 双的
[搭] live in a duplex 住双拼, duplex apartment 复式公寓, duplex house 双拼住宅, upper duplex 楼上单元, duplex unit 双拼单元
[联] apartment 公寓, house 房屋, tenant 租户, rent 租金, flat 公寓, storey 楼层, unit 单元, backyard 后院
[记] 可数。美式 duplex 常见；英式 less common，或说 semi-detached 上下分租。duplex 打印义「双面打印」

**townhouse**
英/美 /ˈtaʊnhaʊs/
n. 联排别墅；城市住宅
[义] A house that is one of a row of similar houses sharing side walls; a tall narrow city home. 一排相似、侧墙相连的房屋之一；城市中的窄高住宅（row of townhouses 联排别墅群，modern townhouse 现代联排）。可数。
[例] ① They bought a townhouse with a small patio out back. 他们买了一套后面带小庭院的联排别墅。 ② Townhouses line both sides of the restored street. 修复后的街道两侧都是联排住宅。
[搭] row of townhouses 联排别墅群, modern townhouse 现代联排, townhouse community 联排社区, three-storey townhouse 三层联排, townhouse development 联排开发项目
[联] house 房屋, row house 联排屋, apartment 公寓, condo 产权公寓, terrace 露台, urban 城市的, neighbour 邻居, patio 庭院
[记] 可数。美式 townhouse；英式 terraced house 更常说。town house 亦指市内第二居所

**high-rise**
英/美 /ˈhaɪ raɪz/
n. 高层建筑
adj. 高层的
[义] A very tall building with many floors; describing such a building. 有许多层楼的极高建筑；高层的（live in a high-rise 住高层，high-rise apartment 高层公寓，high-rise block 高层楼块）。可数。
[例] ① She lives on the twentieth floor of a high-rise downtown. 她住在市中心一栋高层的二十楼。 ② High-rise blocks dominate the skyline east of the river. 河东天际线由高层群楼主导。
[搭] live in a high-rise 住高层, high-rise apartment 高层公寓, high-rise block 高层楼块, high-rise building 高层建筑, high-rise estate 高层住宅区
[联] skyscraper 摩天楼, apartment 公寓, building 建筑物, elevator 电梯, floor 楼层, urban 城市的, tower 塔楼, storey 楼层
[记] 可数。high-rise 作形容词：high-rise flats。与 skyscraper 相比 high-rise 更泛指高层，不一定极高

**storey**
英 /ˈstɔːri/ 美 /ˈstɔːri/
n. 楼层
[义] A level of a building; one floor (British spelling; American story). 建筑的一层（英式拼写；美式 story）（two-storey house 两层房屋，upper storey 上层，single-storey 单层）。可数。
[例] ① They live in a three-storey Victorian house. 他们住在一栋三层维多利亚式房屋里。 ② The upper storey has three bedrooms and a bathroom. 上层有三间卧室和一间浴室。
[搭] two-storey house 两层房屋, upper storey 上层, single-storey 单层, ground storey 底层, top storey 顶层
[联] floor 楼层, building 建筑物, stair 楼梯, level 层, attic 阁楼, basement 地下室, bungalow 平房, elevator 电梯
[记] 可数。英式 storey，美式 story（楼层义）。story 故事义拼写相同，靠语境区分

**interior**
英/美 /ɪnˈtɪəriə(r)/
n. 内部；室内
adj. 内部的；室内的
[义] The inside part of something, especially a building; relating to the inside. 某物内部，尤指建筑内部；与内部有关的（interior design 室内设计，interior wall 内墙，car interior 汽车内饰）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The interior of the house was bright and modern. 房屋内部明亮现代。 ② She works in interior design and chooses furniture for clients. 她做室内设计，帮客户选家具。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *interior*（更内的），*inter*（在内）。
[族] internal 内部的, exterior 外部的
[搭] interior design 室内设计, interior wall 内墙, interior decorator 室内设计师, interior space 内部空间, car interior 汽车内饰
[联] exterior 外部, inside 里面, room 房间, decorate 装饰, furniture 家具, wall 墙, design 设计, domestic 家庭的
[记] 可数/不可数。interior 形容词极常见。Department of the Interior 美国内政部，专名

**exterior**
英/美 /ɪkˈstɪəriə(r)/
n. 外部；外观
adj. 外部的；室外的
[义] The outside part of something, especially a building; relating to the outside. 某物外部，尤指建筑外部；与外部有关的（exterior paint 外墙漆，exterior wall 外墙，building exterior 建筑外观）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The exterior of the house needs repainting. 房屋外墙需要重新粉刷。 ② From the exterior, the church looks centuries old. 从外观看，这座教堂有数百年历史。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *exterior*（更外的），*ex-*（向外）。
[族] external 外部的, interior 内部的
[搭] exterior paint 外墙漆, exterior wall 外墙, building exterior 建筑外观, exterior lighting 室外照明, exterior door 外门
[联] interior 内部, outside 外面, facade 立面, paint 油漆, wall 墙, garden 花园, appearance 外观, weather 天气
[记] 可数/不可数。exterior 与 interior 相对。exterior 亦可指人的外表（cold exterior 冷外表）

**remodel**
英/美 /ˌriːˈmɒdl/
v. 改造；重新设计
n. 改造
[义] To change the structure or appearance of a room or building. 改变房间或建筑的结构或外观（remodel the kitchen 改造厨房，remodelled bathroom 改造后的浴室，home remodel 家居改造）。及物动词；可数（名词）。
[形] remodelled 改造过的, remodeling 改造
[例] ① They remodelled the kitchen to add an island and more storage. 他们改造厨房，加了中岛和更多储物空间。 ② The remodel took three months and cost more than planned. 改造花了三个月，费用超出预算。
[搭] remodel the kitchen 改造厨房, home remodel 家居改造, remodel a bathroom 改造浴室, complete remodel 全面改造, remodel project 改造项目
[联] renovate 翻新, decorate 装饰, construct 建造, kitchen 厨房, design 设计, contractor 承包商, plan 规划, budget 预算
[记] 美式 remodel 常见；英式更常说 refurbish / renovate。remodeling 名词；remodelled 英式拼写

**grout**
英/美 /ɡraʊt/
n. 填缝剂；薄浆
v. 填缝
[义] A thick paste used to fill gaps between tiles; to fill these gaps. 用于填补瓷砖之间缝隙的厚浆；填缝（tile grout 瓷砖填缝剂，grout lines 砖缝，regrout the bathroom 浴室重新填缝）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The white grout between the tiles had turned grey over time. 瓷砖间的白色填缝剂久而久之变灰了。 ② He spent the weekend grouting the new kitchen backsplash. 他周末给新厨房防溅墙填了缝。
[搭] tile grout 瓷砖填缝剂, grout lines 砖缝, regrout the bathroom 浴室重新填缝, apply grout 填缝, grout sealer 填缝密封剂
[联] tile 瓷砖, bathroom 浴室, kitchen 厨房, seal 密封, gap 缝隙, mortar 灰浆, clean 清洁, mould 霉菌
[记] 不可数/可数。grout 动词：grout the tiles 给瓷砖填缝。grout 亦可指注入岩缝的浆体

**settle**
英/美 /ˈsetl/
v. 定居；安顿；解决
[义] To move into a new home and arrange your life there; to resolve or become calm. 搬入新家并在那里安顿生活；解决或平静下来（settle into a new house 安顿新家，settle down 定居下来，settle an argument 解决争执）。不及物/及物。
[例] ① It took them a month to settle into the new apartment. 他们花了一个月才在新公寓安顿下来。 ② Once the children settled, the house finally felt like home. 孩子们安定下来后，这房子终于有了家的感觉。
[搭] settle into a new house 安顿新家, settle down 定居, settle in 安顿下来, settle a bill 结账, settle an argument 解决争执
[联] move 搬家, home 家, apartment 公寓, unpack 拆包, nest 筑巢, comfortable 舒适的, community 社区, adjust 适应
[记] settle in / into 指适应新环境。settle down 可指结婚定居。settlement 定居点；settler 定居者

**unpack**
英/美 /ʌnˈpæk/
v. 拆包；打开行李
[义] To remove things from boxes or bags after moving or travelling. 搬家或旅行后从箱子或袋中取出物品（unpack boxes 拆箱，still unpacking 还在收拾，unpack groceries 取出杂货）。及物/不及物。
[例] ① They spent the evening unpacking boxes in the living room. 他们晚上在客厅拆箱收拾。 ② I haven't unpacked my suitcase yet from the trip. 旅行回来的箱子我还没打开收拾。
[搭] unpack boxes 拆箱, still unpacking 还在收拾, unpack groceries 取出杂货, unpack luggage 打开行李, unpack the kitchen 整理厨房用品
[联] pack 打包, move 搬家, box 箱子, settle 安顿, furniture 家具, kitchen 厨房, suitcase 行李箱, arrange 摆放
[记] unpack 与 pack 相对。still unpacking 搬家后常说。unpack 计算机义「解包」

**alarm**
英/美 /əˈlɑːm/
n. 警报；闹钟；惊慌
v. 使惊恐
[义] A device that warns of danger or wakes you at a set time; a feeling of fear. 警示危险或在设定时间叫醒人的装置；惊慌（ burglar alarm 防盗报警，alarm clock 闹钟，set the alarm 设闹钟/布防）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① She set the burglar alarm before leaving the house. 出门前她设好了防盗报警。 ② The alarm clock rang at six every morning. 闹钟每天早上六点响。
[搭] burglar alarm 防盗报警, alarm clock 闹钟, set the alarm 设闹钟/布防, fire alarm 火警, alarm system 报警系统
[联] smoke alarm 烟雾报警器, security 安全, wake 叫醒, danger 危险, ring 响, lock 锁, door 门, emergency 紧急
[记] 可数/不可数。alarm 动词：alarm sb. 使担忧。alarm 与 alert 相比 alarm 更强调警报告知

**mat**
英/美 /mæt/
n. 垫子；席
v. 缠结；铺席
[义] A small piece of strong material used to cover part of a floor or protect a surface. 用于覆盖部分地面或保护表面的小块坚韧材料（door mat 门垫，bath mat 浴室防滑垫，place mat 餐垫）。可数。
[例] ① A rubber mat kept the bath from getting slippery. 橡胶垫让浴室地面不那么滑。 ② Wipe your shoes on the mat before coming inside. 进来前在垫子上擦擦鞋。
[搭] door mat 门垫, bath mat 浴室垫, place mat 餐垫, floor mat 地垫, welcome mat 欢迎门垫
[联] doormat 门垫, carpet 地毯, rug 小地毯, floor 地板, wipe 擦, bathroom 浴室, rubber 橡胶, kitchen 厨房
[记] 可数。mat 比 rug 小、功能性强。go to the mat for 为……全力拼搏〔美俚〕

**open-plan**
英/美 /ˌəʊpən ˈplæn/
adj. 开放式的
[义] Describing a room or flat where several areas, such as kitchen and living room, are not separated by walls. 描述厨房与客厅等若干区域无墙分隔的房间或公寓（open-plan kitchen 开放式厨房，open-plan living 开放式起居空间，open-plan office 开放式办公）。形容词。
[例] ① The flat has an open-plan kitchen and dining area. 这套公寓的厨房与用餐区是开放式的。 ② Open-plan layouts make small spaces feel larger. 开放式布局让小空间显得更宽敞。
[搭] open-plan kitchen 开放式厨房, open-plan living 开放式起居, open-plan office 开放式办公, open-plan layout 开放式布局, open-plan flat 开放式公寓
[联] kitchen 厨房, living room 客厅, partition 隔断, space 空间, layout 布局, modern 现代的, spacious 宽敞的, wall 墙
[记] 形容词。open-plan 作定语连字符。与 compartmentalized（分隔的）相对

**partition**
英/美 /pɑːˈtɪʃn/
n. 隔断；分隔
v. 隔开；分割
[义] A wall or screen that divides one room from another without reaching the ceiling, or separates areas. 不从地面到天花板、用以分隔房间或区域的墙或屏风（room partition 房间隔断，glass partition 玻璃隔断，partition wall 隔断墙）。可数。
[例] ① A glass partition separates the study from the hallway. 玻璃隔断把书房和走廊分开。 ② They added a partition to create a home office in the corner. 他们加了一道隔断，在角落隔出家庭办公区。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *partitio*（分割），*partire*（分开）。
[族] part 部分, partial 部分的, depart 离开
[搭] room partition 房间隔断, glass partition 玻璃隔断, partition wall 隔断墙, removable partition 可移动隔断, partition off 隔开
[联] wall 墙, open-plan 开放式的, divide 分隔, screen 屏风, room 房间, space 空间, separate 分开, office 办公室
[记] 可数。partition 动词：partition a room 用隔断分隔。历史义「瓜分领土」在新闻中仍见

**screw**
英/美 /skruː/
n. 螺丝；螺旋
v. 拧紧；搞砸
[义] A thin pointed piece of metal with a raised spiral line, turned to hold things together. 带螺旋纹、拧入以固定物体的细长金属件（screw in 拧入，screwdriver 螺丝刀，screw loose 螺丝松了）。可数。
[例] ① One screw was missing from the bookshelf bracket. 书架支架少了一颗螺丝。 ② She screwed the shelf firmly to the wall. 她把搁板牢牢拧固定在墙上。
[搭] screw in 拧入, screw loose 螺丝松了, screw tight 拧紧, screw head 螺丝头, wood screw 木螺丝
[联] nail 钉子, bolt 螺栓, screwdriver 螺丝刀, drill 钻, fix 固定, assemble 组装, bracket 支架, tighten 拧紧
[记] 可数。screw 动词俚语义多（screw up 搞砸）。screw 与 nail：螺丝 vs 钉

**nail**
英/美 /neɪl/
n. 钉子；指甲
v. 钉牢； nailed it 完美做到
[义] A thin piece of metal with a flat head, hammered into wood to hold things together. 带平头、敲入木材以固定物体的细长金属件（hammer a nail 钉钉子，nail polish 指甲油，hit the nail on the head 一针见血〔习语〕）。可数。
[例] ① He hammered nails into the wall to hang the picture frame. 他在墙上钉钉子挂画框。 ② Use nails instead of screws for the temporary shelf. 临时架子用钉子不用螺丝。
[搭] hammer a nail 钉钉子, nail polish 指甲油, hit the nail on the head 一针见血, nail down 确定, nail file 指甲锉
[联] screw 螺丝, hammer 锤子, wood 木头, hang 悬挂, fix 固定, wall 墙, assemble 组装, toolbox 工具箱
[记] 可数。nail 动词：nail it 完美做到。finger nail 指甲，同形异义

**hook**
英/美 /hʊk/
n. 钩子；挂钩
v. 钩住；吸引
[义] A curved piece of metal or plastic for hanging things on. 用于悬挂物品的弯形金属或塑料件（coat hook 衣帽钩，hook on the door 门后挂钩，off the hook 脱身〔俚〕）。可数。
[例] ① She hung her keys on the hook by the front door. 她把钥匙挂在前门边的挂钩上。 ② Picture hooks were spaced evenly along the wall. 墙上等距钉了若干画钩。
[搭] coat hook 衣帽钩, hook on the door 门后挂钩, picture hook 画钩, hook onto 钩在……上, off the hook 脱身
[联] hanger 衣架, rack 架子, wall 墙, hang 悬挂, door 门, coat 外套, key 钥匙, mount 挂载
[记] 可数。by hook or by crook 千方百计。hook up 连接/约会〔口语〕

**latch**
英/美 /lætʃ/
n. 门闩；碰锁
v. 闩上；理解
[义] A device that keeps a door or gate closed, often opened by lifting a lever. 使门或大门保持关闭的装置，常通过抬起杠杆打开（door latch 门闩， latch onto 理解/缠上，on the latch 门虚掩〔英〕）。可数。
[例] ① The gate latch was broken and wouldn't stay shut. 大门门闩坏了，关不紧。 ② Leave the door on the latch if you go out briefly. 短出去时把门虚掩上就好。
[搭] door latch 门闩, gate latch 大门闩, on the latch 门虚掩, latch onto 理解/缠上, spring latch 弹簧闩
[联] lock 锁, bolt 门闩, door 门, gate 大门, hinge 铰链, close 关闭, handle 把手, secure 固定
[记] 可数。latch 比 lock 轻；on the latch 英式指门未锁只闩。latch onto an idea 理解想法

**build**
英/美 /bɪld/
v. 建造；建立
n. 体格
[义] To make something by putting parts or materials together; to develop gradually. 将部件或材料组合成某物；逐渐发展（build a house 盖房，build on 增建，build up 积累）。不规则动词。
[形] built 建造的, building 建筑物, builder 建筑工人
[例] ① They plan to build an extension at the back of the house. 他们计划在屋后增建。 ② Trust is built slowly over years of living together. 信任是在多年同住中慢慢建立的。
[搭] build a house 盖房, build on 增建, build up 积累, build from scratch 从零建造, custom-built 定制的
[联] construct 建造, demolish 拆除, architect 建筑师, brick 砖, wood 木头, foundation 地基, repair 修理, design 设计
[记] build / built / built / building。build 比 construct 日常；construct 偏工程正式

**fix**
英/美 /fɪks/
v. 修理；固定
n. 困境；解决办法
[义] To repair something; to attach firmly in place. 修理某物；牢固固定（fix the tap 修水龙头，fix a shelf to the wall 把搁板固定在墙上，fix dinner 做晚饭〔美〕）。及物动词；可数（名词，口语）。
[例] ① Can you fix the dripping tap in the bathroom? 你能修一下浴室滴水的水龙头吗？ ② They fixed the mirror to the wall with strong adhesive. 他们用强力胶把镜子固定在墙上。
[搭] fix the tap 修水龙头, fix a leak 修漏, fix a shelf 固定搁板, fix up 修缮, quick fix 权宜之计
[联] repair 修理, mend 修补, attach 固定, broken 坏的, tool 工具, problem 问题, install 安装, DIY 自己动手
[记] fix 比 repair 口语。fix sb. up with 撮合/安排。fix 名词：in a fix 陷入困境

**assemble**
英/美 /əˈsembl/
v. 组装；集合
[义] To fit together all the parts of something, especially furniture sold in flat packs. 将某物各部件装配在一起，尤指平板包装出售的家具（assemble furniture 组装家具，assemble a crib 组装婴儿床，some assembly required 需自行组装）。及物/不及物。
[形] assembled 组装好的, assembly 组装
[例] ① It took him two hours to assemble the flat-pack wardrobe. 他花两小时组装了平板包装衣柜。 ② The bed arrives in a box and you assemble it yourself. 床以箱装送达，需自行组装。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *ad-* + *simul*（一起）→ 聚在一起。
[族] assembly 集合/组装, resemble 相似
[搭] assemble furniture 组装家具, assemble a crib 组装婴儿床, some assembly required 需自行组装, easy to assemble 易于组装, self-assembly 自行组装
[联] build 建造, screw 螺丝, nail 钉子, furniture 家具, IKEA 宜家, instruction 说明书, tool 工具, flat-pack 平板包装
[记] assemble 偏「按件装配」；build 偏施工建造。assembly line 流水线

**food processor**
英/美 /ˈfuːd prəˌsesə(r)/
n. 食物料理机
[义] An electric machine with blades that chops, slices, and mixes food. 带刀片、可切碎、切片和混合食物的电器（use a food processor 用料理机，food processor bowl 料理机杯，pulse in the food processor 点动处理）。可数。
[例] ① She used a food processor to chop the onions in seconds. 她用料理机几秒就切好了洋葱。 ② The food processor comes with slicing and grating attachments. 料理机配有切片和刨丝配件。
[搭] use a food processor 用料理机, food processor bowl 料理机杯, pulse in the food processor 点动, attach the blade 装刀片, compact food processor 小型料理机
[联] blender 搅拌机, kitchen 厨房, chop 切碎, slice 切片, appliance 电器, cook 烹饪, mix 混合, prepare 准备
[记] 可数。food processor 比 blender 功能更全（切、搅、揉）；blender 偏打液体

**dustpan**
英/美 /ˈdʌspæn/
n. 簸箕
[义] A flat container with a handle, used with a brush to sweep up dust and dirt. 带柄的扁平容器，与扫帚配合清扫灰尘污物（dustpan and brush 簸箕和刷子，empty the dustpan 倒簸箕）。可数。
[例] ① She swept the crumbs into the dustpan and emptied it. 她把碎屑扫进簸箕倒掉。 ② A dustpan and brush hung behind the kitchen door. 厨房门后挂着簸箕和刷子。
[搭] dustpan and brush 簸箕和刷子, empty the dustpan 倒簸箕, plastic dustpan 塑料簸箕, sweep into the dustpan 扫进簸箕
[联] broom 扫帚, sweep 扫, mop 拖把, clean 清洁, floor 地板, brush 刷子, dirt 灰尘, kitchen 厨房
[记] 可数。复合词 dust + pan。dustpan 与 vacuum 相比用于小范围干扫

**humidifier**
英/美 /hjuːˈmɪdɪfaɪə(r)/
n. 加湿器
[义] A device that adds moisture to the air in a room. 为室内增加湿度的装置（use a humidifier 用加湿器，humidifier tank 加湿器水箱，cool-mist humidifier 冷雾加湿器）。可数。
[例] ① They run a humidifier in the bedroom during dry winters. 干燥冬天他们在卧室开加湿器。 ② Clean the humidifier regularly to prevent mould. 定期清洁加湿器以防霉菌。
[源] 源自 humid（潮湿）+ -ify + -er。
[族] humid 潮湿的, humidity 湿度, humidify 加湿
[搭] use a humidifier 用加湿器, humidifier tank 水箱, cool-mist humidifier 冷雾加湿器, clean the humidifier 清洁加湿器, bedroom humidifier 卧室加湿器
[联] air 空气, dry 干燥的, moisture 湿气, dehumidifier 除湿器, winter 冬天, health 健康, bedroom 卧室, mould 霉菌
[记] 可数。与 dehumidifier 相对。humidity 湿度；humid 形容词

**duct**
英/美 /dʌkt/
n. 管道； ducts
[义] A tube or channel that carries air, liquid, or cables through a building. 建筑中输送空气、液体或电缆的管或通道（air duct 风管，duct tape 强力胶带，heating duct 暖气管道）。可数。
[例] ① Warm air flows through ducts in the floor. 暖风在地板下的管道中流动。 ② They sealed the duct where cold air was leaking in. 他们封住了漏进冷空气的管道口。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *ducere*（引导）→ 引导之物。
[族] conduct 传导, aqueduct 渡槽, educate 教育〔ducere 同族〕
[搭] air duct 风管, heating duct 暖气管道, duct tape 强力胶带, ventilation duct 通风管, seal a duct 封管道
[联] vent 通风口, pipe 管道, air 空气, heat 热, HVAC 暖通, airflow 气流, insulation 隔热, ceiling 天花板
[记] 可数。duct tape 强力胶带家居极常用。duct 亦可指身体导管（医学）

**lighting**
英/美 /ˈlaɪtɪŋ/
n. 照明；灯光
[义] The arrangement or type of light in a room or building; equipment that provides light. 房间或建筑中的灯光布置或类型；提供照明的设备（natural lighting 自然采光，ambient lighting 环境光，lighting design 灯光设计）。不可数。
[例] ① Good lighting makes the kitchen safer and more pleasant to work in. 良好的照明让厨房更安全、更好用。 ② They updated the lighting with dimmable LED bulbs. 他们换了可调光 LED 灯泡升级照明。
[搭] natural lighting 自然采光, ambient lighting 环境光, lighting design 灯光设计, overhead lighting 顶灯, soft lighting 柔光
[联] lamp 灯, light 光, bulb 灯泡, bright 明亮的, dim 暗的, switch 开关, ceiling 天花板, design 设计
[记] 不可数。lighting 指系统或效果；lamp/light 指具体灯具。stage lighting 舞台灯光

**chandelier**
英/美 /ˌʃændəˈlɪə(r)/
n. 吊灯；枝形吊灯
[义] A decorative light fixture hanging from the ceiling with branches for several bulbs or candles. 从天花板垂下、多枝供装灯泡或蜡烛的装饰灯具（crystal chandelier 水晶吊灯，dining-room chandelier 餐厅吊灯）。可数。
[例] ① A crystal chandelier hung above the dining table. 餐桌上方悬着一盏水晶吊灯。 ② They lowered the chandelier slightly to fit the room scale. 他们把吊灯略降以配合房间尺度。
[源] 源自法语 *chandelle*（蜡烛），*chandel*（蜡烛）→ 多烛台 → 枝形吊灯。
[族] candle 蜡烛
[搭] crystal chandelier 水晶吊灯, dining-room chandelier 餐厅吊灯, install a chandelier 安装吊灯, chandelier chain 吊灯链, ornate chandelier 华丽吊灯
[联] lamp 灯, lighting 照明, ceiling 天花板, dining room 餐厅, crystal 水晶, elegant 优雅的, bulb 灯泡, fixture 灯具
[记] 可数。chandelier 偏装饰性、正式空间；pendant light 现代单头吊灯

**banister**
英/美 /ˈbænɪstə(r)/
n. 楼梯栏杆
[义] The row of posts and a rail along the side of stairs. 楼梯一侧的一排立柱与扶手（hold the banister 扶栏杆，banister rail 扶手，polished banister 擦亮的栏杆）。可数。
[例] ① The child slid down the banister despite being told not to. 尽管被告知不许，孩子还是从栏杆上滑了下来。 ② She ran her hand along the smooth wooden banister. 她的手抚过光滑的木栏杆。
[搭] hold the banister 扶栏杆, banister rail 扶手, polished banister 擦亮的栏杆, stair banister 楼梯栏杆, grip the banister 抓住栏杆
[联] stair 楼梯, staircase 楼梯间, railing 栏杆, landing 平台, climb 爬, wood 木头, safety 安全, handrail 扶手
[记] 可数。banister 与 handrail 常互换；balustrade 更正式。slide down the banister 儿童经典冒险

**foyer**
英/美 /ˈfɔɪeɪ/
n. 门厅；前厅
[义] An entrance hall or open area in a house, hotel, or theatre. 房屋、酒店或剧院的入口门厅或敞开区域（hotel foyer 酒店大堂，foyer area 门厅区，through the foyer 穿过前厅）。可数。
[例] ① Guests waited in the foyer before the concert began. 音乐会开始前宾客在前厅等候。 ② The apartment opens onto a small foyer with coat hooks. 公寓开门即入小门厅，有衣帽钩。
[源] 源自法语 *foyer*（壁炉、 hearth），引申为聚会前等候处。
[搭] hotel foyer 酒店大堂, foyer area 门厅区, through the foyer 穿过前厅, spacious foyer 宽敞门厅, foyer lighting 门厅照明
[联] entrance 入口, hallway 走廊, lobby 大堂, corridor 走廊, reception 接待, door 门, welcome 欢迎, theatre 剧院
[记] 可数。foyer 比 hallway 略正式，酒店/剧院常用。美式 entry / entrance hall 亦常见

**veranda**
英/美 /vəˈrændə/
n. 游廊；外廊
[义] A roofed platform along the outside of a house, often wrapping around it. 沿房屋外墙、常环绕的有顶平台（sit on the veranda 坐在游廊上，wrap-around veranda 环绕式游廊）。可数。
[例] ① They had breakfast on the veranda overlooking the garden. 他们在俯瞰花园的游廊上吃早餐。 ② Rocking chairs lined the shaded veranda. 荫凉的游廊上摆了一排摇椅。
[搭] sit on the veranda 坐在游廊上, wrap-around veranda 环绕游廊, shaded veranda 荫凉游廊, veranda post 游廊柱, colonial veranda 殖民风格游廊
[联] porch 门廊, terrace 露台, balcony 阳台, garden 花园, outdoor 户外的, shade 阴凉, chair 椅子, tropical 热带的
[记] 可数。veranda 比 porch 更大、更敞，热带/殖民风格常见。亦拼 verandah

**cellar**
英/美 /ˈselə(r)/
n. 地窖；酒窖
[义] An underground room used for storage, especially wine. 用于储物的地下房间，尤指储酒（wine cellar 酒窖，root cellar 根菜地窖，cellar door 地窖门）。可数。
[例] ① They keep their wine collection in a cool cellar below the house. 他们把藏酒放在屋下凉爽的地窖里。 ② The old house has a stone cellar for storing vegetables. 老宅有石砌地窖存蔬菜。
[搭] wine cellar 酒窖, root cellar 根菜地窖, cellar door 地窖门, go down to the cellar 下地窖, damp cellar 潮湿地窖
[联] basement 地下室, attic 阁楼, storage 储藏, wine 葡萄酒, underground 地下的, cool 凉爽的, bottle 瓶子, shelf 搁板
[记] 可数。cellar 比 basement 更指地下储藏；basement 可住人/作洗衣房。cellar door 语言学上「最美音节」之一〔趣谈〕

**sprinkler**
英/美 /ˈsprɪŋklə(r)/
n. 洒水器；喷淋头
[义] A device that sprays water over a lawn or inside a building to extinguish fire. 向草坪喷水或建筑内喷水灭火的装置（lawn sprinkler 草坪洒水器，fire sprinkler 消防喷淋，sprinkler system 喷淋系统）。可数。
[例] ① The lawn sprinklers came on automatically at dawn. 草坪洒水器黎明时自动启动。 ② Fire sprinklers activated and contained the blaze. 消防喷淋启动，控制住了火势。
[搭] lawn sprinkler 草坪洒水器, fire sprinkler 消防喷淋, sprinkler system 喷淋系统, turn on the sprinklers 开洒水器, automatic sprinkler 自动喷淋
[联] lawn 草坪, garden 花园, water 水, hose 软管, fire 火, irrigation 灌溉, grass 草, safety 安全
[记] 可数。sprinkler 动词 sprinkle：洒。fire sprinkler 现代建筑安全标配

**smoke detector**
英/美 /ˈsməʊk dɪˌtektə(r)/
n. 烟雾探测器
[义] A device on a ceiling that sounds an alarm when it senses smoke. 安装于天花板、感应到烟雾时发出警报的装置（install a smoke detector 安装烟雾探测器, smoke detector battery 探测器电池, test the smoke detector 测试探测器）。可数。
[例] ① Test the smoke detector every month and replace the battery yearly. 每月测试烟雾探测器，每年换电池。 ② Burnt toast set off the smoke detector in the kitchen. 烤焦的吐司触发了厨房烟雾探测器。
[搭] install a smoke detector 安装烟雾探测器, smoke detector battery 探测器电池, test the smoke detector 测试探测器, ceiling smoke detector 天花板烟雾探测器, replace the smoke detector 更换探测器
[联] fire 火, alarm 警报, safety 安全, battery 电池, ceiling 天花板, home 家, kitchen 厨房, emergency 紧急, sensor 传感器, escape 逃生
[记] 可数：a smoke detector。亦说 smoke alarm，口语常混用。租房/酒店常见定期检测要求

**fire extinguisher**
英/美 /ˈfaɪər ɪkˌstɪŋɡwɪʃə(r)/
n. 灭火器
[义] A metal container filled with chemicals or foam used to put out small fires. 内装化学剂或泡沫、用于扑灭小型火灾的金属容器（fire extinguisher on the wall 墙上灭火器, use a fire extinguisher 使用灭火器, check the fire extinguisher 检查灭火器）。可数。
[例] ① Every floor has a fire extinguisher near the stairwell. 每层楼梯口附近都有灭火器。 ② She grabbed the fire extinguisher when oil caught fire in the pan. 锅里油起火时她抓起灭火器。
[搭] fire extinguisher on the wall 墙上灭火器, use a fire extinguisher 使用灭火器, check the fire extinguisher 检查灭火器, portable fire extinguisher 手提灭火器, fire extinguisher sign 灭火器标识
[联] fire 火, safety 安全, emergency 紧急, smoke 烟, alarm 警报, kitchen 厨房, wall 墙, spray 喷洒, escape 逃生, training 培训
[记] 可数：a fire extinguisher。使用前须看标签类型（A/B/C 类火）。pull pin, aim, squeeze, sweep（PASS 口诀）

**hedge**
英/美 /hedʒ/
n. 树篱；对冲
v. 用树篱围；回避
[义] A row of bushes or small trees planted close together along a boundary. 沿边界密植的一排灌木或小树（garden hedge 花园树篱，hedge trimmer 修篱机，hedge your bets 留后路〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① A tall hedge separates their garden from the neighbour's. 高树篱把他们的花园和邻居隔开。 ② They trimmed the hedge before the summer party. 夏日派对前他们修剪了树篱。
[搭] garden hedge 花园树篱, hedge trimmer 修篱机, trim the hedge 修树篱, hedge row 树篱行, privet hedge 女贞树篱
[联] fence 栅栏, garden 花园, boundary 边界, shrub 灌木, trim 修剪, privacy 隐私, neighbour 邻居, lawn 草坪
[记] 可数。hedge 金融义「对冲」与树篱同词。hedgehog 刺猬，不同词

**ironing board**
英/美 /ˈaɪənɪŋ bɔːd/
n. 熨衣板
[义] A narrow padded board, usually on legs that fold, on which clothes are ironed. 通常带折叠支架、供熨烫衣物的窄垫板（set up the ironing board 支起熨衣板，folding ironing board 折叠熨衣板）。可数。
[例] ① She set up the ironing board in front of the television. 她在电视机前支起熨衣板。 ② The ironing board was stored behind the laundry-room door. 熨衣板收在洗衣房门后。
[搭] set up the ironing board 支起熨衣板, folding ironing board 折叠熨衣板, put away the ironing board 收熨衣板, padded ironing board 软垫熨衣板
[联] iron 熨斗, laundry 洗衣, clothes 衣服, shirt 衬衫, fold 折叠, crease 褶皱, press 压平, sleeve 袖子
[记] 可数。复合词 ironing + board。ironing 名词：熨烫这一家务

**countertop**
英/美 /ˈkaʊntətɒp/
n. 台面；工作台面
[义] A flat surface in a kitchen or bathroom, usually fixed and used for working on. 厨房或浴室中通常固定、供操作使用的平面（granite countertop 花岗岩台面，kitchen countertop 厨房台面，wipe the countertop 擦台面）。可数。
[例] ① Marble countertops gave the kitchen a luxurious look. 大理石台面让厨房显得奢华。 ② Keep the countertop clear when you're not cooking. 不做饭时保持台面整洁。
[搭] granite countertop 花岗岩台面, kitchen countertop 厨房台面, wipe the countertop 擦台面, countertop material 台面材料, laminate countertop 复合台面
[联] kitchen 厨房, sink 水槽, cabinet 橱柜, surface 表面, marble 大理石, prepare 准备, clean 清洁, work 工作
[记] 可数。美式 countertop；英式 worktop 更常说厨房台面。counter 柜台同根

**range**
英/美 /reɪndʒ/
n. 厨灶；范围
[义] A large cooker with an oven and rings on top (American English; British cooker). 带烤箱和顶部炉盘的大型厨灶（美式；英式 cooker）；亦指范围（kitchen range 厨灶，wide range 广泛范围）。可数。
[例] ① Their new kitchen has a professional-style gas range. 新厨房装了专业款燃气厨灶。 ② She baked bread in the range oven every weekend. 她每周末用厨灶烤箱烤面包。
[搭] kitchen range 厨灶, gas range 燃气厨灶, range oven 厨灶烤箱, electric range 电厨灶, professional range 专业厨灶
[联] stove 炉灶, cooker 厨灶〔英〕, oven 烤箱, kitchen 厨房, cook 烹饪, gas 煤气, appliance 电器, hob 灶台
[记] 可数。range 作厨灶义美式常见；义「范围」极多，靠语境区分。free-range 散养的，不同义

**hob**
英 /hɒb/ 美 /hɑːb/
n. 灶台；炉盘
[义] The flat top of a cooker with rings or zones for heating pans (British English). 厨灶上供锅具加热的环形或分区平面（英式；美式 burner / cooktop）。可数。
[例] ① She wiped the hob after every meal. 她每顿饭饭后都擦灶台。 ② The induction hob heats pans quickly and safely. 电磁炉盘加热快又安全。
[搭] gas hob 燃气灶盘, induction hob 电磁炉盘, wipe the hob 擦灶台, hob ring 炉盘圈, ceramic hob 陶瓷灶台
[联] cooker 厨灶, stove 炉灶, oven 烤箱, kitchen 厨房, pan 锅, boil 煮沸, heat 加热, ring 炉圈
[记] 可数。英式 hob = 美式 cooktop / stovetop。hob 亦可指 fairy 精灵，罕见

**downspout**
英/美 /ˈdaʊnspaʊt/
n. 落水管；雨水管
[义] A pipe that carries rainwater from a roof gutter to the ground or drain (American English; British downpipe). 将屋顶檐槽雨水引至地面或排水管的竖管（美式；英式 downpipe）。可数。
[例] ① Leaves clogged the downspout and overflowed the gutter. 树叶堵了落水管，檐槽水溢出来。 ② They extended the downspout away from the foundation. 他们把落水管延长，远离地基。
[搭] clogged downspout 堵塞的落水管, extend the downspout 延长落水管, aluminum downspout 铝落水管, downspout drain 落水排水
[联] gutter 檐槽, roof 屋顶, rain 雨, pipe 管道, drain 排水, water 水, foundation 地基, leak 漏水
[记] 可数。美式 downspout = 英式 downpipe。与 gutter 配套使用

**toolbox**
英/美 /ˈtuːlbɒks/
n. 工具箱
[义] A box or case for carrying tools. 盛放工具的箱或盒（keep a toolbox 备工具箱，basic toolbox 基本工具箱，under the sink toolbox 水槽下工具箱）。可数。
[例] ① He grabbed a hammer and screwdriver from the toolbox. 他从工具箱里拿了锤子和螺丝刀。 ② Every homeowner should have a basic toolbox for small repairs. 每个房主都该备个基本工具箱做小维修。
[搭] keep a toolbox 备工具箱, basic toolbox 基本工具箱, portable toolbox 便携工具箱, toolbox essentials 工具箱必备, open the toolbox 打开工具箱
[联] tool 工具, hammer 锤子, screwdriver 螺丝刀, screw 螺丝, repair 修理, DIY 自己动手, fix 修理, garage 车库
[记] 可数。复合词 tool + box。toolbox 喻指技能储备（a writer's toolbox）

**dwelling**
英/美 /ˈdwelɪŋ/
n. 住所；住宅
[义] A place where people live; a house or flat (formal or legal). 人们居住的地方；房屋或公寓（正式或法律用语）（private dwelling 私人住宅，dwelling place 居所，multiple dwelling 多户住宅）。可数。
[例] ① The law protects tenants in rented dwellings. 法律保护租住住宅的租户。 ② Each dwelling in the block has its own balcony. 这栋楼每套住宅都有独立阳台。
[源] 源自 dwell（居住）+ -ing。
[族] dwell 居住, dweller 居民
[搭] private dwelling 私人住宅, dwelling place 居所, multiple dwelling 多户住宅, dwelling unit 居住单元, temporary dwelling 临时住所
[联] residence 住所, house 房屋, apartment 公寓, home 家, inhabit 居住, property 房产, housing 住房, live 居住
[记] 可数。dwelling 正式/法律/规划用语；日常多说 house / home。dwelling 不可数罕见

**neighbourhood**
英 /ˈneɪbəhʊd/ 美 /ˈneɪbərhʊd/
n. 街区；附近
[义] The area around your home; the people who live there. 家附近的区域；住在那里的人（quiet neighbourhood 安静街区，in the neighbourhood 在附近， neighbourhood watch 邻里守望）。可数。
[例] ① They moved to a friendly neighbourhood with good schools. 他们搬到一个有好学校、邻里友善的街区。 ② There's a bakery in the neighbourhood within walking distance. 步行距离内有一家面包店。
[源] 见 neighbour；*-hood* 表状态、区域。
[族] neighbour 邻居, neighborly 友善的
[搭] quiet neighbourhood 安静街区, in the neighbourhood 在附近, neighbourhood watch 邻里守望, safe neighbourhood 安全社区, local neighbourhood 本地街区
[联] neighbour 邻居, community 社区, area 区域, street 街道, local 本地的, house 房屋, friendly 友好的, suburb 郊区
[记] 可数。美式拼写 neighborhood。neighbourhood 指区域+社区感；vicinity 仅指附近范围

**dismantle**
英/美 /dɪsˈmæntl/
v. 拆卸；撤销
[义] To take apart a piece of furniture or equipment; to gradually destroy an organization. 拆开家具或设备；逐步撤销组织（dismantle a bed 拆床，dismantle furniture for moving 搬家拆家具）。及物动词。
[形] dismantled 拆开的
[例] ① They dismantled the wardrobe before carrying it downstairs. 他们把衣柜拆了才搬下楼。 ② The shed was dismantled and rebuilt in the new garden. 工棚被拆卸后在新花园重建。
[搭] dismantle a bed 拆床, dismantle furniture 拆家具, dismantle for moving 为搬家拆卸, easily dismantled 易拆卸, dismantle a shelf 拆架子
[联] assemble 组装, take apart 拆开, move 搬家, furniture 家具, screw 螺丝, build 建造, pack 打包, rebuild 重建
[记] dismantle 与 assemble 相对。dis-* + mantle（覆盖/ mantel 壁炉台），词源「取去斗篷」

**dwell**
英/美 /dwel/
v. 居住； dwell on 纠结
[义] To live in a place (literary); to think or speak about something for too long. 居住于某处（文）；过长地思考或谈论（dwell in 寓于，dwell on the past 沉湎过去）。不及物动词。
[例] ① Ancient tribes dwelt in caves along the river. 古代部落居住在沿河洞穴中。 ② Try not to dwell on what went wrong with the renovation. 尽量别老纠结翻新出了什么岔子。
[族] dwelling 住所, dweller 居民
[搭] dwell in 寓于, dwell on the past 沉湎过去, dwell together 同住, cave dweller 穴居者, city dweller 城市居民
[联] live 居住, reside 居住, inhabit 居住, home 家, stay 停留, linger 逗留, past 过去, poetic 诗意的
[记] dwell 居住义偏文学/古。dwell on/upon 反复想/说。dweller：city dweller 城市居民

**worktop**
英/美 /ˈwɜːktɒp/
n. 操作台面；厨房台面〔尤英式〕
[义] A flat surface in a kitchen where food is prepared (British English; American countertop). 厨房中准备食物的平面（英式；美式 countertop）。可数。
[例] ① Wipe the worktop after preparing raw meat. 处理生肉后擦净台面。 ② Granite worktops are durable but expensive. 花岗岩台面耐用但价高。
[搭] granite worktop 花岗岩台面, wipe the worktop 擦台面, kitchen worktop 厨房台面, worktop surface 操作面, laminate worktop 复合台面
[联] countertop 台面〔美〕, kitchen 厨房, sink 水槽, cabinet 橱柜, prepare 准备, clean 清洁, surface 表面, marble 大理石
[记] 可数。英式 worktop = 美式 countertop。复合词 work + top

**doorframe**
英/美 /ˈdɔːfreɪm/
n. 门框
[义] The frame surrounding a door, fixed to the wall. 固定于墙面、环绕门的框架（doorframe and door 门框与门，paint the doorframe 漆门框）。可数。
[例] ① The old doorframe was warped and let in drafts. 旧门框变形，漏风。 ② They replaced the doorframe when installing a wider door. 装更宽的门时他们换了门框。

[联] door 门, hinge 铰链, latch 门闩, lock 锁, wall 墙, entrance 入口, wood 木头, install 安装
[记] 可数。复合词 door + frame。doorjamb 门框侧柱，较专业

**mantelpiece**
英/美 /ˈmæntlpiːs/
n. 壁炉台
[义] A shelf above a fireplace. 壁炉上方的搁架（family photos on the mantelpiece 壁炉台上的全家福，mantel clock 壁炉台钟）。可数。
[例] ① Candles and family photos lined the mantelpiece. 壁炉台上摆着蜡烛和全家福。 ② She dusted the mantelpiece every week. 她每周掸壁炉台的灰。

[记] 可数。亦简写 mantel / mantle（拼写混用，壁炉台义）。mantelpiece 英式家庭意象常见

**detergent**
英/美 /dɪˈtɜːdʒənt/
n. 洗涤剂
adj. 洗涤的
[义] A chemical substance used for washing clothes or dishes. 用于洗衣或洗碗的化学清洁品（laundry detergent 洗衣液，dish detergent 洗洁精，eco-friendly detergent 环保洗涤剂）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① She added detergent to the washing machine and started a cycle. 她往洗衣机里加了洗涤剂并开始洗涤。 ② Use a mild detergent on delicate fabrics. 精细织物用温和洗涤剂。

[族] deterge 洗净〔罕〕
[搭] laundry detergent 洗衣液, dish detergent 洗洁精, liquid detergent 液态洗涤剂, eco-friendly detergent 环保洗涤剂, add detergent 加洗涤剂
[联] soap 肥皂, laundry 洗衣, wash 洗, clean 清洁, bleach 漂白剂, fabric 织物, dish 餐具, machine 机器
[记] 可数/不可数。detergent 偏化学配方；soap 偏传统皂。dishwasher detergent 洗碗块/粉

**bleach**
英/美 /bliːtʃ/
n. 漂白剂
v. 漂白；褪色
[义] A chemical used to whiten or disinfect; to make something white or pale. 用于漂白或消毒的化学品；使变白或褪色（household bleach 家用漂白剂，bleach the bathroom 用漂白剂清洁浴室）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① She wore gloves when using bleach to clean the tiles. 她用漂白剂清洁瓷砖时戴了手套。 ② Sun had bleached the curtains over the years. 多年日晒把窗帘晒褪色了。
[搭] household bleach 家用漂白剂, bleach the bathroom 漂白清洁浴室, bleach stains 漂白污渍, chlorine bleach 氯漂白剂, diluted bleach 稀释漂白剂
[联] detergent 洗涤剂, clean 清洁, disinfect 消毒, white 白色的, stain 污渍, bathroom 浴室, chemical 化学品, fade 褪色
[记] 不可数/可数。bleach 动词：bleached hair 漂染发。勿与漂白剂混用不同化学品

adj. 半独立的；双拼的
n. 双拼住宅
[义] Joined to another house on one side only. 仅一侧与另一栋相连（semi-detached house 双拼别墅，live in a semi 住双拼〔口语〕）。形容词/可数名词。
[例] ① They grew up in a semi-detached house in the suburbs. 他们在郊区一栋双拼里长大。 ② Semi-detached homes share one wall with neighbours. 双拼住宅与邻居共一面墙。

[联] detached 独栋, townhouse 联排, house 房屋, neighbour 邻居, suburb 郊区, wall 墙, garden 花园, property 房产
[记] 形容词/可数。英式 semi 极常见；美式 less common。与 detached、terraced 构成英国住宅三类

**detached**
英/美 /dɪˈtætʃt/
adj. 独立的；独栋的
[义] Not joined to another building; standing alone (detached house 独栋住宅). 不与另一建筑相连；独立（detached garage 独立车库）。形容词。
[例] ① They saved for years to buy a detached house with a garden. 他们攒多年买了带花园的独栋。 ② A detached garage sits at the end of the driveway. 独立车库在车道尽头。
[搭] detached house 独栋住宅, detached garage 独立车库, fully detached 完全独立, detached property 独立房产, detached dwelling 独立住宅

[记] 形容词。detached 亦指情感疏离（emotionally detached），语境区分。detached house 英式规划用语

**ranch**
英/美 /rɑːntʃ/
n. 牧场；单层 ranch 住宅
[义] A large farm for cattle or horses; a single-storey house, especially in the American West (ranch house). 牛马大型农场；单层住宅，尤指美国西部（ranch house ranch 式住宅，ranch dressing  ranch 酱）。可数。
[例] ① They live in a ranch-style house with a wide porch in Texas. 他们住在德州一座带宽门廊的 ranch 式住宅里。 ② The property includes the main ranch and outbuildings. 地产含主牧场与附属建筑。

[联] bungalow 平房, house 房屋, farmhouse 农舍, porch 门廊, suburban 郊区的, American 美国的, single-storey 单层的, land 土地
[记] 可数。ranch house 美式指单层宽浅住宅；与 bungalow 有重叠。ranch 牧场义在西部片常见

**studio**
英/美 /ˈstjuːdiəʊ/
n. 单间公寓；工作室
[义] A small flat with one main room combining living and sleeping space, with a separate bathroom and kitchenette. 主要空间合一、含独立卫浴和小厨房的小公寓；亦指工作室（studio flat 单间公寓，art studio 艺术工作室）。可数。
[例] ① Her first apartment was a tiny studio near the university. 她第一套公寓是大学附近的一间小单间。 ② The studio has a kitchenette and a sleeping alcove. 单间配有简易厨房和睡眠凹室。

[记] 可数。studio flat 英式；studio apartment 美式。studio 艺术/录音工作室义极常见

**extension**
英/美 /ɪkˈstenʃn/
n. 扩建；延伸
[义] A new room or rooms added to an existing building; the act of extending. 在既有建筑上加建的新房间；延伸行为（build an extension 建扩建部分，kitchen extension 厨房扩建，extension cable 延长线）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① They got planning permission for a two-storey extension. 他们拿到了建两层扩建的许可。 ② The extension added a bedroom and a larger kitchen. 扩建部分增加了一间卧室和更大的厨房。

[族] extend 延伸, extensive 广泛的, extent 程度

[记] 可数/不可数。extension 亦指电话分机、文件延期、头发接发，语境区分

**extend**
英/美 /ɪkˈstend/
v. 延伸；扩建
[义] To make a building larger by adding rooms; to stretch or continue. 通过增建房间使建筑更大；延伸或继续（extend the kitchen 扩建厨房，extend a lease 续租，extend credit 提供信贷）。及物/不及物。
[例] ① They plan to extend the house at the back. 他们计划在屋后扩建。 ② The patio extends across the full width of the garden. 露台横跨花园整个宽度。

[族] extension 扩建, extensive 广泛的, extent 程度
[搭] extend the kitchen 扩建厨房, extend a lease 续租, extend the house 扩建房屋, extend outward 向外延伸, extend the patio 延伸露台
[联] build 建造, enlarge 扩大, add 增加, stretch 伸展, renovation 翻新, room 房间, widen 加宽, continue 继续

**refit**

[例] ① The bathroom was refitted with new tiles and a walk-in shower. 浴室重装，换了新瓷砖和步入式淋浴。 ② They refitted the entire flat before moving in. 搬入前他们把整套公寓重新装修了一遍。

[例] ① The average household spends a third of its income on housing. 普通家庭把三分之一收入花在住房上。 ② Household cleaning products should be kept away from children. 家用清洁品应远离儿童。

n. 门阶

[例] ① Milk was once delivered to the doorstep every morning. 牛奶曾每天早上送到门阶。 ② She sat on the doorstep waiting for her friend. 她坐在门阶上等朋友。

adj. 联排的

**facade**

[义] A row of short posts with a rail along the top, supporting a balcony or staircase. 一排短柱加顶栏，支撑阳台或楼梯（stone balustrade 石栏杆，balustrade rail 栏杆扶手）。可数。

[例] ① He grabbed the plunger when the toilet blocked again. 马桶又堵了他抓起搋子。 ② Keep a plunger in the bathroom cupboard for emergencies. 浴室柜里备个搋子应急。

**door chain**

n. 门链；防盗链
[义] A chain fitted to a door that allows it to open slightly while still secured. 装在门上、可在仍锁闭时略开一条缝的链（put on the door chain 挂上门链，answer with the chain on 挂着链应门）。可数。
[例] ① She put the door chain on before opening the door to a stranger. 给陌生人开门前她挂上了门链。 ② The door chain lets you see who is outside without fully opening up. 门链让你不全开门也能看清外面是谁。
[搭] put on the door chain 挂上门链, answer with the chain on 挂链应门, security door chain 防盗链, door chain lock 门链锁, fit a door chain 装门链
[联] door 门, lock 锁, peephole 猫眼, security 安全, stranger 陌生人, entrance 入口, safety 安全, latch 门闩
[记] 可数。door chain 与 peephole、burglar alarm 组成居家安防用语

**bedsit**
英 /ˈbedsɪt/ 美 /ˈbedsɪt/
n. 卧室兼客厅单间〔尤英式〕
[义] A rented room that serves as both bedroom and living room, with shared kitchen and bathroom. 兼作卧室和客厅出租的单间，厨房与卫生间与他人共用（live in a bedsit 住 bedsit，tiny bedsit 极小单间）。可数。
[例] ① She lived in a bedsit while saving for her own flat. 存够钱买公寓前她住在一间 bedsit 里。 ② The bedsit had a hotplate and a shared bathroom down the hall. 单间里有电热板，卫生间在走廊共用。
[搭] live in a bedsit 住 bedsit, tiny bedsit 极小单间, rent a bedsit 租 bedsit, bedsit landlady 女房东, convert a bedsit 改造单间
[联] studio 单间, flat 公寓, rent 租金, tenant 租户, room 房间, landlord 房东, small 小的, urban 城市的
[记] 可数。bedsit = bed-sitting room。比 studio 更暗示设施简陋、共用厨卫

**sunroom**
英/美 /ˈsʌnruːm/
n. 日光室
[义] A room with many windows, designed to let in a lot of sunlight. 窗户多、旨在引入大量阳光的房间（sit in the sunroom 坐在日光室，sunroom extension 日光室扩建）。可数。
[例] ① They added a sunroom where they grow herbs in winter. 他们加了一间日光室，冬天种香草。 ② Coffee in the sunroom is her favourite part of the morning. 在日光室喝咖啡是她早晨最爱的事。
[搭] sit in the sunroom 坐在日光室, sunroom extension 日光室扩建, glass sunroom 玻璃日光室, sunroom furniture 日光室家具, bright sunroom 明亮日光室
[联] conservatory 玻璃暖房, garden 花园, glass 玻璃, light 光线, plant 植物, patio 庭院, warm 温暖, extension 扩建
[记] 可数。美式 sunroom；英式亦用 conservatory / garden room。与 conservatory 相比 sunroom 偏休闲坐息

**greenhouse**
英/美 /ˈɡriːnhaʊs/
n. 温室
[义] A glass building used for growing plants that need warmth and protection. 用于培育需温暖与保护的植物的玻璃建筑（back-garden greenhouse 后院温室，greenhouse gases 温室气体〔全球〕）。可数。
[例] ① Tomatoes ripen early in the greenhouse behind the house. 屋后温室里的番茄熟得早。 ② She spends Sunday afternoons potting plants in the greenhouse. 她周日下午在温室里换盆。
[搭] back-garden greenhouse 后院温室, greenhouse plants 温室植物, heated greenhouse 加热温室, small greenhouse 小温室, greenhouse door 温室门
[联] conservatory 玻璃暖房, garden 花园, plant 植物, glass 玻璃, warm 温暖, grow 种植, sunroom 日光室, shed 棚屋
[记] 可数。greenhouse effect / greenhouse gases 全球变暖语境，义延伸。greenhouse 比 conservatory 更偏种植

**fixture**
英/美 /ˈfɪkstʃə(r)/
n. 固定装置； fixtures
[义] A piece of equipment or furniture fixed in position in a house; a sporting event already arranged. 固定安装于房屋内的设备或家具；已定赛事（light fixtures 灯具，plumbing fixtures 卫浴洁具，kitchen fixtures 厨房固定装置）。可数，常用复数 fixtures。
[例] ① Light fixtures were updated during the renovation. 翻新时更新了灯具。 ② The sale includes all kitchen fixtures and fittings. 出售包括全部厨房固定装置与配件。
[搭] light fixtures 灯具, plumbing fixtures 卫浴洁具, kitchen fixtures 厨房固定装置, bathroom fixtures 浴室固定装置, include fixtures 含固定装置
[联] fitting 配件, install 安装, bathroom 浴室, kitchen 厨房, tap 水龙头, sink 水槽, light 灯, permanent 固定的
[记] 可数，常用复数 fixtures。fixtures and fittings 英式房产交易常用语。fixture 体育义「固定赛事」

**fitting**
英/美 /ˈfɪtɪŋ/
n. 配件； fittings
adj. 适当的
[义] A small part or item supplied with a building, especially for bathrooms or kitchens; suitable. 随建筑配备的小部件，尤指厨卫；适当的（light fittings 灯具配件，bathroom fittings 浴室配件，fixtures and fittings 固定装置与配件）。可数，常用复数 fittings。
[例] ① Chrome fittings gave the bathroom a modern look. 镀铬配件让浴室显得现代。 ② The landlord replaced worn fittings in the kitchen. 房东更换了厨房里磨损的配件。
[搭] light fittings 灯具配件, bathroom fittings 浴室配件, fixtures and fittings 固定装置与配件, kitchen fittings 厨房配件, replace fittings 更换配件
[联] fixture 固定装置, tap 水龙头, bathroom 浴室, kitchen 厨房, install 安装, chrome 镀铬, pipe 管道, accessory 配件
[记] 可数，常用复数 fittings。fixtures and fittings 买卖房产时区分「固定不可拆」与「可拆配件」

**ventilate**
英/美 /ˈventɪleɪt/
v. 通风
[义] To let fresh air into a room or building. 使新鲜空气进入房间或建筑（ventilate the room 给房间通风，well ventilated 通风良好，ventilate after cooking 做饭后通风）。及物动词。
[形] ventilated 通风的, ventilation 通风
[例] ① Open windows to ventilate the house after painting. 刷漆后开窗给房屋通风。 ② The bathroom fan helps ventilate steam after showers. 浴室风扇帮助淋浴后排出蒸汽。
[源] 见 vent；*ventilare*（吹风）。
[族] ventilation 通风, vent 通风口, ventilator 通风设备
[搭] ventilate the room 给房间通风, well ventilated 通风良好, ventilate after cooking 做饭后通风, poorly ventilated 通风不良, ventilate the attic 给阁楼通风
[联] vent 通风口, air 空气, window 窗户, fan 风扇, mould 霉菌, damp 潮湿, fresh 新鲜的, steam 蒸汽
[记] ventilate 比 open a window 更正式。ventilation 不可数；poor ventilation 致 damp/mould

**home improvement**
英/美 /həʊm ɪmˈpruːvmənt/
n. 家居改善；家装
[义] Work done to make a house more comfortable, attractive, or valuable. 使房屋更舒适、美观或增值的工程（home improvement store 家装商店，DIY home improvement  DIY 家装，home improvement loan 家装贷款）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① They spent the weekend on home improvement projects. 他们周末做家装项目。 ② A new kitchen is the home improvement that adds the most value. 换新厨房是最增值的家居改善。
[搭] home improvement store 家装商店, DIY home improvement  DIY 家装, home improvement loan 家装贷款, home improvement project 家装项目, major home improvement 大型家装
[联] renovate 翻新, remodel 改造, DIY 自己动手, repair 修理, kitchen 厨房, bathroom 浴室, value 价值, contractor 承包商
[记] 不可数/可数。美式 home improvement store（如 Home Depot）。英式 DIY store / home centre

**patch**
英/美 /pætʃ/
n. 补丁
v. 修补
[义] A piece of material used to cover a hole; to repair with such a piece or temporarily. 用于遮盖洞口的材料块；用补丁或临时方式修理（patch the wall 补墙，patch up 临时修好，patch of damp 一块潮斑）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① He patched the hole in the plaster until the builder came. 在师傅来之前他先把灰泥洞补上。 ② A dark patch on the ceiling showed where the leak had been. 天花板上一块深色潮斑显示曾经漏过水。
[搭] patch the wall 补墙, patch up 临时修好, patch of damp 一块潮斑, patch a hole 补洞, patch repair 局部修补
[联] mend 修补, repair 修理, fix 修理, plaster 灰泥, wall 墙, hole 洞, damp 潮湿, temporary 临时的
[记] 可数/不可数。patch up 指临时凑合修好（patch up a quarrel 言和）。patch 动词：patch software 打补丁〔IT〕

**council house**
英/美 /ˈkaʊnsl haʊs/
n. 公房；市政住房〔尤英式〕
[义] A house owned by a local council and rented at a lower cost (British English). 由地方市政拥有、以较低租金出租的房屋（英式）。可数。
[例] ① She grew up on a council estate in the north of England. 她在英格兰北部公房区长大。 ② Council house tenants can buy their homes under certain schemes. 公房租户在特定政策下可购买所住房屋。
[搭] council estate 公房区, council house tenant 公房租户, buy a council house 购买公房, council housing 公房体系, former council house 原公房
[联] rent 租金, tenant 租户, landlord 房东, housing 住房, estate 住宅区, property 房产, affordable 负担得起的, suburb 郊区
[记] 可数。英式 council house / council flat；Right to Buy 政策下 many 转为私有。美式 public housing 近义

**sublet**
英/美 /ˌsʌbˈlet/
v. 转租
n. 转租
[义] To rent a property you rent to someone else; such an arrangement. 将您租住的房产再租给他人；此类安排（sublet a room 转租房间，illegal sublet 非法转租，sublet agreement 转租协议）。及物/不及物动词；可数（名词）。
[形] subletting 转租中
[例] ① Their lease forbids subletting without the landlord's consent. 租约禁止未经房东同意转租。 ② She sublet her flat while travelling for six months. 外出六个月期间她把公寓转租了出去。
[搭] sublet a room 转租房间, illegal sublet 非法转租, sublet agreement 转租协议, sublet without permission 擅自转租, sublet the flat 转租公寓
[联] rent 租金, lease 租约, tenant 租户, landlord 房东, room 房间, flat 公寓, permission 许可, lodger 房客
[记] sub- + let。sublet 与 assign 租约不同；须看 lease 是否允许。subletter 转租者

**evict**
英/美 /ɪˈvɪkt/
v. 驱逐；赶出
[义] To force a tenant to leave a property, usually by law. 迫使租户离开房产，通常通过法律程序（evict a tenant 驱逐租户，face eviction 面临驱逐，eviction notice 驱逐通知）。及物动词。
[形] evicted 被驱逐的, eviction 驱逐
[例] ① The landlord tried to evict them for unpaid rent. 房东试图因欠租驱逐他们。 ② Tenants cannot be evicted without proper legal process. 未经合法程序不得驱逐租户。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *evincere*（征服），*e-*（出）+ *vincere*（战胜）→ 逐出。
[族] eviction 驱逐
[搭] evict a tenant 驱逐租户, face eviction 面临驱逐, evict for non-payment 因欠租驱逐, illegally evicted 非法驱逐, court order to evict 法院驱逐令
[联] tenant 租户, landlord 房东, rent 租金, lease 租约, court 法庭, notice 通知, housing 住房, homeless 无家可归
[记] eviction 名词。evict 须走 legal process；self-evict 非正式说法少见

**eviction**
英/美 /ɪˈvɪkʃn/
n. 驱逐；赶出
[义] The legal process of forcing someone to leave a property they rent. 强制某人离开所租房产的法律程序（face eviction 面临驱逐，eviction notice 驱逐通知，stop an eviction 阻止驱逐）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① They received an eviction notice after months of missed payments. 数月欠租后他们收到了驱逐通知。 ② Eviction can be delayed if the tenant disputes the grounds. 若租户对理由有异议，驱逐可延期。
[搭] face eviction 面临驱逐, eviction notice 驱逐通知, stop an eviction 阻止驱逐, eviction process 驱逐程序, risk of eviction 遭驱逐风险
[联] evict 驱逐, tenant 租户, landlord 房东, rent 租金, court 法庭, notice 通知, housing 住房, homeless 无家可归
[记] 可数/不可数。eviction moratorium 驱逐暂停令〔新闻〕。no-fault eviction 无过错驱逐

**draught excluder**
英/美 /drɑːft ɪkˈskluːdə(r)/
n. 挡风条；门缝条
[义] A strip of material fitted to a door or window to stop draughts (British English). 装于门或窗上以防漏风的条带（英式）。可数。
[例] ① A draught excluder under the door kept the room warmer. 门下的挡风条让房间更暖和。 ② They fitted draught excluders before winter. 入冬前他们装了挡风条。
[搭] fit draught excluders 装挡风条, door draught excluder 门缝挡风条, self-adhesive draught excluder 自粘挡风条, replace draught excluder 更换挡风条
[联] draught 穿堂风, draft 穿堂风〔美〕, door 门, window 窗户, seal 密封, insulation 隔热, cold 冷的, winter 冬天
[记] 可数。英式 draught excluder；美式 draft stopper / weather stripping。snake draught excluder 指布条塞门缝

**water stain**
英/美 /ˈwɔːtə steɪn/
n. 水渍
[义] A mark left on a surface by water, often yellow or brown. 水在表面留下的痕迹，常呈黄或褐色（water stain on the ceiling 天花板水渍，remove water stains 去除水渍）。可数。
[例] ① A brown water stain on the ceiling showed where the roof had leaked. 天花板上的褐色水渍标出曾经漏雨的位置。 ② Paint will not cover water stains unless you treat them first. 不先处理水渍，油漆盖不住。
[搭] water stain on the ceiling 天花板水渍, remove water stains 去除水渍, yellow water stain 黄色水渍, cover water stains 遮盖水渍, old water stain 旧水渍
[联] stain 污渍, leak 漏水, ceiling 天花板, damp 潮湿, mould 霉菌, repair 修理, roof 屋顶, patch 补丁
[记] 可数。water stain 与 damp patch 常并用。bleach 可除部分水渍，但须先试

**stain**
英/美 /steɪn/
n. 污渍；色渍
v. 弄脏；染色
[义] A mark left on a surface by a liquid or dirt; to leave such a mark or colour wood. 液体或污垢在表面留下的痕迹；弄脏或给木材上色（coffee stain 咖啡渍，wood stain 木器色漆，stain the carpet 弄脏地毯）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① They could not remove the stain from the living-room carpet. 客厅地毯上的污渍去不掉。 ② He stained the shelf to match the floor. 他给搁板上了色以配地板。
[搭] coffee stain 咖啡渍, wood stain 木器色漆, remove a stain 去污渍, stain remover 去污剂, stubborn stain 顽固污渍
[联] water stain 水渍, carpet 地毯, clean 清洁, spill 泼洒, wood 木头, varnish 清漆, mark 痕迹, remove 去除
[记] 可数/不可数。stain 动词：stain wood 给木头上色。stainless steel 不锈钢，不同义

**lodger**
英/美 /ˈlɒdʒə(r)/
n. 寄宿客；房客
[义] A person who pays to live in someone else's house, usually with meals included. 付费住在他人家中的人，通常含餐（take in a lodger 收寄宿客，lodger's room 寄宿客房间）。可数。
[例] ① They took in a lodger to help pay the mortgage. 他们收房客帮忙还按揭。 ② The lodger has use of the kitchen but not the living room. 房客可用厨房但不用客厅。
[源] 源自 lodge（ lodging）+ -er。
[族] lodge 借宿, lodging  lodging, lodgings 出租房间
[搭] take in a lodger 收房客, lodger's room 房客房间, paying lodger 付费房客, lodger agreement 房客协议, live as a lodger 作房客居住
[联] tenant 租户, rent 租金, landlord 房东, room 房间, board 膳宿, house 房屋, guest 客人, income 收入
[记] 可数。lodger 与 tenant：lodger 常指与房东同住；tenant 更独立租约。lodgings 复数指出租房间

**flatmate**
英 /ˈflætmeɪt/ 美 /ˈflætmeɪt/
n. 合租室友〔尤英式〕
[义] A person who shares a flat with you (British English; American roommate). 与您合租公寓的人（英式；美式 roommate）。可数。
[例] ① She lives with two flatmates near the city centre. 她和两个合租室友住在市中心附近。 ② Flatmates share bills for electricity and internet. 合租室友分摊电费和网费。
[搭] live with flatmates 与合租室友同住, new flatmate 新室友, flatmate agreement 合租协议, share with flatmates 与室友分摊, former flatmate 前室友
[联] roommate 室友〔美〕, flat 公寓, rent 租金, tenant 租户, share 分摊, bill 账单, kitchen 厨房, neighbour 邻居
[记] 可数。英式 flatmate = 美式 roommate（同住公寓）。housemate 同住一 house 而非 flat

**wear and tear**
英/美 /weər ənd teə(r)/
n. 正常磨损
[义] Damage that happens naturally from ordinary use over time. 日常使用随时间自然产生的损坏（normal wear and tear 正常磨损，fair wear and tear 合理磨损〔租约〕）。不可数。
[例] ① The deposit was returned minus normal wear and tear. 押金扣除正常磨损后退还。 ② Landlords cannot charge tenants for fair wear and tear. 房东不能向租户收取合理磨损费。
[搭] normal wear and tear 正常磨损, fair wear and tear 合理磨损, signs of wear and tear 磨损痕迹, exclude wear and tear 不含磨损, general wear and tear 一般磨损
[联] deposit 押金, tenant 租户, landlord 房东, damage 损坏, repair 修理, furniture 家具, carpet 地毯, lease 租约
[记] 不可数。fair wear and tear 英式租约关键词：合理老化不算 damage

**viewing**
英/美 /ˈvjuːɪŋ/
n. 看房
[义] An occasion when someone looks around a property that is for sale or rent. 查看待售或待租房产的场合（book a viewing 预约看房， flat viewing 公寓看房，second viewing 二看）。可数。
[例] ① They booked a viewing for Saturday morning. 他们预约了周六上午看房。 ② Three couples came to the viewing last night. 昨晚三对夫妇来看房。
[搭] book a viewing 预约看房, flat viewing 公寓看房, second viewing 二看, property viewing 房产看房, arrange a viewing 安排看房
[联] estate agent 房产经纪人, rent 租金, buy 购买, property 房产, apartment 公寓, tenant 租户, show round 带看, appointment 预约
[记] 可数。viewing 亦指「观看、放映」；property viewing 靠语境。open viewing 开放看房日

**open house**
英/美 /ˌəʊpən ˈhaʊs/
n. 开放看房日
[义] A period when a property for sale is open for anyone to view without an appointment. 待售房产在特定时段开放、无需预约即可参观（hold an open house 举办开放看房，open house weekend 开放看房周末）。可数。
[例] ① The estate agent held an open house on Sunday afternoon. 房产中介周日下午办了开放看房。 ② Dozens of people walked through during the open house. 开放看房期间数十人进来参观。

[联] viewing 看房, estate agent 房产经纪人, property 房产, sale 出售, buyer 买家, show round 带看, appointment 预约, weekend 周末
[记] 可数。美式 open house 售房常见；英式 less formal。open house 亦指「随时欢迎来访」

**inventory**
英/美 /ˈɪnvəntri/
n. 物品清单；盘点表
[义] A detailed list of the contents and condition of a rented property at the start or end of a tenancy. 租期开始或结束时对所租房产内物品及状况的详细清单（check the inventory 核对清单，inventory check 清单检查）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① They signed the inventory when they moved in. 搬入时他们签了物品清单。 ② Compare the exit inventory with the one from move-in day. 将退租清单与入住日那份对照。
[搭] check the inventory 核对清单, inventory check 清单检查, signed inventory 已签清单, move-in inventory 入住清单, detailed inventory 详细清单
[联] tenant 租户, landlord 房东, lease 租约, deposit 押金, move in 搬入, condition 状况, furniture 家具, damage 损坏
[记] 可数/不可数。英式 rental inventory 极重要，关系 deposit 退还。inventory 亦指商店存货

**house sitter**
英/美 /ˈhaʊs sɪtə(r)/
n. 看屋人
[义] A person who stays in someone's home while they are away, to care for it and sometimes pets. 主人外出期间代为看屋、有时照料宠物的人（hire a house sitter 请看屋人，house-sitting 代为看屋）。可数。
[例] ① They hired a house sitter while on a month-long trip. 出门旅行一个月期间他们请了看屋人。 ② The house sitter watered plants and collected the mail. 看屋人浇花并取邮件。
[搭] hire a house sitter 请看屋人, house-sitting job 看屋工作, trusted house sitter 可信看屋人, house sitter for pets 兼照料宠物, live-in house sitter 住家看屋
[联] neighbour 邻居, holiday 假期, pet 宠物, mail 邮件, security 安全, home 家, travel 旅行, trust 信任
[记] 可数。house-sit 动词：代为看屋。与 lodger 不同：house sitter 不付长期 rent

**carbon monoxide alarm**
英/美 /ˌkɑːbən məˈnɒksaɪd əˌlɑːm/
n. 一氧化碳报警器
[义] A device that warns when dangerous levels of carbon monoxide are present. 检测到危险浓度一氧化碳时发出警示的装置（install a carbon monoxide alarm 装一氧化碳报警器，test the alarm 测试报警器）。可数。
[例] ① A carbon monoxide alarm is required near any fuel-burning appliance. 任何燃烧燃料的电器附近都应装一氧化碳报警器。 ② Test the carbon monoxide alarm when you check smoke alarms. 检查烟雾报警器时一并测试一氧化碳报警器。
[搭] install a carbon monoxide alarm 装报警器, test the alarm 测试报警器, carbon monoxide detector 一氧化碳探测器, battery-powered alarm 电池报警器, alarm beeps 报警器鸣响
[联] smoke alarm 烟雾报警器, gas 煤气, boiler 锅炉, fireplace 壁炉, safety 安全, poison 中毒, battery 电池, detect 检测
[记] 可数。与 smoke alarm 并列安装。CO 无色无味，alarm 可 lifesaving

**immersion heater**
英/美 /ɪˈmɜːʃn ˈhiːtə(r)/
n. 浸没式热水器〔尤英式〕
[义] An electric heater inside a hot-water tank (chiefly British). 热水箱内的电加热器（主要英式）。可数。
[例] ① The immersion heater heats water overnight on a cheaper tariff. 浸没式热水器在低价电价时段夜间烧水。 ② They turned off the immersion heater to save energy. 他们为节能关掉了浸没式热水器。
[搭] turn on the immersion heater 开浸没式热水器, immersion heater switch 热水器开关, hot-water immersion heater 热水浸没加热器, faulty immersion heater 故障热水器
[联] boiler 锅炉, water heater 热水器, hot water 热水, electricity 电, tank 水箱, bathroom 浴室, energy 能源, switch 开关
[记] 可数。英式常见；美式 more often water heater / boiler。immersion 指浸没式加热棒

**guttering**
英/美 /ˈɡʌtərɪŋ/
n. 排水槽系统
[义] The gutters and pipes that carry rainwater from a roof (British English; often gutters in American). 从屋顶排走雨水的檐槽与管道（英式；美式常只说 gutters）。不可数。
[例] ① Blocked guttering caused water to overflow down the wall. 排水槽堵塞导致水沿墙溢出。 ② They replaced the old guttering during the roof repair. 修屋顶时他们换了旧排水槽。
[搭] blocked guttering 堵塞排水槽, replace guttering 更换排水槽, clean the guttering 清理排水槽, plastic guttering 塑料排水槽, guttering system 排水槽系统
[联] gutter 檐槽, downspout 落水管, roof 屋顶, rain 雨, leak 漏水, drain 排水, water 水, repair 修理
[记] 不可数。guttering 指整套系统；gutter 单段檐槽。美式 gutters 可数复数

**retile**
英/美 /ˌriːˈtaɪl/
v. 重新铺砖
[义] To cover a surface with new tiles. 用新瓷砖覆盖表面（retile the bathroom 浴室重铺瓷砖，retile the kitchen floor 厨房地面重铺砖）。及物动词。
[例] ① They retiled the shower after removing mouldy grout. 去掉发霉填缝剂后他们给淋浴间重铺了砖。 ② Retiling the kitchen took a full weekend. 厨房重铺砖花了一整个周末。
[搭] retile the bathroom 浴室重铺砖, retile the kitchen floor 厨房地面重铺砖, retile a wall 墙面重铺砖, professionally retiled 专业重铺, retile the splashback 防溅墙重铺砖
[联] tile 瓷砖, grout 填缝剂, bathroom 浴室, kitchen 厨房, mould 霉菌, renovate 翻新, floor 地板, wall 墙
[记] re-* + tile。retile 比 replace a few tiles 指更大范围
